# Changelog Archive

> Older entries archived from `Changelog.md` (rolling 20-version window).

| Version | Key change |
|---|---|
| **2.79.2** | **Reviewed commitment-imprint poem round.** Added four Can-approved selections to `assets/poems.js`: the opening three lines of a traditional Asante talking-drum verse recorded and translated by R. S. Rattray, the choice beat from Olive Schreiner's 'Life's Gifts', Ricardo Jaimes Freyre's rivulet image from 'Inner Landscapes' translated by Alice Stone Blackwell, and the complete five-line presentation of Kahlil Gibran's 'The Fox'. Each selection is 2–5 displayed lines; the oral tradition and all named authors, recorders, and translators pass the worldwide-public-domain screen. Corpus grows 119 → 123 (W14 / Sp16 / Su12 / Au10 / year-round 71). Smoke coverage locks the new count, attributions, and six-line ceiling. No storage or sync interfaces changed. |
| **2.79.1** | **Fix: the same waiting task was described twice in AI context.** `neverStarted` is a strict subset of `returning`, so any 7+ day zero-session task was emitted under both "Tasks that keep waiting" and "Tasks not yet started despite being on the list", then a third time in the numbered task list. Found by intercepting a real nudge payload: one task appeared three times and was by far the most-repeated string in the prompt. A model choosing from ~12 unranked facts selects on salience, so repetition was effectively doing the selecting. Focus avoidance now rides as an inline marker ("never opened" at 7+ days, "not yet started" below) — same signal, one emission per task. |
| **2.79.0** | **Feat: voice memory — `appMemory.spokenLines`.** Every generative surface previously spoke in isolation: none knew what another had said, or what it had said yesterday, and `recentConversations` stored only the user's messages, never the app's replies. New slot records `{ surface, date, text }`, 30 days, one entry per surface per day (a regenerated line replaces rather than stacks), written at the morning nudge, focus question, Sunday reflection, week theme and Monday intention — deliberately not the assistant chat, which is user-initiated dialogue rather than the app's unprompted voice. Surfaced to every AI call through `_memoryForAI()`. `dropbox.js` merges by `date\|surface`; the same commit also added `obligationHistory` to `_mergeAppMemory`, which v2.78.0 had omitted, leaving that log silently per-device. |
| **2.78.1** | **Feat/revert: 12b companion voice.** Three lines added to the nudge instruction telling the model to use accumulated history as judgment rather than report it as counts. Supersedes three earlier prompt revisions (2.78.1–2.78.3 as originally cut, never pushed, collapsed into this one entry) which were reverted: worked examples collapsed the output onto a single template, and replacing "a friend noticing, not a coach" with a negative rule cost the warmth that made acknowledgment lines land. System prompt restored verbatim to its pre-12b baseline. |
| **2.78.0** | **Feat: obligation tasks stored by name with 90-day history.** `_aiCheckObligationLanguage` tightened — minimum 3 words, and "should/must be [adj]" excluded as descriptive rather than obligation framing. `obligationLanguageTally` gains `tasks[]` for the current week; new `obligationHistory` keeps a rolling 90-day log of `{ text, date, done }`, re-validated against the current detector on every load. `_memoryForAI` surfaces pending obligation tasks by name and a long-term completion rate once 10+ entries exist over 30 days. |
| **2.77.27** | **Reviewed poem geography and North-Star round.** Added five Can-approved selections to `assets/poems.js`: the closing couplet of al-Ma'arri's quatrain LXIX in Ameen Rihani's translation, Abu-Yshac's complete 'The Roses' in E. Powys Mathers's version, the first two stanzas of Raphael Patkanian's 'The Sure Hope' translated by Alice Stone Blackwell, the closing stanza of Claude McKay's 'Joy in the Woods', and Antonio Machado's 'Counsels' translated by Thomas Walsh. All named authors/translators died before 1956 and the text was checked against proofread or scanned public-domain editions. The corpus grows 114 → 119 (W14 / Sp16 / Su12 / Au10 / year-round 67). Four candidates from the latest round are recorded as final skips. Future candidates are limited to six displayed lines and gain a North-Star theme: choices, returning, releasing, and self-recognition around commitments—an imprint, not an instruction. Smoke coverage locks the approved and skipped candidate state. No storage or sync interfaces changed. |
| **2.77.20** | **Feat: task tip obligation detection + split guard.** New `obligation_language` tip type: if a task contains "have to", "need to", "should", "must", "ought to", "supposed to", or "have got to", the tip shows "Have to — or choosing to?" — client-side, no AI call. Split tip now requires ≥ 2 subtasks at both the render layer (`_aiShowSuggestion`) and the gate layer (`_aiDoAnalyze`), so a single-subtask AI response silently drops instead of showing a "Split into 1" button. |
| **2.77.19** | **Fix: numerals rule added to all About-panel AI nudges.** Week theme, Sunday reflection, and Monday intention system prompts now include "Use numerals for all numbers (3 not three)" — completing the numerals fix that v2.77.17 applied to the day nudge only. |
| **2.77.17** | **Fix: day nudge numerals + all-nudge voice consistency.** Day nudge system prompt now says "Use numerals for all numbers (3 not three)" and "Second person — address the user as 'you'". All remaining "About this person:" / "this person" references purged from `nudge.js`, `focus.js`, and `insights.js` (`_memoryForAI`). |
| **2.77.16** | **Fix: Noticed week nudge now speaks directly to you.** All three About-panel AI nudges (`_fetchWeekThemeAI`, `_fetchWeekReflection`, `_fetchMondayIntention`) had their prompts framing the user as "this person", which caused the AI to write in third-person ("This person consistently works..."). All three system prompts now explicitly say "Second person — address the user as 'you'", and user messages no longer say "this person". |
| **2.77.13** | **Debug: AI error logging.** `focus.js`, `gmail.js`, and `memory-panel.js` now log the exact Anthropic API error message to the browser console (`[focus ask]`, `[draft reply]`, `[memory abstract]`) when an `ai-assist` call fails, so the root cause of any 400/500 is visible in DevTools without digging into the Network tab. |
| **2.77.6** | **Fix BUG-089: “Open in Mail” leaves the PWA correctly.** The Gmail draft action now opens its `mailto:` link in a new external target so the operating system can hand it to the native Mail app instead of navigating inside the standalone app or browser. The action is also available outside installed-PWA mode. Awaiting real-device verification. |
| **2.77.5** | **Reviewed poem curation.** The traditional !kun 'Prayer to the Young Moon' closing excerpt returns to the daily rotation after Can approved it; José Rizal's 'To the Flowers of Heidelberg' was reviewed and skipped, and is now a final cut. A concurrent tone pass also removed Rilke's "Autumn"; together with the earlier Osler one-line cut, the reviewed corpus is 114. The smoke guard asserts the approved !kun entry is present and the skipped Rizal entry remains absent. |
| **2.77.4** | **Poem geography pass: !kun oral tradition and José Rizal.** Added the contiguous closing excerpt of 'Prayer to the Young Moon', a traditional !kun prayer recited by !nanni and recorded by Lucy Lloyd in 1880, plus the first stanza of Rizal's 'To the Flowers of Heidelberg' in Charles Derbyshire's translation. Both paths were checked against early scans/archives and are public domain worldwide; the attribution distinguishes !nanni's prayer from Dia!kwain's nearby Moon-and-Hare narrative. Corpus grows 115 → 116 (one grandfathered single-line aphorism removed for failing the 2-line minimum, two new poems added), with Africa and the Philippines entering the rotation for the first time. Smoke coverage now locks corpus count, schema, 2–11-line shape, season values, and both additions. |
| **2.77.3** | **Fix BUG-088: inline AI helper follows its task through reorder and render.** The helper row is intentionally a full-width sibling of `.task`, but drag and Option+Arrow reorder move only task elements; moving the suggested task upward could therefore leave the helper below a different task. `renderManual()` also replaces task markup wholesale and could detach a visible helper. The assistant now exposes an internal `_aiReanchorSuggestion()` that resolves the owner from its stable task ID and restores the helper immediately after that row. Drag persistence invokes it for pointer, touch, and keyboard moves; `renderManual()` invokes it after rebuilding. The active exposure/outcome record and helper element are preserved—no duplicate offer or storage/sync format change. Browser coverage verifies both reorder directions, one-helper-only render recovery, exposure, and dismissal. |
| **2.77.2** | **Fix BUG-087: emoji restored in the task input.** The animated input mirror indexed JavaScript strings by UTF-16 code unit and placed each unit in a separate span. That split surrogate pairs and prevented emoji—especially skin-tone, flag, and ZWJ sequences—from shaping as one glyph even though the native input value remained correct. `task-bounce.js` now segments text with `Intl.Segmenter({ granularity: 'grapheme' })`, falls back to code-point iteration, and uses grapheme counts for mirror rebuilding, insertion boundaries, and bulk-input detection. The native input, IME composition path, task storage, and sync formats are unchanged. Static coverage verifies the Unicode-safe implementation; the task-actions browser suite renders and saves `👩🏽‍💻` and `🇸🇪` intact. |
| **2.77.1** | **Fix: stats double-counting (BUG-086).** Dropbox merge was leaving a task in both `manualTasks` (TODAY) and `pastTasks` (PAST) when the TODAY entry had no `zoneChangedAt` — the guard required `inToday.zoneChangedAt` to exist before evicting from pastMap, so old untracked tasks that a remote device had moved to PAST stayed double-counted. Fix: TODAY now always wins over PAST when PAST has no timestamp, or when TODAY's timestamp is newer or equal. Same fix applied to `remotePast.forEach` entry guard. `updateStats` also gets a defensive `Math.min(done, total)` cap with `console.warn` logging if the impossible state ever occurs. |
| **2.76.4** | **Fix: agent enrichment timeout.** Netlify function had no timeout configured — default 10s was killing web search calls that take 15-25s. Added `[functions] timeout = 26` to `netlify.toml`. Model changed from `claude-sonnet-5` to `claude-opus-5` for better tool support. Internal `TIMEOUT_MS` set to 24s (2s headroom). Also handles `stop_reason: tool_use` alongside `pause_turn` by splitting tool_result blocks into the user turn per API spec. API errors now logged via `console.error` (visible in Netlify function logs). |
| **2.76.3** | **Fix: Gmail enrichment matches actual sender/recipient.** `_classifyTask` was returning a bare name ("Johanna") as `searchQuery`, and `_gmailSearch` passed it straight to the Gmail API as a free-text query — matching any email that contained the word anywhere in the body. Fix: `_buildQueryFallback` and the AI system prompt now produce `from:Name OR to:Name` (with quotes for multi-word names). Stale cached classifications in old format (no `from:`/`to:` operators) are detected on read and cleared along with their enrichment cache, so existing wrong matches re-fetch on next focus open. |
| **2.76.2** | **Fix: focus block now visible in focus mode.** `.focus-timer.open` had `max-height: 36px` (exact timer bar height) with `overflow: hidden`, so the Gmail `↩` and agent `↗` enrichment blocks were always clipped and never shown. Fix: `openUI` now sets `timerEl.style.maxHeight = timerEl.scrollHeight + 'px'` in the RAF after adding `.open`, expanding to fit actual content. For the async path (enrichment completes after focus opens), `window._focusExpandTimer` is exposed and called from `_doRenderBlock` in both `gmail.js` and `task-enrich.js`. `closeUI` resets the inline `maxHeight` so each open starts with the CSS 0→36px slide animation. |
| **2.76.1** | **Fix: remove stale BUG-082 diagnostics.** `console.warn`/`console.trace` blocks in `updateStats` (task-actions.js) and `mergeRemoteData` (dropbox.js) were firing as false positives at day start — `done=0` is normal when nothing is checked off yet, and `pastDone=0` is correct since past task IDs are cleaned from `doneIds` during day rollover. BUG-082 itself was fixed in v2.71.34; the diagnostic logging was left in accidentally. |
| **2.76.0** | **Feat: task agent enrichment (Stage 1).** Web search fires on task add for actionable tasks (URL present, or verbs: book, order, where, directions, price, hours, find, look up, compare, research, navigate, reserve, schedule). Backend: new `task-enrich` Netlify function calls Claude with `web_search_20250305` server tool; handles `pause_turn` continuation (max 3 turns); returns validated card `{ icon, headline, body, cta }` or `null`. Cache: `agent_enrichment_{taskId}` with explicit `success`/`no_result` states — transient failures not cached. UI: `↗` indicator in task row (same pattern as Gmail `↩`); full card renders in `#focusAgentBlock` when entering focus mode; cleared on focus close. Hook: `_agentEnrichTask` called alongside `_gmailEnrichTask` in `task-actions.js`; `_agentRestoreAllIndicators` called alongside Gmail's in `renderManual` (connections.js). |
| **2.75.22** | **Fix: legacy keyword history preserved through schema migration.** When `_memoryOnTaskComplete` created a new `days` object for a keyword that previously had only `{ completed: N }` (the old lifetime-total schema), it started the `days` object empty — causing `_kwCount` to return 0 for all prior observations (it sums `days` values and ignores `completed` once `days` exists). Fix: when `days` is missing and `completed` exists, copy it to `days['_legacy']` as a floor before writing the new day entry. |
| **2.75.21** | **Fix: Gmail focus block cleared before async fetch; race condition guard added.** Two P1 bugs from Codex review: (1) entering the on-demand async path in `_gmailRenderFocusBlock` no longer leaves the previous task's email visible — `block.hidden = true; block.innerHTML = ''` now runs before the classify+search chain starts; (2) a `dataset.focusTaskId` stamp on the block lets the `.then()` handler bail out if the user switched to a different task while the fetch was in-flight. All three `localStorage.setItem` calls in `_classifyTask` wrapped in try-catch so storage quota or privacy-mode errors return the fallback instead of rejecting. |
| **2.75.20** | **Fix: "Reflect" button now appears in Memory panel.** `renderMemoryPanel()` was setting `el.innerHTML` for the four cognitive blocks (SEMANTIC/EPISODIC/PROCEDURAL/META) but never calling `_reflectionRenderMemory(el)` afterward — so the HOW DAYS FELT section, including the Reflect button, was never injected into the DOM. One-line fix: call `window._reflectionRenderMemory(el)` after the innerHTML assignment, guarded by a `typeof` check for load-order safety. |
| **2.75.19** | **Fix: let-go and revive reason tracking switched from lifetime totals to per-day counts.** Same MAX-merge undercount as keywords — applies here because triage events are infrequent and may happen on different devices across different days. Schema migrated from `{ reason: N }` plain numbers to `{ reason: { days: { "YYYY-MM-DD": N } } }` objects; `_lrCount(v)` helper in `util.js` sums day values, falling back to plain numbers for legacy entries. Write in `_memoryOnTaskLetgo` and `_memoryOnRevive` (insights.js) now writes into `days[today]`, preserving any existing plain-number value into `days['_legacy']`. Merge in `_mergeAppMemory` (dropbox.js) does per-day MAX for both `letgoReasons` and `reviveReasons`, normalising both sides before comparing. Read sites in memory-panel.js and assistant.js updated to use `_lrCount`. |
| **2.75.18** | **Fix: task keyword tracking switched from a lifetime total to per-day counts.** Old schema `{ completed: N }` merged with MAX — cross-device completions on different days were silently lost. New schema `{ days: { "YYYY-MM-DD": N } }` merges with per-day MAX so each device's unique-day contributions survive sync. `_kwCount(kw)` helper in `util.js` sums day values, falling back to the legacy `completed` field for old entries. Write path in `_memoryOnTaskComplete` (insights.js) writes today's count into `days[today]` and prunes entries older than 90 days. Merge in `_mergeAppMemory` (dropbox.js) does per-day MAX; legacy remote entries without `.days` are bucketed into `days['_legacy']` as a floor. All three read sites (memory-panel render, `_memoryAbstract`, assistant greeting) updated to `_kwCount`. Also removed the always-zero `avgDaysToComplete` field from the AI abstract keyword line — it was never populated. |
| **2.75.17** | **Feat: Gmail comm-task detection upgraded from regex to AI.** `_isCommTask` + `_buildQuery` replaced by `_classifyTask(taskId, taskText)` — async, calls `ai-assist`, returns `{ isComm, searchQuery }`. Fast verb pre-filter skips the AI call for non-comm tasks. Result cached in `localStorage` (`gmail_classify_{taskId}`). Falls back to regex silently if AI fails. `searchQuery` stored in enrichment record; `gmailDisconnect` clears both `gmail_enrichment_*` and `gmail_classify_*` keys. |
| **2.75.16** | **Fix: re-checking a task no longer double-counts it in memory.** `_memoryOnTaskComplete` now checks `recentCompletedTasks` for an existing `text+date` entry before incrementing any counter (`totalTasksCompleted`, `completionsByHour`, `taskKeywords`, `taskLifespanSamples`). If the task was already recorded today, the call returns immediately — consistent with `recentCompletedTasks`' own sync dedup which already prevented list duplicates. Tasks without text (edge case) are unaffected by the guard and pass through as before. |
| **2.75.15** | **UX: header and recording buttons exit focus mode before opening their panel.** Logo, habits, connections, about, recording (voice note and meeting) were all inert while a focus session was active — un-inerted `sticky-header` so its buttons receive clicks. A single guard in the document click handler (`#todayLogo, #habitsBtn, #trelloBtn, #infoBtn, #voiceNoteBtn, #meetingBtn`) calls `closeUI(false)` and returns; the button's own onclick already fired the panel toggle before the document listener ran (bubbling order: element first, document second), so no double-call. |
| **2.75.13** | **Fix BUG-086: Memory completion rate corrected — denominator now includes tasks at day start.** `tasksDone` counts all completions (carried-over tasks + Trello + reactive additions) but `tasksAdded` only counted new tasks typed in that day, making >100% rates possible. Fix: `day-lifecycle.js` now stores `dayStartCount` (tasks on the list at the start of each day) alongside each `dailyHistory` entry. Memory panel's two completion-rate read paths (`renderMemoryPanel` and `_memoryAbstract`) use `dayStartCount + tasksAdded` as the denominator for entries that have it, falling back to `tasksAdded` alone for older entries. `_mergeDailyHistory` in `dropbox.js` merges `dayStartCount` with `Math.max` so neither device's observation is discarded. Label updated from "tasks added" to "tasks on the list" to match the corrected semantics. 4th root cause; prior passes: v2.71.9 (never reset), v2.71.11 (migration guard missing), v2.71.38–44 (cumulative artifacts surviving Dropbox sync). |
| **2.75.9** | **Fix: Gmail OAuth popup switched to postMessage — eliminates COOP warning.** Chrome warns that `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy` will eventually sever `popup.closed` / `popup.location` access when the popup navigates to Google's auth page. Replaced the `setInterval` poll in `_gmailDoAuth` with a `window.addEventListener('message', ...)` handler. A new early IIFE in `index.html` detects `window.opener` + `?code=` on load, sends `{ type: 'oauth_callback', search }` via `postMessage` to the origin, then closes — no cross-origin DOM access needed. A separate 800ms `setInterval` only checks `popup.closed` as a fallback for user-dismissed popups. |
| **2.75.8** | **Fix: Gmail thread search unblocked — CSP `connect-src` gap.** Every `_gmailFetch` call to `gmail.googleapis.com` was blocked by Content Security Policy: `connect-src` only listed Dropbox and Trello endpoints. Added `https://gmail.googleapis.com` and `https://accounts.google.com` to `connect-src` in `netlify.toml`. |
| **2.75.7** | **UX: tapping the add bar exits focus mode and opens input.** Previously tapping the add-bar area during a focus session did nothing visible. Now the tap exits focus mode and positions the cursor in the task input — enables adding the next task without separate gestures. |
| **2.75.6** | **Fix: Gmail Connect needs no Client ID input — served from Netlify env.** Removed the `gmailClientIdInput` field and three-branch `_gmailRowHTML` from `connections.js`. `gmail.js` now fetches client_id lazily via `GET /.netlify/functions/gmail-token` (new GET handler added to the function). `gmailDisconnect` also clears `gmail_client_id` from localStorage for existing installs. |
| **2.75.5** | **UI: removed add-bar submission animation.** Reverted the v2.74.0 accent-sweep animation on task submit. |
| **2.75.4** | **Fix: Gmail token exchange routed through Netlify function.** Google "Web application" OAuth clients are confidential — the browser cannot call `oauth2.googleapis.com/token` directly without a `client_secret` (CORS block). New `netlify/functions/gmail-token.js` mirrors `dropbox-token.js`: receives `{ code, code_verifier, redirect_uri }` or `{ refresh_token }`, POSTs to Google with `GMAIL_CLIENT_ID` + `GMAIL_CLIENT_SECRET` env vars, returns tokens. `gmail.js` `_gmailExchangeCode` and `_gmailRefreshTokens` now call `/.netlify/functions/gmail-token` instead. |
| **2.75.3** | **UI: add-bar confirmation changes from a sweep to a border pulse ring.** |
| **2.75.2** | **Fix: add-bar focus highlight on load; habit strength stays visible after flick animation.** |
| **2.75.1** | **Connections: Gmail enrichment.** New `assets/gmail.js` module: PKCE OAuth (`gmailAuth`), Gmail thread search, 24h enrichment cache in localStorage (`gmail_enrichment_{taskId}`). Pattern detection (`_isCommTask`) triggers on task add when action verb + capitalised name found. `✉` indicator appears on matching task rows. Focus overlay gains `#focusGmailBlock` — shows sender, date, thread snippet, and a lazy "Draft reply" button that calls `ai-assist` and writes the reply inline. Three new Google origins added to sw.js BYPASS_ORIGINS (`gmail.googleapis.com`, `oauth2.googleapis.com`, `accounts.google.com`). Gmail card added to connections panel (grid and stacked layouts). |
| **2.75.0** | **UI: habit strength flick + focus final exhale.** See prior entry. |
| **2.74.3** | **AI: focus companion sees the rest of your task list.** `_focusAskAI` now appends up to 5 other pending tasks (with age and session count) to the user message, giving the companion awareness of what else is waiting. Enables questions about prioritisation or energy fit relative to the full list — not just the current task in isolation. |
| **2.74.2** | **AI: week theme sees aging tasks alongside rhythm stats.** `_fetchWeekThemeAI` now sends pending tasks (2+ days old) with age and focus session count alongside the existing behavioral stats. System prompt updated to allow task-specific observations when they illuminate a real pattern. Week theme can now say "Answer to Mäklare has been waiting while your rhythm stayed strong" rather than only abstract timing observations. |
| **2.75.0** | **UI: habit strength flick + focus final exhale.** (1) When you log a habit and the strength % changes, the number flicks out translateY(-6px)/opacity→0 over 100ms ease-in, then the new value enters from translateY(6px) over 130ms spring. Same-value and `prefers-reduced-motion` guards skip it. (2) When a focus session timer reaches zero, `_breatheRun(fillEl, false)` cancels the session breathe immediately, then one final slow exhale — `{ opacity: 1 } → { opacity: 0.65 } → { opacity: 1 }` over 2500ms ease-in-out — plays before `_pulseComplete` begins its loop. `prefers-reduced-motion` skips straight to `_pulseComplete`. No storage, sync, or data changes. |
| **2.74.1** | **UI: habit today-dot spring pop on log.** When you check off a habit, the today dot (`.week-dot.is-today`) springs into its filled state — scale(0.4)→scale(1.5)→scale(1) + opacity(0.5→1) over 200ms with `cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)`. Per Motion.md small-element rule: opacity alone doesn't read at 5px, so scale is paired. `prefers-reduced-motion` guard skips animation. Unchecking is instant (no exit animation). No storage, sync, or data changes. |
| **2.74.0** | **UI: add-bar sent flash.** When you press Enter to add a task, a translucent accent sweep (rgba(200,240,96,0.18) gradient, 150ms ease-in-out) crosses the input field left→right before it clears. Confirms the submission the instant it happens. Implemented as a short-lived absolutely-positioned overlay appended to the input wrapper; removed in `onfinish`. Guards: empty input check already in `addManual`; `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` skips the animation. No storage, sync, or data changes. |
| **2.73.0** | **UI: zone task-count flick animation.** `.section-count` badges on the Soon and Past zone headers now animate when their count changes: the old digit exits with a 120ms ease-in translateY(-8px) + opacity fade, then the new digit enters with a 150ms spring (cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)) from translateY(8px). Guards: same-value no-ops skip animation; `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` falls back to a plain text swap. `display: inline-block` added to `.section-count` so WAAPI transforms apply. No storage, sync, or data changes. |
| **2.72.1** | **AI: post-add suggestions are delivered on viewport entry, not merely generated after a timer.** Task analysis still begins after the existing two-second debounce, but a successful result remains pending while its task is off-screen. An `IntersectionObserver` waits until the unchanged, undone row reaches a viewport zone with 64px of bottom space; only then is the suggestion inserted, animated, counted as offered, and handed to v2.72.0's visible-exposure clock. Pending state re-anchors by task ID through `renderManual()`/sync DOM replacement. A per-analysis sequence makes the newest eligible addition win even if an earlier provider response returns late. Editing, completing, deleting, moving, or superseding the task—or opening the AI panel—disconnects the observers without recording an offer or ignored outcome. The non-IntersectionObserver fallback checks the same delivery zone on scroll, resize, and foreground return. Assistant regressions verify deferred mount/offer, exposure handoff, newest-request wins, re-render re-anchoring, and pre-entry removal. No storage, sync, prompt-data, or visible-surface changes. **Release-history note:** the runtime portion was swept into the concurrent v2.72.0 commit while this patch was in progress; v2.72.1 completes its user-facing version/cache labeling, documentation, and corrected stale-edit-aware fixtures. |
| **2.72.0** | **AI: one measurable intervention loop on the existing post-add suggestion row.** Every inline offer now records a stable ID, recommendation pattern, explicit reason enum, visible reason text, and full timestamps for applied, dismissed, or ignored outcomes. Ignored requires ten accumulated seconds while the row intersects the viewport and the document is visible; off-screen/background time and programmatic replacement do not count. Applying a split also records its generated task IDs. Completing one generated step marks the recommendation helpful; after a ten-minute undo grace, recreating the original or discarding every generated step marks it later-reversed. Existing duplicate tasks are snapshotted at apply time so they cannot create false reversals. Reason-level policy waits for four decisions, then reduces a category with ≥70% dismissal/ignore/reversal evidence to deterministic one-in-four exploration rather than banning it; categories with completed-step evidence are identified to the model as preferred when several reasons fit. The old global suppression gate is removed so one weak category cannot silence every kind. Records are capped at 100, cleared with Memory, and Dropbox-merger unioned by offer ID with monotonic event/result preservation; no backup schema bump. No new UI surface or visible control. Added a dedicated non-network provider-free outcome suite plus assistant and Dropbox integration regressions; the default runner now covers 24 suites. |
| **2.71.44** | **Fix: daily task-add history is sanitized consistently across sync and Memory.** Added the shared `_sanitizeDailyTasksAdded()` leaf with a 30-task plausibility ceiling. `_mergeDailyHistory()` now normalizes local, duplicate-date, and remote-only entries, closing the one-sided restore path left by v2.71.43. Both completion-rate read paths consume the same sanitizer instead of duplicating numeric thresholds. Dropbox regression coverage keeps 30, rejects 31/42/70 across all merge shapes, and preserves a valid remote-only 12; Memory coverage verifies an invalid restored day cannot distort the displayed rate. No storage schema or backup version change. |
| **2.71.43** | **Fix: first-pass daily task-add plausibility ceiling.** Lowered the duplicate-date Dropbox merge and Memory completion-rate filters from 100 to 30 after cumulative migration artifacts around 70 per day survived the earlier threshold. Superseded by v2.71.44's shared sanitizer and one-sided merge coverage. |
| **2.71.42** | **Fix: remove the streak line from the Memory panel.** The streak already appears in the primary stats surface; removing the duplicate lowers noise without changing stored memory or sync behavior. |
| **2.71.41** | **Poems: Chang Chih-ho "A World Apart" (Cranmer-Byng 1909).** 8-line Tang dynasty poem on solitude and nature by Chang Chih-ho (c. 750), trans. L. Cranmer-Byng, from A Lute of Jade (1909), Gutenberg #390. Both PD worldwide. Corpus 115 poems. |
| **2.71.40** | **Poems: anon Tang "True Pleasures" (Giles 1898) + Sappho "If death be good" (Carman 1904).** Chinese anon. Tang poem trans. H.A. Giles, Chinese Poetry in English Verse (1898); Sappho lyric LXXIV trans. Bliss Carman, Sappho: One Hundred Lyrics (1904), Gutenberg #12389. Both PD worldwide. Corpus 114 poems. |
| **2.71.18** | **Full-runner inventory and flake enforcement.** `scripts/test-all.mjs` now discovers every local `*-test.mjs` file before running and compares that inventory with its ordered `SUITE`; a missing, stale, or duplicate entry fails immediately. `ai-test` is the sole explicit exclusion because it requires `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and real provider calls. Failed suites still receive one diagnostic retry, but a retry-only pass is now counted and reported as flaky and exits non-zero instead of masquerading as a clean pass. Design lint and memory-file validation remain separate pre-push gates. No runtime, storage, or sync behavior changed. |
| **2.71.17** | **Weekly-reflection unit boundary and Trello regression coverage.** Extracted the deterministic Sunday candidate ranker and output guard from `about.js` into DOM-free `assets/week-reflection-policy.js`, exposed through the same browser globals and CommonJS for direct Node testing. `week-reflection-unit-test.mjs` adds 16 cases for minimum samples, flat-week abstention, focus/habit/recurring-day/burst thresholds, candidate priority, and identity/causal/tenure/length rejection. Added `trello-test.mjs` with eight browser scenarios covering escaped board/list selection, OAuth-header secrecy, list/due/completion-grace filtering, synced order, checklist/tag/link/done rendering, cache/first-seen state, manual/background error behavior, post-Dropbox reconciliation, disconnect cleanup, popup failure, and static loading/precache. Both suites join `test-all.mjs`, taking the default non-credentialed runner to 23 suites. Runtime weekly-reflection behavior and all data/sync formats are unchanged. |
| **2.71.16** | **Task-bounce regression gate repaired.** `scripts/task-bounce-test.mjs` now asserts that the inner scrolling wrapper (`mirrorContent`) is cleared before rebuilding characters, matching the v2.67.0 implementation instead of the pre-wrapper `mirror` container. Added `task-bounce-test` to `scripts/test-all.mjs`; the default runner now executes all 21 local suites and continues to exclude only the credentialed live `ai-test`. No runtime behavior changed. |
| **2.71.15** | **Task action ownership completed.** The delegated task-row copy/check/delete handler moved from the inline composition root into `assets/task-actions.js`, alongside the mutations it invokes. `drawFavicon()` moved into the same module as the sole consumer, `updateStats()`, and remains private. The public task-action exports, startup order, user behavior, storage, and sync interfaces are unchanged. `task-actions-test.mjs` now exercises delegated copy feedback, focus interception, delegated deletion/haptics, favicon refresh, and verifies that both responsibilities are absent from `index.html`. |
| **2.71.34** | **Fix BUG-082 (proper fix): done count and Flow now use `_doneTodayCount()`.** Previous attempts added `pastTasks` to the spread but the real issue is `_clearAllDone()` deletes IDs from `doneIds` entirely — so no spread works. `_doneTodayCount()` (dropbox.js) reads the check/uncheck timestamp log in localStorage and survives clearing. Both graph bar (line 229) and Flow % (line 185) now call it. |
| **2.71.33** | **Attempted fix BUG-082 (wrong model): added `pastTasks` to Flow % spread.** Moot — `_clearAllDone()` removes done IDs from `doneIds` so filter returns 0 regardless. Superseded by v2.71.34. |
| **2.71.38** | **Fix: tasksAdded merge sanitizes values > 100.** Per-day delta values above 100 are cumulative artifacts. The v2 migration zeroed localStorage but the next Dropbox sync from the other device restored large values via `Math.max(0, 2086) = 2086` — the zero never stuck. `_mergeDailyHistory` now wraps both sides in `_sa()` (returns 0 if > 100) before the comparison, so cumulative artifacts are discarded at merge time regardless of which device they come from. Filter in memory-panel.js (`<= 100`) is belt-and-suspenders. |
| **2.71.37** | **Fix: focus kbd hint — explicit height centers labels, restores size.** `inline-flex` without `line-height: 1` used the browser's default line-height (~1.4), making the boxes grow. Root fix: `height: calc(1em + 6px)` with `padding: 0 var(--space-2)` and `line-height: 1` — the explicit height gives `align-items: center` actual slack (6px) to center the 1em text line, producing true vertical centering. Same visual size as the original `line-height:1` + `padding:3px` approach. |
| **2.71.36** | **Fix: focus kbd hint — inline-flex + vertical-align:middle.** `display: inline-block` with default `vertical-align: baseline` made the kbd boxes sit on the text baseline, appearing misaligned with "breathe" / "rest" beside them. Changed to `inline-flex` with `align-items: center`, `justify-content: center`, and `vertical-align: middle`. |
| **2.71.35** | **Perf: pre-warm WAAPI compositor at idle time.** First `el.animate()` call initializes compositor-thread infrastructure lazily — a one-time 50–200ms stall on mobile. `_breatheRun(fillEl)` in focus.js was the first call in a typical session (no prior WAAPI in celebration.js or task-actions.js for a focus-first flow). A no-op `fillEl.animate([{opacity:1},{opacity:1}], {duration:100, fill:'none'})` now fires via `requestIdleCallback({timeout:2000})` at module init, moving the cost before first tap. `setTimeout(300)` fallback covers Safari < 16.4. |
| **2.71.39** | **Poems: three Japanese couplets (Bashō + anonymous, trans. B.H. Chamberlain 1902).** Bashō #34 (toad's croak under the fancier's house), anonymous #2 (cool air through a shower / setting sun), Danrin school (garments chest / spring in hiding). All PD worldwide; verbatim verified. Corpus 112 poems. |
| **2.71.32** | **Poems: two more Tirukkural couplets (Thiruvalluvar, trans. G. U. Pope, 1886).** Kural 1306 ("Love without hatred is ripened fruit / Without some lesser strife, fruit immature") and Kural 1312 (the sneeze during a lovers' quarrel). Both verified verbatim from Wikisource Ch. 131-132. Corpus 109 poems. |
| **2.71.30** | **Poems: two Tirukkural couplets (Thiruvalluvar, trans. G. U. Pope, 1886).** Kural 72 ("Possession of Love" — loveless/loving contrast, "to the very bone") and Kural 78 (flowers on dead trees — impossibility image). Both PD worldwide (Thiruvalluvar ~300 CE, Pope d. 1908). Corpus 107 poems. |
| **2.71.25** | **Fix BUG-085: NEW WEEK nudge no longer names tasks completed before midnight.** `_fetchMondayIntention()` now sends explicit task-naming constraints: when the list is empty the AI is told not to name tasks; when tasks exist it is told to name only those in the current list (not from `suggestionHistory`). System prompt updated to match. |
| **2.71.23** | **Poem: one new Arabic voice.** Abd Alsalam Ben Raghib, "To a Female Cupbearer" (trans. J. D. Carlyle, 1796). Corpus 106 poems. |
| **2.71.22** | **Poems: two new voices.** Al-Shafi'i (Arabic, trans. Carlyle 1796) and a Korean seasonal verse (Korea Review, 1906). Corpus 105 poems. |
| **2.71.14** | **Poem corpus expansion.** Added two Latin American voices: María Fermina de Urbina and Rufino Blanco Fombona, translated by Alice Stone Blackwell. |
| **2.71.13** | **Fix BUG-083: Past→Soon revive black screen (iOS GPU compositor glitch).** `reviveFromPast()` in `zones.js` now runs an inline `#main-app` display-toggle repaint immediately after `renderPast()`/`renderSoon()`. When `soonSection` transitions from `display:none` → `display:block` for the first time during a revive, iOS Safari's GPU compositor layer goes stale and blacks out the screen (same family as BUG-004/056/071). The fix is identical in mechanism to `_forceRepaint()` in `dropbox.js` but inlined as an IIFE since `_forceRepaint` is scoped inside `_onWake` and not exported. Scroll position preserved across the toggle. |
| **2.71.12** | **Sunday reflection: earned personality from verified patterns.** `_buildWeekReflectionInsight()` now deterministically ranks focus/completion, habit/completion, recurring-weekday, and concentrated-burst candidates from the seven calendar days shown in About. Each candidate carries explicit evidence and a useful interpretation; `_fetchWeekReflection()` sends only the winner to the model for phrasing. Removed lifetime `_memoryForAI('weekly')`, total-days-active tenure, and the loose `recentCompletedTasks` list that produced the reported “Avios videos and manicures … that's who you are” faux insight. The prompt permits warmth, metaphor, and dry wit while banning invented facts, identity claims, and causal claims; `_weekReflectionTextIsGrounded()` programmatically rejects identity/causal/tenure overclaims. A flat week, missing AI, offline state, `none`, or invalid response hides the sentence; the bland counter fallback is removed because the grid already shows it. `week_policy_<date> = earned-v1` provides negative caching and invalidates earlier copy; Dropbox backs it up as the additive `week_reflection_policy` field and ignores reflection text without the current policy. About and Dropbox suites cover candidate selection, abstention, prompt isolation, overclaim rejection, stale-cache removal, and cross-device policy gating. Full-suite cleanup also restores the AI setup links with safe DOM construction (model text remains `textContent`) and updates stale meeting/reflection fixtures to observable async state and the six current feeling choices. No backup schema bump. |
| **2.71.11** | **Fix: data-accuracy audit — four bugs corrected.** (A) `_memoryAbstract()` in `memory-panel.js` now runs the same `tasksAddedFixed` migration guard as `renderMemoryPanel` before summing `tasksAdded`, preventing inflated completion-rate in AI prompts on fresh sessions where triage or Dropbox sync fires first. (B) Sunday/Monday reflection week habits (`_wHK`/`_wHT`) now derive from the same 7-calendar-day `_days` array as tasks and focus, instead of `_history.slice(-7)` which stretches further back whenever the user skips days. (C) `_mergeDailyHistory` in `dropbox.js` replaced winner-takes-all scoring with per-field `Math.max`, so neither device's `focusMins`, `habitsKept`, or `tasksAdded` is silently discarded. (D) Inline suggestion suppression gate now counts auto-expiry (10 s timeout) as `autoDismissed`; gate uses `(dismissed + autoDismissed) / offered > 0.7` — users who consistently ignore suggestions will now correctly stop receiving them. Schema adds `autoDismissed: 0` with migration guard. No backup schema version change. |
| **2.71.10** | **Delete-toast edit path.** The undo toast includes Edit, which restores the deleted task text to the input for quick correction and leaves the deletion committed. |
| **2.71.9** | **Fix: completion rate stat corrected — tasksAdded now resets daily.** `appMemory.patterns.tasksAddedToday` was a lifetime running total that never reset at day rollover; each `dailyHistory` entry stored a cumulative total (not a per-day count), so summing them across 30 entries grossly inflated the denominator. Three fixes: (1) `day-lifecycle.js` resets `tasksAddedToday = 0` after snapshotting it into `dailyHistory`; (2) `memory-panel.js` runs a one-time migration at panel open — converts stored cumulative totals to per-day deltas by diffing consecutive entries, marks entries with `tasksAddedFixed: true` so migration only runs once; (3) same migration guard in `about.js` so the "Noticed" AI text is also corrected before memory panel is opened. No sync or backup schema changes. |
| **2.71.8** | **Fix: mobile checkmark confetti uses the canvas coordinate space.** Checkbox origins arrive from `getBoundingClientRect()` in CSS viewport pixels, while `#celebCanvas` draws in backing-buffer pixels sized from `innerWidth`/`innerHeight`. On mobile Safari the CSS canvas (`100vw × 100dvh`) can differ from that backing size, so the browser scaled a correct-looking coordinate into the wrong visible position. `fireEmberDrift()` now converts each client point through the canvas’s live bounding rect before drawing. Desktop and all-done burst behavior are unchanged. The smoke suite forces mismatched CSS/backing dimensions and verifies the converted point. No data or sync interfaces changed. |
| **2.71.0** | **Season moments: solar term label.** `_noticedLines()` now returns `{ _season: true, term, line }` objects instead of plain strings when a sekki fires; `about.js` renders the Japanese solar term name (e.g. `処暑 · End of Heat`) above the evocative line in a muted `week-label` style via `.noticed-term`. Season moments suppress all other Noticed lines for that day — `about.js` early-returns the season entry when detected in `_prior` or `_fresh`. SEASON_MOMENTS map changed from `{ 'MM-DD': string }` to `{ 'MM-DD': { term, line } }` for all 24 sekki. |
| **2.70.1** | **UX: reflection feelings updated.** "Steady" renamed to "present"; "off" added. Mood buttons show surface background color. |
| **2.70.0** | **Poems: four new spring tanka from the Hyakunin Isshu** — Ki no Tomonori (33), Ki no Tsurayuki (35), Ise no Taifu (61), Ōe no Masafusa (73), all via William N. Porter (1909). Corpus now 101 poems. |
| **2.69.12** | **Fix: confetti canvas uses `100dvh` instead of `100vh`** so the burst aligns with the checkbox on iOS (where `100vh` overflows behind the status bar). |
| **2.69.11** | **UX: post-triage panel flush + mood grid.** Panel scrolls to bottom after triage; mood buttons 2-col grid with 12px/12px sizing. |
| **2.69.10** | **Fix: task count never exceeds total** — `pastDone` included in both `done` and `total` so the logo counter can't show done > total. |
| **2.69.2** | **Security hardening.** CSP + 5 security headers in `netlify.toml` (`X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy`, `Permissions-Policy`, `Strict-Transport-Security`); `connect-src` restricts exfil to known Dropbox/Trello endpoints. XSS: `esc()` applied to 4 AI-generated `innerHTML` surfaces (`about.js` week-reflection cache, `assistant.js` message box, `memory-panel.js` inference text, `reflections.js` reflection result); `assistant.js` `_aiSetMsg` switched to `textContent`. CORS origin on 3 Netlify AI-proxy functions locked to `ALLOWED_ORIGIN` env var (default `https://today-here.netlify.app`) instead of `*`. Trello OAuth token moved from URL query param to `Authorization: OAuth` header in all 4 API calls (`trello.js` + `dropbox.js`). Dropbox OAuth CSRF state now uses `crypto.getRandomValues()` instead of `Math.random()`. SW BYPASS_ORIGINS uses exact hostname match. `.gitignore` gains `.env*` entries. |
| **2.69.0** | **Feat: micro-delight — decision motion, focus breath, stat rollup, time texture.** (1) Triage decision animation: `_triageDecisionAnim(el, decision)` fires a WAAPI exit animation on the task row before re-rendering — kept snaps right (150ms spring), soon slides left (150ms spring), done scales + brightens (180ms spring), letgo shrinks-fades (130ms ease-in); re-render delayed 140–160ms; `prefers-reduced-motion` guard. (2) Focus breathing: `_breatheRun(fillEl, on)` pulses `#focusFill` opacity 1→0.88→1 at a 6s ease-in-out WAAPI infinite when timer is running, stops on pause/complete/close. Hooked into `setPaused()` so all state transitions are covered; reset on `openUI` reuse. (3) Stat counter rollup: `_rollupStats(el, parts)` renders numbers as 0 then counts up via cubic ease-out rAF, 300ms per stat, 150ms stagger. Handles `"N done"`, `"Nm focused"`, `"1h Nm focused"`, and `"day N"` patterns. (4) Time texture: `_applyTimeTexture()` runs at init — sets `--accent` hsl 5° cooler at dawn (5–8am), 5° warmer at dusk (6–10pm), slightly dimmer at night (10pm–5am); `#manualEmpty` copy becomes contextual ("Still early. Good." / "Wrapped up early." / "Rest is work too."); `assets/idle.js` IDLE_THRESHOLD drops from 45s to 25s at night. |
| **2.68.2** | **Quiet accessibility refinement + focus-copy fix.** `_applyDoneStyles()` restores the original 25% completed-row opacity and removes the v2.68.0 inline muted-color override while preserving `aria-pressed`, accessible names, and strikethrough. Main-surface muted/control tokens are softened to `#80808d` and `#636370`; elevated panels and focused rows inherit `#858594` and `#6b6b78`, keeping contextual 4.5:1 text and 3:1 control thresholds. The Memory footer’s v2.68.0 padding and 24px minimum targets are deliberately retained and regression-tested. Task-copy feedback now stores its timeout on the button; `_resetCopyFeedback()` clears the timer, label, and `.copied` class synchronously on focus close and defensively on open, while a generation guard ignores late clipboard callbacks after exit. Accessibility docs now record the completed-state WCAG 1.4.3/1.4.11 exception alongside 2.5.7. Tests cover the fade/semantics combination, contextual contrast, Memory footer sizing, and focus-copy lifecycle. No data or sync interfaces changed. |
| **2.68.1** | **UX: post-triage mood confirmation.** `_buildConfirmedHTML()` in `assets/reflections.js` now renders "today felt *feeling*." — surrounding text in muted DM Mono xs, word in accent base size. `.reflection-confirmed` CSS gains `font-family`, `font-size: var(--text-xs)`, and `color: var(--muted)` for the sentence wrapper. `reflectionSelect()` hides `#triageUndoBtn` after mood is set to remove the confusing undo-all prompt. `.reflection-feeling-btn` resting color changed from `var(--text)` to `var(--muted)` to match other TODAY pill buttons. |
| **2.68.0** | **Accessibility audit and remediation.** Added `assets/accessibility.js` with visually-hidden polite/assertive live regions, disclosure synchronization, modal/nonmodal dialog focus handling, temporary background `inert`, focus restoration, and shared row keyboard behavior. Reachable controls now use explicit native semantics, names, and expanded/pressed/busy/progress state; task and habit rows support Enter, Space, and persisted Option+Arrow reordering with boundary announcements. Triage and meeting review now behave as modal dialogs; header panels are disclosures; closed panels, overlays, toasts, backdrops, parked focus UI, and the unreachable AI sheet no longer leak into tab order or the accessibility tree. Focus mode isolates receded content, and focus/meeting PiP gain named keyboard controls, state/progress output, focus-within reveal, and reduced-motion handling. Added skip navigation, semantic headings/lists/forms, habit-history summaries, 24px targets, visible focus, 320px reflow, zoom-capable viewport metadata, stronger muted/control contrast, and poem-page semantics/share status. Added `scripts/accessibility-test.mjs` using axe-core/Puppeteer and integrated it into the full suite. Documented the deliberately accepted WCAG 2.2 criterion 2.5.7 exception: pointer reorder still requires dragging, so this release is not represented as fully WCAG-conformant. No storage, Dropbox, or Trello data interfaces changed. |
| **2.67.3** | **Fix: poem coda hold starts after fade-in completes.** Root cause: `_runPoemCoda()` started the hold timer (`Math.min(Math.max(5000, words×200), 8000)`) at the same instant `classList.add('visible')` was called, meaning the 900ms CSS fade-in consumed 900ms of the floor. A 30-word poem → 6000ms hold → only 5100ms at full opacity. Fix: added a 900ms inner `setTimeout` (matching the CSS transition) before starting `holdTimer` and attaching the `click`-to-dismiss listener. The hold floor/ceiling now represents actual reading time; accidental taps during fade-in no longer dismiss the poem on mobile. Also reverts v2.67.2 misdiagnosed changes: no-coda `overlayDelay` back to `420ms`, mobile poem CSS back to `var(--text-sm2)` / `line-height:1.7`. |
| **2.67.2** | **Fix (misdiagnosed, reverted in v2.67.3):** No-coda splash dismiss `overlayDelay` 420→750ms; mobile poem CSS to `clamp(12px,3.5vw,14px)` — both reverted as they addressed wrong root causes. |
| **2.67.1** | **Feat: background memory abstraction + new-item accent dot.** `_memoryAbstract()` moved from panel-open trigger to post-triage hook (`triageApplyAll` end, fire-and-forget async) plus cold-start once-per-day fallback in `_onWake`. `_memoryAbstracting` renamed `_memoryAbstractRunning` (concurrency guard kept; spinner UI removed). Each newly abstracted item gets `isNew: true` in its schema slot; flag cleared on panel open in `toggleMemory` (not in `renderMemoryPanel` — avoids clearing dots when panel is already open during a live abstraction). Memory panel footer: "noticing…" state and `@keyframes _noticingPulse` CSS removed entirely. New `.memory-item-new` CSS: 5px filled accent circle (opacity 0.6, no animation) next to `isNew` items in `typeBlock()`. Dropbox merge strips `isNew: false` from remote items (device A's notifications should not appear as new on device B). `assets/triage.js`, `assets/memory-panel.js`, `assets/dropbox.js` modified; CSS in `index.html`. |
| **2.67.0** | **Feat: per-character bounce animation on task input.** New `assets/task-bounce.js` (IIFE, ~90 lines). Technique: real `<input id="newTask">` keeps native focus/a11y/IME behaviour; its text is hidden via `.has-mirror { color: transparent; caret-color: var(--text) }`. An absolutely-positioned `<div id="newTaskMirror">` overlays it with one `<span>` per character. On each `input` event, the module rebuilds the mirror and applies `.mirror-char-new` (WAAPI `charBounce` keyframe — `translateY(-5px) scale(0.8) → translateY(1px) scale(1.03) → 0`, 120ms ease-out) to the newly inserted character spans only. The animation range is `[animateFrom, animateTo)` so only genuinely new chars bounce — chars that shift position on a mid-word insert stay static. Edge cases: IME (`compositionstart/end`) suppresses per-char animation and syncs the full composed string as a bulk insert; paste/autofill/drop detected via `InputEvent.inputType` + delta-count fallback (`addedCount > 1`) for browsers that omit `inputType` (iOS Safari autofill); programmatic `input.value = ''` calls in `addManual()`, `clearTaskInput()`, and `assistant.js` intercepted via `Object.defineProperty` on the element instance (zero changes to other files); `focus` event re-syncs if value changed while unfocused (meeting.js voice-note placeholder). Skips entirely when `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`. CSS keyframe wrapped in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` as belt-and-suspenders. New `scripts/task-bounce-test.mjs` (32 wiring tests — all green). |
| **2.66.5** | **UX: removed per-item "forget" buttons from Memory panel.** "clear all memory" is the single action for resetting AI memory — per-item forget added surface area without a meaningful incremental use case. |
| **2.66.4** | **Fix: "noticing…" pulses like the focus ask button.** `.memory-abstracting` in the Memory panel was static muted text. Added `@keyframes _noticingPulse` (opacity 0.25→0.65→0.25, 2.4s ease-in-out infinite) matching the WAAPI pulse on the focus ✦ ask button. Applied via `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` — users with reduced-motion get the static label unchanged. |
| **2.66.3** | **Feat: inline AI suggestions learn from user actions.** `assets/insights.js` — added `inlineSuggestions: { offered, applied, dismissed }` counter to `appMemory.patterns` schema plus migration guard. `assets/assistant.js` — `_aiShowSuggestion` increments `offered`; `_aiApplyBreakdown` increments `applied`; `_aiDismissSuggestion(source)` increments `dismissed` only when `source === 'user'` (auto-expire passes no source). `_aiDoAnalyze` suppresses future suggestions when `offered >= 5 && dismissed/offered > 0.7`; injects acceptance rate and dominant letgo reason into the inline system prompt when enough data exists. |
| **2.66.2** | **Fix: post-triage mood buttons restyled + selection confirmation.** Two UX issues in `assets/reflections.js` and `index.html`. (1) Mood buttons (`reflection-feeling-btn`) were missing `font-family: var(--font-mono)`, used `min-height: 52px` (tall square blocks), `border-radius: var(--radius-lg)`, and a `display: grid` container — all inconsistent with TODAY's pill/DM Mono design language. Fixed: layout changed to `display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center`, buttons restyled to `border-radius: var(--radius-full)`, `padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-3)`, `font-family: var(--font-mono)`, `font-size: var(--text-xs)`, transparent background — matching `triage-reason-btn`. Grid-column `@media` overrides removed (flex wraps naturally). (2) After selecting a mood, `reflectionSelect` hid the buttons but left the question text visible with no feedback — the user saw "Beyond what got done, how did today feel?" with no answer. Fixed: replaced the button-hiding logic with `el.innerHTML = _buildConfirmedHTML(feeling)` which renders the selected word in `var(--accent)` DM Mono inside a centred `reflection-confirmed` div. New CSS classes `reflection-confirmed` and `reflection-confirmed-word` added. |
| **2.66.1** | **Fix: BUG-079 age-bucket opacity scoped to `@media (hover: hover)`.** Age-bucket opacity rules (young 0.75, mid 0.55, old 0.35, hover restore 0.85) were outside any media query, applying globally including touch devices. On iOS, tapping a task briefly fires its `:hover` rule (brightening it to 0.85) while surrounding aged tasks remain dim — perceived as "tasks dim when another is tapped." Fix: wrapped the four opacity rules in `@media (hover: hover)` in `index.html`. Transition rule (`transition: opacity`) kept global (used by focus mode exit animations). Mobile tasks always render at full opacity. |
| **2.66.0** | **Maintenance: full-suite test runner + `_parseAIText` extraction fixes.** `scripts/test-all.mjs` added — runs all 19 `*-test.mjs` files in order, prints one pass/fail line per test, exits 1 with the last 6 lines of any failure. Bug fixed across three modules: `_parseAIText` was defined as a private function inside `nudge.js`'s IIFE closure and never exported, but `memory-panel.js` (line 482), `about.js` (lines 452, 553, 594), and `focus.js` (line 310) all called it bare — each call threw a silent `ReferenceError` caught by the surrounding try/catch, causing AI abstraction and focus companion responses to be silently discarded. Fix: added a local copy of `_parseAIText` at the top of each affected module's closure. Two test fixes: `triage-test.mjs` `setterCalled` assertion broadened to search `dropbox.js` in addition to `index.html` (the call site moved during dropbox extraction); `reflections-test.mjs` `cacheVersion` assertion changed from hardcoded `'today-v2.65.7'` to dynamically extract the first CHANGELOG key. |
| **2.65.17** | **Fix: BUG-078 `TRIAGE_HISTORY_MAX` out of scope in `dropbox.js`.** The constant was defined inside `triage.js`'s IIFE closure but referenced bare in `dropbox.js`'s merge path — threw `ReferenceError` on initial Dropbox pull and restore. Replaced with the literal `50`. |
| **2.65.16** | **Maintenance: `scripts/insights-test.mjs` added.** 12 regression tests for `assets/insights.js` (appMemory system — 825 lines, 18 functions, zero DOM, zero async). Tests cover: fresh-state schema init, seeded-memory init via `today_memory` localStorage, `_stripTag`, `_memoryOnTaskComplete` (totalTasksCompleted++, hour tracking, keyword indexing, recentCompletedTasks), `_memoryOnFocusComplete` (focusMinutesTotal accumulation), `_memoryOnTaskLetgo` (letgoReasons + dragKeywords array), `_memoryOnStreakUpdate` (bestStreak update, streak_milestone moment, no-downgrade guard), `_memoryOnDaySummary` (totalDaysActive++, big_clear gated at ≥5), `_memoryForAI` (non-empty string, nudge scope shorter than full), `_getProactiveObservations` (typed array with type/priority/text, streak_record fires), `_pickObservationToMention` (high-priority pick, 24h cooldown gates repeat type), static wiring (18 functions present, `appMemory` not on `window`). |

Moved as a private named function inside the `_startDayLifecycle` closure. No behavior change, no new exports. `day-lifecycle-test.mjs` static wiring test gains 2 assertions (`pruneRemovedFromIdx`, `pruneInModule`). |

| **2.65.12** | **Fix: "Read me" label corrected to sentence case.** Was "Read Me" — inconsistent with "This week" and "Changelog" which are sentence case. One-character label fix; no behaviour change. |
| **2.65.11** | **Fix: `connections.js` exports `_playTagInteractionShimmer`.** Was silently missing from the export block. `zones.js` calls `window._playTagInteractionShimmer(tagEl)` on tag interaction — would throw `ReferenceError` on Soon zone tag clicks. |
| **2.65.10** | **Fix: four memory-quality corrections.** (1) `dropbox.js` lateAdditions merge: was using `Set` (collapses duplicate hours, e.g. recording three 2pm additions as one), now picks the longer of local vs. remote arrays — duplicates are data. (2) `insights.js` task lifespan: now skips non-manual tasks (`manual_` prefix guard) — Trello/soon IDs have no reliable creation timestamp. (3) `memory-panel.js` reactive/planned label: switched from average-hour ≥ 11 to percentage-≥-14h ≥ 40% — more stable threshold that matches the "after 2pm" intuition. (4) `memory-panel.js` tracking-since UTC+ fix: `new Date(m.firstSeen)` parses a bare date string as UTC midnight, landing on yesterday for UTC+ users; fixed by appending `T12:00:00` to noon-anchor the parse. |
| **2.65.9** | **Maintenance: day-lifecycle.js extracted + midnight nudge reset fix + memory-panel date format.** `applyNewDayCleanup()` (~197 lines) extracted from inline `index.html` into `assets/day-lifecycle.js` (`_startDayLifecycle()` closure). 1 export: `window.applyNewDayCleanup`. `_ageSoon` and `_purgePast` already in `zones.js` — not moved. No strict mode (shares `let triageDismissedToday` with index.html global scope). Pre-existing bug fixed: `checkNewDay()` in `dropbox.js` (strict mode) was assigning to `_nudgeRendered`/`_nudgeRacing`/`_nudgeIsFallback` — now `window._nudgeRendered` is no longer global (lives in nudge.js closure); the direct assignments threw `ReferenceError` at midnight in strict mode. Fix: added `window._nudgeOnNewDay = function() { ... }` to nudge.js exports; `checkNewDay()` now calls `window._nudgeOnNewDay()` instead. Also bundled: `memory-panel.js` tracking-since date now formats as "Aug 17, 2026" instead of raw ISO string; task-added count now resets on new day. `scripts/day-lifecycle-test.mjs` (11 tests): first-open guard, same-day guard, done→PAST graduation, BUG-055 cross-device timestamp, BUG-063 midnight focus snapshot, streak+, streak break, tombstone purge, SOON→PAST aging, delayed backup, static wiring. |
| **2.65.8** | **Maintenance: nudge.js extracted.** Morning AI banner (`checkDayNudge` — race timer vs. AI fetch, stale-done invalidation, fallback upgrade gate) and badge nudge functions (`checkVersionNudge`, `checkSundayNudge`, `checkHabitNudge`) extracted from inline `index.html` into `assets/nudge.js` (`_startNudge()` closure, ~374 lines). Closure state: `_nudgeRendered`, `_nudgeRacing`, `_nudgeIsFallback`. Stays inline: `_aiBadgeShown` (write-shared between nudge.js and assistant.js via shared script scope). `_showNudge` const stays nested inside `checkDayNudge` (closes over `_dismissKey`). 4 exports: `checkDayNudge`, `checkVersionNudge`, `checkSundayNudge`, `checkHabitNudge`. Bug fixed during extraction: `_applyDoneStyles` was not exported from `task-actions.js`; added `window._applyDoneStyles` export so `connections.js` and `trello.js` calls succeed when done tasks exist on load. `scripts/nudge-test.mjs` (10 tests): cached AI, noon+ hidden, 1s fallback, fallback upgrade, stale-done invalidation, dismiss, already-dismissed, offline fallback, badge nudges, static wiring. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.65.6** | **Polish: TODAY header logo letter-spacing hover animation.** On hover, the `h1` letter-spacing expands from its tight baseline (`clamp(-1px, -0.04em, -2px)`) to `0.10em` over 300ms using `--ease-out`. Wrapped in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` — the existing `transition: color var(--dur-fast)` stays on the base rule so reduced-motion users retain color feedback; only the letter-spacing transition is gated. CSS is correct here (Rule 29 — hover is not a loop, WAAPI not needed). Implementation: one `@media` block after `.header-left h1` base rule, no HTML changes. The `0.10em` target is a starting point the user intends to refine live. |
| **2.65.5** | **Maintenance: task actions refactored.** Task management actions (adding, checking off, deleting, and undoing) reorganised internally with no change to visible behaviour. `scripts/task-actions-test.mjs` added. |
| **2.65.4** | **Fix: BUG-077 Trello "Network error" flash in Connections panel on reconnect.** `loadTrello()` was called without `fromSync=true` in two background paths in `dropbox.js`: (1) Dropbox reconnect after offline, and (2) midnight new-day boundary. Without the flag, any transient network error during reconnection opened the config panel with the alarming "check your API Key and Token" message. Both calls now pass `fromSync=true`. Pre-extraction, the inline code at those sites had the same bug — this is not a regression, just newly noticed. |
| **2.65.3** | **Fix: BUG-076 Safari splash exit completion.** The staged exit previously launched separate accelerated opacity animations for all five letter spans and relied on animation fill state, allowing Safari/WebKit to intermittently leave `O` and `AY` visible while the poem coda disappeared. Static `#splash-word-to` and `#splash-word-day` wrappers now own one animation each; `_fade()` uses explicit `1 → 0` keyframes and persists `style.opacity = '0'` beneath the animation. TO/DAY/date/burst/coda timing is unchanged. `scripts/splash-test.mjs` now repeats desktop and 375px exits, verifies word-layer ownership and ordering, rejects per-letter animations, and confirms final opacity cannot reappear. This deployment also includes the `_fetchMondayIntention` source correction documented in v2.65.1. |
| **2.65.2** | **Module extraction: `assistant.js`.** AI assistant panel (toggleAI, openAI, closeAI, _aiShowSetup, _aiLoad, _aiIntroMessage, _aiSendFromInput, _aiAskFromPanel, _aiRenderResult, _aiSetThinking, _aiSetMsg, _aiSetChips, _showNlRow, _hideNlRow, _aiCall, _aiContext, _getWeeklyStats, _aiSystemPrompt, _aiExecute) and post-add suggestion controller (_aiAnalyzeTask, _aiDoAnalyze, _aiShowSuggestion, _aiDismissSuggestion, _aiApplyBreakdown, _aiClearBadge) extracted from inline `index.html` into `assets/assistant.js` (`_startAssistant()` closure, 1,246 lines). Closure state: `_aiLoadedOnce`, `_aiReqSeq`, `_aiThread`, `_aiAnalyzeTimeout`, `_aiCurrentSuggestion`. Stays inline: `_aiPanelOpen` (read by ESC listener / `_updateBarPlaceholder`), `_aiBadgeShown` (written by `checkDayNudge`). ESC listener stays inline. 8 exports: `toggleAI`, `openAI`, `closeAI`, `_aiAskFromPanel`, `_aiAnalyzeTask`, `_aiDismissSuggestion`, `_aiSendFromInput`, `_aiApplyBreakdown`. Fix during extraction: `_aiShowSuggestion` and `_aiApplyBreakdown` used `data-task-id` (hyphenated) but DOM uses `data-taskid` — corrected to `data-taskid` with `CSS.escape`. Smoke-test guard updated: BUG-073 exact string check updated to match v2.65.0 focus companion prompt text (em dash, not semicolon). `scripts/assistant-test.mjs`: 9 tests (panel open/close, setup screen, thinking state, chips render, add_task action, post-add suggest + dismiss, breakdown apply, static wiring). Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.65.1** | **Fix: `_fetchMondayIntention` now includes all task sources.** Was: top 5 manual tasks only. Now: manual (undone, up to 5) + Soon (up to 4) + Trello cards (up to 4), each in a labeled section. `pastTasks` and `doneIds` filtered from manual list to avoid showing already-completed tasks. |
| **2.65.0** | **Improvement: focus companion (`_focusAskAI`) question quality.** Root cause: system prompt described tone but gave no taxonomy of what makes a question unblocking; `dragKeywords` and `letgoReasons` from `appMemory.patterns` were never used; no last-worked recency signal existed. Changes: (1) Added `_workedToday` / `_lastWorkedDaysAgo` from `manualTask.lastActive` — companion now knows if the task was already worked on today vs. N days ago. (2) Added drag-word match — task words intersected with `appMemory.preferences.dragKeywords` (freq ≥ 2, len > 3); surfaced as explicit context. (3) Added dominant letgo reason from `appMemory.patterns.letgoReasons` (≥35% of ≥8 total). (4) Rewrote system prompt: role changed from "quiet companion" to "focus catalyst"; explicit taxonomy of question types (scope-setter, obstacle-surfacer, scope-challenger, pick-up, pivot, revival check, deferred check, avoidance probe, energy-fit, peak lever, wind-down); bad-question list added (vague check-ins, affirmations, obvious yes/no). (5) Word cap increased 18 → 22. `memory/architecture/AI.md` signals table updated. |
| **2.64.38** | **Fold-in: AI provider config → `connections.js`.** `AI_BUILD_PROVIDER`/`AI_DEFAULT_PROVIDER` constants, `_aiGetProvider`/`_aiGetKey`/`_aiIsConfigured` helpers, `_aiInit` IIFE (legacy key migration + first-visit provider seed), and config panel functions `_aiRenderConfig`/`_aiUpdateConnectBtn`/`saveAIKey`/`clearAIKey`/`setDefaultProvider` folded from inline `index.html` into `assets/connections.js` `_startConnections()` closure. 8 new `window.*` exports (total 23). `_aiRenderConfig` is now closure-local; `toggleConfig`'s `setTimeout(_aiRenderConfig, 0)` uses the closure reference. Panel state vars `_aiLoadedOnce`/`_aiReqSeq`/`_aiThread` stay inline (AI assistant panel, not provider config). `connections-test.mjs` updated: `waitForFunction` adds `saveAIKey`/`_aiGetKey` checks, removed stale `window._aiRenderConfig` stub (function is test-safe when called for real), 6 new tests (provider/key basics, `_aiIsConfigured`, `_aiRenderConfig` renders rows, `saveAIKey` valid key saves, `clearAIKey` removes and switches default, `setDefaultProvider` switches default). 17 tests total; static wiring check verifies 23 exports and AI functions removed from `index.html`. |
| **2.64.37** | **Fix: BUG-076 splash word groups no longer leave `O` / `AY` behind in Safari.** The staged exit previously launched separate accelerated WAAPI opacity animations for all five letter spans, in a two-animation `T`/`O` batch followed by a three-animation `D`/`A`/`Y` batch, then depended solely on `fill: 'forwards'`. Safari/WebKit could lose later sibling compositor/fill states while the longer poem coda continued, exposing exactly `O` and `AY`. Static `#splash-word-to` and `#splash-word-day` wrappers now own one opacity animation each. `_fade()` uses explicit `1 → 0` keyframes and also persists `style.opacity = '0'`, so loss of an animation fill cannot reveal content. TO/DAY/date/burst/coda timing is unchanged. `scripts/splash-test.mjs` now repeats desktop and 375px exits, verifies word-layer ownership and ordering, rejects per-letter animations, and confirms both persisted final states. |
| **2.64.36** | **Module extraction: `dropbox.js`.** Dropbox auth (PKCE), backup/restore, mergeRemoteData, Trello/Dropbox sync ticker, wake handling, and state helpers (checked/unchecked/deleted ID logs, Trello tracking maps) extracted from inline script to `assets/dropbox.js` (1,975 lines). The live-sync IIFE (syncTrello, syncDropbox, checkNewDay, startTicker/stopTicker, 4 event listeners) was inlined directly into `_startDropbox()` body — IIFE wrapper stripped. 27 exports; `_pendingBackup` and `_backupAttempt` move to closure (shared between `_scheduleBackup` and `syncDropbox`). `_appReady` stays as an inline global — `assets/splash.js` writes it as a bare identifier (`_appReady = true`). New `scripts/dropbox-test.mjs` (11 assertions: tombstone write/read, doneTodayCount zero and counted, setLastLocalChange, checked/unchecked round-trips, autoSave no-op without token, backup returns false without token, mergeRemoteData adds remote task, mergeRemoteData calls renderManual, module wiring). Smoke test updated: backup block regex and privacy-key count now span `indexSrc + dropboxSrc`. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.52.0** | **Memory panel: 4 product-thinking improvements.** (1) SEMANTIC peak-hour widened to a productive window — algorithm groups hours within 50% of peak count into consecutive runs (e.g. "9am–11am" instead of "around 9am"). (2) EPISODIC focus-correlation moved to PROCEDURAL — it uses all-time history, not "lately". Wording tightened: "focus days average X tasks vs Y without a session". (3) META tracking-since line now shows "active on M of N days" using `totalDaysActive` vs calendar span. (4) Deferred vocabulary tracked: `_memoryOnTaskLetgo()` added to insights.js, called at triage letgo — extracts normalized keywords into `appMemory.preferences.dragKeywords` (rolling 100). When 10+ entries and a word appears 2+ times, PROCEDURAL shows "tends to defer: X, Y". Focus hours moved to META (was PROCEDURAL). |
| **2.51.0** | **Memory: focus/habit correlations surfaced in panel + AI inference flow improved.** EPISODIC: "focus days: X tasks avg vs Y without" (shown if ≥5 focus days and ≥5 non-focus days in `today_daily_history`). PROCEDURAL: "all habits done: X tasks avg vs Y on missed-habit days" (shown if ≥7 habit days, ≥3 full and ≥3 partial). `_memoryAbstract()` enriched: same two correlations added to AI data payload, plus completion rate ratio; DOW timezone bug fixed (`new Date(e.date).getDay()` → `_parseDowAbs` local-date parser); prompt reframed from "describes the person" → "surfaces a pattern useful for deciding what to work on or when to start"; cap ≤10 → ≤15 words; 1–2 → 2–3 observations. AI inferences now auto-confirm (no ✓ step) — ✕ dismiss remains. Pending rendering path simplified: italic + confirm button removed, all items render uniformly with a "forget" dismiss button. CSS `.memory-item-pending`, `.memory-item-confirm`, `.memory-item-actions` removed. |
| **2.50.0** | **Memory panel insight expansion.** EPISODIC: added "best day this week" (max `tasksDone` day in last 7, shown if ≥3 tasks) and "averaging N tasks on active days" (shown if ≥3 active days this week). SEMANTIC: added day-of-week preference derived from all `today_daily_history` (shown if ≥14 entries, ≥3 days have ≥2 samples, and top day beats second by >20%). PROCEDURAL: keyword cap raised from 3→5; completion rate added (`tasksAdded` field now saved in daily_history at day rollover, incremented in `addManual()`, displayed once ≥5 days of data). |
| **2.49.1** | Polish: `poem-block` `padding-right` increased from 44px to 64px — clears the absolute-positioned share button (right:8px + ~50px wide = left edge at ~58px) on narrow mobile screens where poem lines wrap closer to the right edge. |
| **2.49.0** | **Nav reorganization: TODAY logo opens Memory panel; ✦ star button removed from input bar.** `<h1 id="todayLogo">` gains `onclick="toggleMemory()"` and `title="Memory"`. `.header-left h1` gains `cursor:pointer`, `user-select:none`, `transition:color var(--dur-fast)`; glows `var(--accent)` when the panel is open via `.header-left h1.active`. `$.todayLogo` caches the element; `syncActiveButtons` toggles `.active` on it. `_aiClearBadge()` selector fixed from `.add-ai-btn .ai-badge` (never matched) to `#todayLogo .ai-badge`. Offline/online text-swap handlers on the old button removed — inapplicable to an h1. AI panel active-state add/remove on the old button removed. All 9 `addAiBtn` references cleared. |
| **2.48.4** | **Fix: Trello card 🍅 sessions persist across days (BUG-068).** Added `today_trello_focus_total` — a permanent per-card session counter that lives alongside the existing daily `today_trello_focus` map. The daily map keeps its role as the activity/un-dim signal (BUG-043/064); the total map is what displays on the badge and feeds the triage AI. Syncs cross-device via MAX-merge (no date guard). Pruned in `loadTrello()` when cards leave the board, matching the pattern of `today_trello_firstseen` and `today_trello_lastactive`. |
| **2.48.3** | **Fix: focus ask button — "thinking…" text restored during loading.** Animation overhauled: uses `focusAIBtn.animate()` directly (stored in `_thinkAnim` closure var) with a gentle breath `[{opacity:0.2},{opacity:0.65},{opacity:0.2}]` at 2000ms instead of `_KF_BLINK`'s harsh 0.2→1.0 spike at 1200ms. `_thinkAnim` is explicitly cancelled before the question text is shown (so the question doesn't pulse) and before `_focusResetAI()` restores "✦︎ ask" (so the reset button doesn't pulse). Previous `_breatheAnim` approach was broken: `_breathe()` doesn't return the animation, so `_breatheAnim` stored `undefined` and could never be cancelled. |
| **2.48.2** | **Sunday recap + Monday intention enriched with memory context.** Both `_fetchWeekReflection()` and `_fetchMondayIntention()` now inject `_memoryForAI('weekly')` into the AI prompt — peak hour, behavioral patterns, confirmed inferences, lifespan, task style, suggestion history. System prompt updated to instruct specificity over generics. Old cached values (keyed by date) are unaffected; new memory-enriched versions generate on the next Sunday/Monday. To test today: `localStorage.removeItem('monday_intention_' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('sv'))` in console then reopen About. |
| **2.48.1** | **Memory panel data quality fixes (three issues from live data).** (1) Lifespan humanized: `< 0.5 days` → "most tasks close the same day", `< 1.5` → "about a day to close", otherwise rounded integer. (2) Streak deduplication: SEMANTIC "longest streak" only shows when `best > curStreak`; when they're equal the EPISODIC "on a N-day streak" already covers it. (3) Keyword noise cleaned: stop-word list added (when/what/with/done/that etc.), min word length raised from >3 to >=5 chars, trailing non-alpha stripped at extraction time in `insights.js` and at display time in `renderMemoryPanel()`. |
| **2.48.0** | **Memory abstraction: AI surfaces pending inferences from behavioral data.** On Memory panel open (once/day), `_memoryAbstract()` calls the AI with a compact behavioral summary (completion hours, task lifespan, late-add pattern, top keywords, day-of-week cadence). Returns 1–2 cross-cutting observations as typed inferences (`semantic`/`episodic`/`procedural`), stored with `status:'pending'`. Panel renders pending items in italic with ✓ confirm / ✕ dismiss buttons (Step 4). `_memoryForAI()` in `insights.js` now appends user-confirmed inferences into AI context for morning nudge, focus companion, and triage hints (Step 5). |
| **2.47.0** | **Memory panel: ✦ in task bar now opens Memory, AI ask flow removed.** `addAiBtn` repurposed from `handleBarSubmit()` to `toggleMemory()`. `handleBarSubmit()` removed (dead code). `handleBarKey()` simplified — Enter always calls `addManual()`, Escape still closes AI panel from other surfaces. `toggleClearBtn()` simplified — AI ask-state removed. `_updateBarPlaceholder()` always shows default placeholder. `_aiProactiveSuggestion()` disabled (was badging the now-Memory button). `add-ai-btn` CSS replaced with simple `memory-btn`. `#memoryPanel` added (config-panel pattern). `toggleMemory()`, `renderMemoryPanel()`, `_memoryForget()`, `_memoryClearAll()` added. `appMemory.memory` typed slots (`semantic`, `episodic`, `procedural`) added to schema with migration guard in `insights.js`. Panel shows four-type taxonomy with derived inferences from existing behavioral data. |
| **2.46.1** | **Focus companion question now reads task history.** `_focusAskAI` looks up the task object by `uiTaskId` in `manualTasks` (Trello falls back to `_getTrelloFocus()`). Extracts: `focusSessions` count, age in days, `revived` flag, `zoneChangedAt` (was-deferred). Builds a `_ctx` array of signals and appends them to the user message (`Task: "..."\nContext: N sessions, 7 days old, revived from past`). System prompt updated to a backtick template literal with per-context coaching guidance: first session → done-enough framing; multiple sessions → curious about what's in the way; revived → what changed; long-standing/deferred → gentle check-in. Fixed two syntax errors: apostrophe in CHANGELOG entry, literal newline in single-quoted JS string (both escaped-character hazards per feedback memory). |
| **2.46.0** | **Fix: focus progress fill stays visible during AI question states.** Removed `.focus-timer-fill` from the `.focus-timer.ai-active` fade rule — only `.focus-timer-time` and `#focusPaused` fade out. The fill continues animating behind the answer text (it's `position: absolute` at z-index below the button). |
| **2.45.9** | **Fix: Trello cards due today now carry a signal in the morning nudge prompt.** `dueDate < todayStr` only matched overdue (past) cards. A card due today (`dueDate === todayStr`) got no marker — the AI saw just the card name with no urgency signal. Added `dueDate === todayStr ? ' — due today'` branch so same-day deadlines are explicitly flagged. |
| **2.45.8** | **Fix: focus timer bar no longer changes height when AI question is active.** Removed `max-height: 80px` / `height: auto` expansion from `.focus-timer.ai-active`.
