Sequencing note: this ships *before* Noticed's own W3 verdict (due 2026-08-02) — a deliberate call by Can after judging the current four signals insufficient, not an oversight of the gate. |
| **2.37.9** | **Fix: Noticed's theme-of-week keyword miner let generic verbs through.** Can's report: got "'take' keeps coming up this week" — read as a real theme but was just a common light verb showing up across unrelated tasks ("take out trash", "take a call", etc.), not an actual thematic signal. Same class of gap as v2.36.2's tag-prefix fix. The stop-word list already excluded `call`/`send`/`make` as low-information action verbs but missed close siblings — `take`, `get`, `keep`, `give`, `need`, `want`, `check`, `look`, `come`, `done`, `going` added to `_noticedLines()`'s theme-of-week stop set (`assets/insights.js`). |
| **2.37.8** | **Fix: BUG-061 — Sunday/habit badges could silently fail on a fresh device (same root cause as BUG-060).** Found by audit, not yet reported: `checkSundayNudge()` reads `today_daily_history` and `checkHabitNudge()` reads `habitsList`/`habitCompletions` — both genuinely Dropbox-synced, both read from module-level variables initialized once at script-parse time. Both are called only from `init()`, which runs before Dropbox restore lands — on a fresh device they see empty/stale local state and silently return early, no badge, even when real synced data exists that should trigger one. Unlike BUG-060 this is a false negative rather than a false positive, but the identical architectural gap: neither function had ever been added to the post-merge re-check pattern already established for `checkTriageBar()`/`checkDayNudge()`. Fix: both now get re-checked at the same two post-merge points `checkDayNudge()` already uses (the Dropbox restore path and the primary cold-start load handler). |
| **2.37.7** | **Fix: BUG-060 — completed Trello card reappeared as active on a fresh device connect.** Can's report: an old, overdue Trello task he'd already completed showed up on the list when he connected a new desktop PWA. Root cause: `loadTrello()` fires from `init()` before Dropbox restore lands, so `doneIds` is still an empty `Set` on a genuinely fresh device — the completed card's own Trello record has no local "done" flag yet, so it passes the "overdue, not done → show" filter. This would normally self-correct on the next 7s ticker cycle, except `syncTrello()` only re-fetches Trello when the board's own `dateLastActivity` changes — and that value gets seeded to current *before* the Dropbox merge runs, and never changes again since the completion only ever happened in TODAY, never on the real Trello card. Same bug class as `checkTriageBar()`/`checkDayNudge()`'s existing post-merge re-checks — `loadTrello()` had never been given one. New `_reconcileTrelloAfterMerge()` in `trello.js`, called right after `mergeRemoteData()` in the load handler: re-filters the already-loaded `trelloTasks` against the now-correct `doneIds` using the same done+grace-window rule `loadTrello()` already applies. No extra Trello API call. |
| **2.37.6** | **Fix: morning nudge could be generated minutes before it was actually read.** Can's report: the nudge sometimes referenced tasks he'd already checked off by the time he saw it. Root cause: `checkDayNudge()` fires from `init()` before the Dropbox sync pull lands (the new-day-cleanup code has an explicit comment about waiting for this same reason; the nudge check didn't). Since the AI nudge generates once per day and caches permanently (`day_nudge_ai_<date>`, never regenerated for the rest of that day), whichever call happens to be first to find no cache "wins" — and until now that was always the pre-sync `init()` call, using whatever state this device had before it heard from Dropbox. `checkDayNudge()` now takes an `allowGenerate` param (default `true`); the `init()` call passes `false` — it can still show an already-cached line, just can't be the one to generate for the first time. Every other call site (post-restore, remote `day_nudge_ai` arrival, and the post-sync re-check already in the `window 'load'` handler) keeps default `true`. Net effect: on a genuine cold start, the post-sync re-check — which fires right before `_appLoadDone` gates splash dismissal — becomes the one generating the nudge, so it reads the just-synced, cross-device-current task list, and generation overlaps time already being spent on the splash animation instead of adding a new wait. |
| **2.37.5** | **Housekeeping: `/design-review` findings on the meeting mode work.** Two real findings, both fixed: (1) Rule 31 violation — the `index.html` `CHANGELOG` object had drifted to 15 entries (v2.36.0 through v2.37.4) against the hard 3-entry cap (`slice(0, 1 + HISTORY_SHOWN)` in the About panel never shows past 3 anyway); trimmed back to the current 3. (2) The v2.37.3 entry's parenthetical — "(Reverts v2.37.2's approach — hiding items was the wrong fix.)" — named a version number and narrated an internal implementation reversal in user-facing copy, exactly the "root-cause archaeology" Rule 31 exists to keep out. Removed; the entry now says what changed, not how we got there. |
| **2.37.4** | **Fix + feature: meeting mode attributes by speaker, and now measures its own accuracy.** Can's follow-up on v2.37.3: that fix over-corrected — it treated every unnamed first-person "I'll..." as the user's by default, but a meeting has more than one voice, and more than one person self-commits to their own task. Real fix: the prompt now tracks speaker turns and tags a self-commitment's `owner` with whichever speaker said it (using their real name if the conversation reveals it elsewhere — a greeting, an introduction, someone addressing them), falling back to assuming the user only when a speaker truly can't be distinguished from them. The server-side owner/mine safeguard is now bidirectional and fully authoritative: `owner` decides `mine` in both directions (a named other person now forces `mine:false` even if the model's own boolean disagreed, not just the reverse). Feature: `appMemory.meetingAttribution` — four cumulative counters (`mineShown`/`mineKept`/`othersShown`/`othersSelected`) recorded on every `_meetingAccept()`, comparing what the model called mine/others against what the user actually kept selected. No UI surface yet (no Wallpaper Test needed — nothing recurs on screen); synced max-wins like the other lifetime counters in `appMemory`. Exists so real attribution accuracy can be checked on request rather than guessed at from anecdote. |
| **2.37.3** | **Fix: meeting mode attribution — two real misses, wrong approach reverted.** Can's report: (1) unnamed self-commitments ("I'll send that") were sometimes missed entirely — the prompt required either explicit assignment by name or being addressed by name by someone else, so a task nobody named out loud defaulted to `mine:false` and, after v2.37.2, vanished with no way to recover it; (2) some items showed the user's own name as owner but weren't pre-selected — the model's `owner` and `mine` fields could disagree with each other. Reverted v2.37.2's hard client-side filter (hiding non-mine items was the wrong fix — it turned false negatives from recoverable-but-unselected into invisible). Real fix, two parts: (a) prompt now treats an unnamed first-person commitment ("I'll...", "let me...") as `${name}`'s by default — `${name}` is often never spoken aloud in their own meeting, so "unclear" was the wrong default; `mine:false` now requires a specific *other* named person as the clear owner; (b) server-side post-process safeguard in `meeting-extract.js` — if `owner` is empty or matches `${name}` (case-insensitive, checked against every name in the Connections list), `mine` is forced `true` regardless of what the model returned, so the two fields can no longer visibly contradict each other. Net effect: fewer real tasks missed, and the "unclear" bucket now leans toward showing you the item (pre-selected, easy to deselect) rather than assuming it isn't yours. |
| **2.37.2** | **Fix: meeting mode showed items meant for someone else in the review list.** Can's finding: the mic button's own tooltip promises "leaves behind only your tasks," but the review overlay listed every extracted action item — including ones clearly assigned to a named colleague — just unselected, so the user had to visually filter them out each time. Root: `mine: false` items were still pushed into `state.items` for display, only skipped at accept-time by not being pre-selected. Fix: filter at capture time — `if (!item.mine) return` before an item ever enters `state.items`. Removed the now-dead `.meeting-owner` badge, its CSS rule, and the `:not(.meeting-owner)` selector in `_meetingAccept()`. Review sub-copy updated from "Yours are pre-selected · tap others to grab them" to "Tap to leave one out" — there's no longer an "others" category to describe. Server-side attribution logic in `meeting-extract.js` unchanged; this is a client-side display fix. |
| **2.37.1** | **Poem corpus: round 23 — three Teasdale additions (93 → 96, winter and summer).** All from *Stars To-night* (1930), archive.org scan, verified verbatim; Teasdale d. 1933 — PD worldwide. 'Night' (6 lines, winter — "Look for a lovely thing and you will find it"), 'Winter Noon' (8 lines, winter, couplets), 'Summer Evening' (8 lines, summer). Considered and skipped: 'February Twilight' (already in corpus since an earlier round — caught late), 'A June Day', 'Winter Solstice' (light-lost ending), all Crapsey cinquains (consistently death-adjacent — whole poet cut), 'June Night' (death ending), 'Summer Storm' / 'Winter Dusk' / 'Winter Stars' (over 11 lines). Seasons now W12 / Sp11 / Su11 / Au11 / yr51. |
| **2.37.0** | **Feature: Season moments — six quiet Noticed lines a year (Backlog item approved 2026-07-19).** Date-derived nature awareness, zero new data: meteorological season starts (Mar 1 / Jun 1 / Sep 1 / Dec 1 — Scandinavia convention; Can is the only user) + the two solstices pinned to the 21st (off by a day some years — acceptable for a quiet line). Hand-written lines in `_noticedLines()` block 0, pushed first so the 2-line cap can't drop them: four plain "First day of …" plus "Midsummer — the year's longest day." and "The year's shortest day. The light turns back tomorrow." Show-once via `noticed.seasonDate` (synced — fires once across devices). Wallpaper escape 1 by construction. Sekki (24-division) deepening → Parked. |
| **2.36.9** | **Fix: pinch-to-zoom locked on mobile — it was breaking the PWA layout.** Viewport meta now `maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no`. Accessibility tradeoff (iOS zoom gesture disabled) documented in Performance-audit Known Issues; re-enable toggle parked (surface TBD, not Connections). *(Mirror entry added late — was missing at v2.36.9 commit time.)* |
| **2.36.8** | **Poem splash timing: word-count-aware duration (floor 5s, ceiling 8s).** Previously line-count with a 9.5s cap — a long line and a short line counted the same. Switched to word count (~200ms/word). W3 verdict for poem coda: kept + iterated early (2026-07-21) — never became wallpaper, second-home use in About confirmed. |
|---|---|
| **2.64.35** | **Module extraction: `connections.js`.** Config panel, privacy gate, connections rendering, and task HTML extracted from inline script to `assets/connections.js` (~522 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startConnections()` initializer. Two state vars moved into closure: `const _CONNECTIONS_PRIVACY_SEEN_KEY` and `let _connectionsPrivacyVisible` (neither is wholesale-replaced by the Dropbox sync/merge layer). All other state (`manualTasks`, `doneIds`, `soonTasks`, `pastTasks`, `trelloTasks`, `$`) stays as inline globals. 15 exports: `setTrelloIcon`, `syncActiveButtons`, `_renderConnectionsPrivacy`, `_endConnectionsPrivacyVisit`, `toggleConfig`, `_applyOfflinePanel`, `renderConnections`, `dropboxDisconnect`, `_getDueStr`, `_queueTagArrivalShimmer`, `renderManual`, `_wireManualTagShimmer`, `taskHTML`, `_getCreatedFromId`, `_getAgeDays`. 4 private: `_connectionsHaveCredentials`, `_beginConnectionsPrivacyVisit`, `_playTagShimmer`, `_playTagInteractionShimmer`. Critical complications: `_getAgeDays` called as bare identifier in triage.js (must export as `window._getAgeDays`); `_beginConnectionsPrivacyVisit` is private (called by `toggleConfig` on panel open). Load order: `connections.js` script tag BEFORE `trello.js` (trello.js calls exported functions at runtime). Startup order: `window._startConnections()` FIRST in startup block because `init()` calls `renderManual()`, `setTrelloIcon(true)`, `_applyOfflinePanel()` directly without `window.` prefix. Smoke test updated: privacy-key occurrence check now spans both `index.html` and `assets/connections.js`. New `scripts/connections-test.mjs` (11 assertions: toggleConfig open/close, _applyOfflinePanel offline/online, renderConnections renders, _renderConnectionsPrivacy no-creds/with-creds via toggleConfig, _endConnectionsPrivacyVisit hides via toggleConfig-close, _getDueStr returns time/empty, renderManual renders 2 rows, module wiring). Both pre- and post-extraction runs pass. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.34** | **Module extraction: `about.js`.** Info panel, poem display, weekly digest, AI reflection, and stat rendering extracted from inline script to `assets/about.js` (~605 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startAbout()` initializer. No state moves into the closure — the section had zero module-level `let`/`const`/`var` declarations; all referenced globals (`manualTasks`, `trelloTasks`, `doneIds`, `habitsList`, `habitCompletions`, `APP_VERSION`, `$`, `appMemory`) are pre-existing inline globals read (never written) by this module. 6 exports: `toggleInfo`, `_poemOfTheDay`, `_onPoemTap`, `_shareDailyPoem`, `_copyToClipboard`, `renderInfoStats`. 6 private: `renderDailyPoem`, `_nudgeBlockShow`, `_nudgeTextResolve`, `_fetchWeekReflection`, `_fetchWeekThemeAI`, `_fetchMondayIntention`. `document.addEventListener('click', ...)` block (poem collapse handler, was module-level) moved inside `_startAbout()` body. `_versionBadgeBreathe` was already exported from memory-panel.js (no change needed). New `scripts/about-test.mjs` (11 assertions: toggleInfo opens panel, _poemOfTheDay returns poem, renderDailyPoem via toggleInfo, _onPoemTap reveal+collapse, _copyToClipboard callback, _shareDailyPoem clipboard path, renderInfoStats week grid, Noticed block, Sunday reflection block, Monday intention block, module wiring). Headless Chrome reports `(hover: hover)` → mocked `matchMedia` in test 4 to force touch/reveal path. Dispatch from `document.body` (not `document`) to avoid `e.target.closest` error in other listeners. Both pre- and post-extraction runs pass. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.33** | **Module extraction: `habits.js`.** Habit tracking, completions, strength calculation, and archive/undo flow extracted from inline script to `assets/habits.js` (~403 lines). Same no-closure-state pattern as zones.js: all four state vars (`habitsList`, `habitCompletions`, `habitEvents`, `habitEditMode`) stay as inline globals — wholesale-reassigned by Dropbox merge layer, read directly by drag.js, focus module, and merge helpers. 13 functions exported: `_saveHabits`, `_getHabitStrength`, `renderHabits`, `toggleHabits`, `toggleHabitEditMode`, `_enterHabitEditMode`, `handleHabitEditKey`, `toggleHabitInput`, `handleHabitKey`, `addHabit`, `_pulseCheck`, `toggleHabitDone`, `archiveHabit`. `_getHabitDates` is private (only called within `renderHabits`). Cross-boundary: `archiveHabit()` references `_archivedHabitStack` (declared in Undo block outside the habits section) and `_showUndoToast()` — both remain global and the module reads them by name. `_undoArchiveHabit()` (Undo block, outside module) calls back into exported functions. New `scripts/habits-test.mjs` written first (11 assertions: 3am rollover, renderHabits empty/with-habits, toggleHabitDone check/uncheck, addHabit, toggleHabitEditMode, archiveHabit+undo, _getHabitStrength, focus integration, module wiring). Both pre- and post-extraction runs pass. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.32** | **Module extraction: `zones.js`.** Soon/Past zone management extracted from inline script to `assets/zones.js` (~293 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startZones()` initializer, called before `window._startTriage()` and `init()`. Key difference from all prior extractions: **no state variables move into the module closure** — `soonTasks`, `pastTasks`, and `triageDismissedToday` remain as inline globals because they are wholesale-reassigned by the merge layer and mutated directly from `assets/triage.js`. The module is purely a function-extraction: 14 functions exported (`_saveSoon`, `_savePast`, `_saveManual`, `_saveDone`, `_ageSoon`, `_purgePast`, `toggleZone`, `renderSoon`, `renderPast`, `pullFromSoon`, `reviveFromPastShowReason`, `reviveFromPast`, `moveToSoon`, `moveToPast`). No setter shim needed (unlike `_setTriageBarSilent`). Section header comment removed; nudge system starting at `_parseAIText` stays inline (out of scope). New `scripts/zones-test.mjs` written first (11 assertions: save helpers, renderSoon/renderPast, pullFromSoon, moveToSoon, moveToPast, reviveFromPast, _ageSoon, _purgePast, module wiring). Both pre- and post-extraction runs pass. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.31** | **Module extraction: `triage.js`.** Evening triage flow extracted from inline script to `assets/triage.js` (~545 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startTriage()` initializer, called before `window._startMemoryPanel()` and `init()`. Eight private state vars moved into module closure (`triageDecisions`, `_triageSnapshot`, `_triageAutoCloseTimer`, `_triageAutoCloseStart`, `_triageAutoCloseRemaining`, `_triageActive`, `_triageBarSilent`, `_triageBarShown`); `triageDismissedToday` stays inline (mutated from 3+ outside sites). Complication: `_onWake` directly assigned `_triageBarSilent` — fixed by exporting `window._setTriageBarSilent = v => { _triageBarSilent = v; }` and updating the two assignments in `_onWake`. Private helpers `_undoneTasks`, `_saveTriageHistory`, `_getTriageHistory` remain unexported (only called within the module). 11 public exports + 1 setter. New `scripts/triage-test.mjs` written first (10 assertions covering bar visibility, expand/render, per-task decisions, apply full flow, undo, history, minimize/close, and module wiring). Both pre- and post-extraction runs pass. Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.30** | **Module extraction: `memory-panel.js`.** Memory panel and AI abstraction extracted from inline script to `assets/memory-panel.js` (~520 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startMemoryPanel()` initializer, called before `window._startMeeting()` and `init()`. Both private state vars (`_memoryClearPending`, `_memoryAbstracting`) moved into module closure. 11 exports: `toggleMemory`, `renderMemoryPanel`, `_memoryGoToConnections`, `_memoryClearRequest`, `_memoryClearCancel`, `_memoryClearConfirm`, `_memoryForget`, `_memoryConfirm`, `_memoryDismiss`, `_memoryAbstract`, `_versionBadgeBreathe` (the last called from `toggleInfo()` in the inline section). New `scripts/memory-panel-test.mjs` written first (baseline passes pre-extraction, all 9 assertions pass post-extraction). Precached in sw.js. |
| **2.64.29** | **Module extraction: `meeting.js`.** Meeting mode and voice note extracted from inline script to `assets/meeting.js` (~715 lines). Pattern: IIFE with `window._startMeeting()` initializer, called before `init()` so `_meetingInit`/`_voiceNoteInit` are available when connections panel runs. Two extra exports needed beyond the planned 14: `_getUserNames` (called by meeting-test.mjs) and `_meetingUpdateCount` (called from inline onclick handlers generated by `_meetingRenderReview`). Private state (`_mtg`, `_vn`, `_mtgPipWin`, etc.) moved fully into module closure. Precached in sw.js. All 14 meeting-test assertions pass. |
| **2.64.28** | **Fix: triage bar count visible; fix: done count correct after triage cleanup.** Two bugs fixed. (1) The "X didn't happen" label in the triage bar was invisible: `.triage-btn.primary` had `flex: 1` (inherited from the shared `.triage-btn` rule), absorbing all bar space and collapsing the `.triage-msg` span. Fixed by giving `.triage-btn.primary` `flex: 0 0 auto` and `.triage-msg` `flex: 1; min-width: 0`. (2) After triage, `updateStats()` showed 0 done: the post-triage cleanup (v2.64.0) moves done tasks to `pastTasks` before stats are read; `updateStats` only counted from `[...trelloTasks, ...manualTasks]`. Fixed by counting done from `[...all, ...pastTasks]` — safe because `doneIds` is session-scoped. Dead code removed: `open_triage` AI tool entry, `_fetchTriageHints()` function (~110 lines) and all its references (`.triage-task-hint` CSS, declaration, call site, render template). |
| **2.64.27** | **Fix + refactor: drag/reorder extracted to `assets/drag.js`, with mobile Trello persistence repaired.** Added `scripts/drag-test.mjs` first and ran its baseline mode green against the inline controllers across desktop and touch reordering for manual tasks, Trello cards, and habits. The test confirmed that touch Trello reorder updated memory/cache but omitted `today_trello_order`, `today_trello_order_at`, `last_local_change`, and Dropbox autosave; the touch path now matches desktop for those persistence and recency signals. Both delegated controllers moved into an inert, idempotent classic-script module exposed through `window._startDrag()` at the original boundary and precached by the service worker. The strict suite also covers controls/done/editing guards, cross-list rejection, long-press activation, early-movement cancellation, touch cancellation, haptics, ghost/classes cleanup, and module wiring. No DOM, CSS, storage schema, drag timing, desktop behavior, or network API changed. |
| **2.64.26** | **Refactor: browser platform behaviors extracted to `assets/platform.js`.** Moved service-worker registration/update handling, mobile `visualViewport` keyboard positioning, Android `beforeinstallprompt`/`appinstalled`, Safari/Firefox install promotion, and persisted `pageshow` wake handling out of `index.html`. The inert classic-script module exposes one idempotent `window._startPlatform()` initializer at the original end-of-script boundary; `installPWA`, `_pwaShowSteps`, and `_pwaCopyLink` remain explicit `window` functions for existing inline handlers. New `scripts/platform-test.mjs` was run green before and after extraction and covers install-event lifecycle, Safari fixed-height instructions, alternate-browser copy routes, standalone suppression, keyboard positioning/reset, `/sw.js` registration, and bfcache wake dispatch. No platform behavior, CSS, storage, or update policy changed. |
| **2.64.25** | **Refactor: splash controller extracted to `assets/splash.js`.** Moved the two-flag readiness gate, safety timers, 30-minute skip path, particle engine, date typewriter, poem coda, staged dismissal, font gate, and app reveal out of `index.html` into an inert classic-script module. The module exposes `window._startSplash()`, invoked immediately after `init()` at the original execution point; existing `_onAppLoadDone`, `_onSplashAnimDone`, and `_doSplashDismiss` hooks are preserved. `/assets/splash.js` is service-worker precached. `scripts/splash-test.mjs` now clears `splash_shown_at` before its animation passes so it genuinely exercises the no-coda and coda paths, adds an explicit cooldown-path test, and reads exit assertions from the extracted module. No visual, timing, storage, or startup behavior changed. |
| **2.64.24** | **Fix: splash logo letters fade out in consistent order.** `_doSplashDismiss()` previously assigned `style.transition` and `style.opacity` in the same synchronous update, which did not reliably create a transition boundary and could make individual TO/DAY letters snap away out of sequence. The shared `_fade()` helper now uses WAAPI `animate()` with `fill: 'forwards'`, preserving the staged TO → DAY timing without same-frame transition skips. |
| **2.64.23** | **Memory auto-sync** — `_mergeAppMemory()` extracted from the manual Restore Backup path and called from `mergeRemoteData()`. Every 7-second sync tick now reconciles `appMemory` across devices — patterns, AI inferences (`semantic`/`episodic`/`procedural`), moments, task keywords, and lifetime counters no longer require a manual restore to propagate. Previously only the per-tick `dropboxRestore(!fromSync)` path merged full appMemory; the periodic `mergeRemoteData()` path skipped it. |
| **2.64.22** | **Sync hardening (8 fixes)** — (1) `checked_ids` / `unchecked_ids` now use true LWW: `Math.max`-per-id across both maps, not blind union (stale check could outlive a newer uncheck). (2) `stat_streak` takes `Math.max` only when `remoteStreakDate >= localStreakDate`, preventing a ticker-reversed device from re-inflating yesterday's count after midnight reset. (3) `trello_config` fills cross-device if local is absent (Connections entered on one device now propagates). (4) AI `recentConversations` union-merged in `mergeRemoteData()`. (5) `_pruneTrelloMaps()` cleans `today_trello_firstseen` and `today_trello_lastactive` entries for cards absent >90 days. (6) `_pruneLS` added at 4 write sites for `reflection_<date>`, `nudge_done_count_<date>`, `noticed_lines_<date>`, and `week_theme_tried_<week>` keys. (7) `habit_events` tombstone cap (200 entries). (8) `sunday_nudge_seen_<date>` added to `_DISMISS_SYNC` registry so Sunday nudge dismissal propagates cross-device. |
| **2.64.21** | **Sync fix: AI inference + pattern field merge** — `mergeRemoteData()` now union-merges `appMemory.semantic`, `appMemory.episodic`, and `appMemory.procedural` inference arrays by `id`. Missing pattern fields (`triageUndos`, `soonPulls`, `letgoReasons`, `reviveReasons`, `lateAdditions`, `taskLifespanSamples`) added to the pattern merge. Previously only `recentConversations` was merged in the periodic sync path; pattern fields and inferences were left stale cross-device until a manual Restore Backup. |
| **2.64.20** | **Fix: tagged-task arrival and hover shimmers no longer compete.** A newly inserted tagged row could receive its viewport-triggered `task-tag-shimmer` and the desktop `_soon-shimmer` hover class at the same time. Because the hover rule appeared later and finished sooner, it replaced the two-pass arrival timing; both `animationend` handlers then removed their classes at the hover boundary, producing a brief colour flash and truncating the intended arrival. All tag shimmer triggers now pass through `_playTagShimmer()`, which holds an exclusive `data-tag-shimmer` state. `_queueTagArrivalShimmer()` reserves that state synchronously before `IntersectionObserver` runs, preventing a stationary pointer from stealing the arrival animation. Arrival and interaction classes also share one gradient rule, guaranteeing the same muted/accent paint while retaining their intentional two-pass versus one-pass timing. The smoke test reproduces an immediate `mouseenter`, verifies arrival priority and accent paint, waits for cleanup, then verifies the hover pass uses the identical gradient. |
| **2.64.12** | **Fix: shared poem links no longer crash at the Netlify edge.** The poem edge function tried to isolate the `POEMS` array with a start-anchored replacement, but `assets/poems.js` begins with header comments. Those comments survived, the generated function began with `return // TODAY…`, automatic semicolon insertion returned `undefined`, and the subsequent `POEMS.length` access threw outside the fallback block—every `/poem.html` request returned HTTP 500. Extraction now removes everything through the actual `const POEMS =` declaration, wraps the array expression, checks the asset response and validates `Array.isArray(parsed)` before selection. `poemForDate()` also rejects non-arrays defensively. Modified responses discard stale `content-length`/`content-encoding` headers. The smoke test now invokes the real edge handler against the real commented corpus, verifies populated OG metadata for `2026-08-12`, and confirms malformed corpus input falls back to the static page instead of throwing. |
| **2.64.11** | **Privacy: one-time reassurance in Connections.** On the first Connections-panel open, a fully disconnected installation shows: “Private by design: no account, no analytics. You own your data and choose every connection.” The line sits below the title as muted text without card chrome or animation, then disappears when the panel closes or an AI connection succeeds. `today_connections_privacy_seen=1` is written locally on that first open whether or not the line qualifies, so it never appears later as a reconnection prompt. The gate treats Trello credentials/config, Dropbox access/refresh/expired state, and either AI provider key as external connection history; PWA installation and meeting names do not count. The seen flag is deliberately absent from Dropbox backup and sync. |
| **2.64.10** | **Style: About actions use the Focus Copy CTA.** The `see more` and poem `share` controls now share a single `.copy-cta` visual component with task Focus → Copy: transparent one-pixel border at rest, `--radius-md`, identical mono type, letter spacing, padding, line height, and the same muted → bordered hover → accent confirmation states. Parent-hover still reveals both About actions contextually; touch reveal shows the border immediately because touch has no second hover state. The poem restores a permanent 64px right gutter so the newly padded CTA cannot overlap poem text. |
| **2.64.9** | **Fix: Focus Companion supplies an exact local clock value.** `_focusAskAI()` previously sent only a broad period such as `late night`, leaving the model no concrete time to use and producing vague questions like “this late.” Its context now includes the device-local formatted hour and minute plus the period (for example, `local time 23:14 (late night)`). The system prompt explicitly requires the supplied value whenever the question mentions time, while still allowing time to be omitted when another signal matters more. |
| **2.64.8** | **Privacy boundary: no in-app analytics or session replay.** Umami Cloud was evaluated and rejected for `index.html`: anonymous sessions still create third-party observation, default URL collection could capture Dropbox OAuth query codes or Trello hash tokens, and replay can expose rendered task text. Rule 32 records the boundary; Backlog and Performance audit record the product/security rationale. `scripts/design-lint.mjs` now fails on external runtime script tags and known analytics/replay markers in the app shell and its locally loaded scripts. Separate public landing-page analytics remains an allowed future lane; in-app diagnostics would require explicit opt-in and content/identifier-free aggregates. Also corrected v2.64.7's raw `#fff` instruction color to the existing `--text` token. |
| **2.64.7** | **PWA install instructions replace the CTA in place.** Clicking “How to add ↓” now swaps the button's own text to the Safari instruction, keeps the button's dimensions fixed, changes it to the primary text color, and applies the existing breathing animation. The row no longer relies on an invisible button plus a separate status-text replacement, so its height remains stable while the instruction is more legible. |
| **2.64.6** | **Fix: Safari install instructions no longer resize the Connections row.** `_pwaShowSteps()` still replaces the one-line status text in place, but now hides the “How to add ↓” button with `visibility: hidden` instead of `display: none`. The invisible button keeps its flex layout slot, so the status area cannot expand into the released width and the row height remains fixed on both desktop and mobile Safari. Also corrected the copy-link confirmation from “Copied!” to the voice-compliant “Copied”. |
| **2.64.5** | **Polish: PWA install promotion for non-Chrome browsers.** Browser detection block runs on load (IIFE): detects iOS Safari (`/iPad\|iPhone\|iPod/` UA + `!navigator.standalone`), macOS Safari (UA excludes chrome/android + Mac platform), Firefox (UA includes Firefox). When not in standalone mode, populates `#pwaInstallSection` (new empty div after `#statusMsg` in the Connections panel) with a `connection-row` showing "INSTALL APP" title and a browser-specific instruction: iOS → "Tap Share ↑ → Add to Home Screen"; macOS → "File → Add to Dock"; Firefox → redirect to Chrome/Edge/Safari. Chromium browsers (Chrome, Edge, Samsung, Opera) are unchanged — still handled by `beforeinstallprompt`. |
| **2.64.4** | **Polish: Soon list tag shimmer on interaction.** Added `._soon-shimmer` class — same `tagShimmer` keyframe but single forward sweep (400ms, no delay, 1 iteration). In `renderSoon()`, after rendering, detects pointer capability: hover devices wire `mouseenter` on each `.task` to shimmer its `.task-tag`; touch devices use an `IntersectionObserver` (threshold 0.8) that disconnects after the first in-view trigger. Guard: if `._soon-shimmer` is already active, the new enter is ignored. |
| **2.64.3** | **Polish: nudge content animates in on the About panel.** Added `@keyframes _nudgeBlockIn` (opacity 0→1, translateY 6→0, 300ms ease-out) and `@keyframes _nudgeTextIn` (300ms, 4px). `_nudgeBlockShow(el, delayMs, forceAnimate)` helper triggers entrance animation when a block transitions from hidden to visible; each block increments a stagger counter (60ms apart) so "This week", "Today", and "Noticed" cascade in. `_nudgeTextResolve(el)` fires on the `.week-summary` element when an async AI call resolves "reflecting…" → real text. Fresh "Noticed" lines (new vs prior from cache) marked `._nudge-fresh` and animated individually when the block is already visible. |
| **2.64.2** | **Polish: nudge differentiation — "Noticed" now uses behavioral data.** `_fetchWeekThemeAI()` completely replaces its data source: instead of reading `recentCompletedTasks` (same pool as "This week"), it computes behavioral metrics from `today_daily_history` and `appMemory.patterns` — peak completion hour, focus-day vs non-focus-day task averages, habit-kept vs partial-habit task averages, completion rate (done/added), late-addition percentage, day-of-week averages. Prompt explicitly forbids describing task content ("that's covered elsewhere"). Falls back to `null` if `< 2` data points exist. Morning nudge system prompt gains two guardrails: don't open with the streak count (shown in UI), don't echo task themes (that's "This week"'s job). |
| **2.64.1** | **Polish: hover-to-pause countdowns.** `#triageComplete` and `#undoToast` both gain `mouseenter`/`mouseleave` listeners (wired once via `_hoverWired` flag). On `mouseenter`: timer is cleared, remaining ms is computed (`_triageAutoCloseRemaining` / `_undoTimeoutRemaining`). On `mouseleave`: if the action (undo/dismiss) hasn't fired, a new timer starts with the remaining duration (min 600ms grace). Triage check: `if (!_triageSnapshot) return` — prevents restart after undo. Toast check: `if (!toast.classList.contains('show')) return`. The toast auto-expire path now also calls `_memoryOnTaskLetgo` (previously only `_hideUndoToast` did this — inconsistency fixed). |
| **2.64.0** | **Feat: post-triage cleanup.** After `triageApplyAll()` removes letgo/soon tasks from `manualTasks`, a new cleanup pass also moves all done tasks still in `manualTasks` (triage-marked done + pre-existing checked tasks) to `pastTasks` immediately (`zone='past'`, `status='done'`, `zoneChangedAt` stamped). This leaves the main list showing only tasks explicitly kept for tomorrow. Undo (`triageUndo()`) correctly restores everything via the pre-triage snapshot (which includes `pastTasks`). New-day cleanup is unaffected — it filters from `manualTasks`, where these tasks no longer exist. `_savePast()` guard updated to fire if `letgoCount > 0 || _postTriageDone.length > 0`. |
| **2.63.3** | **Style: tag shimmer update.** IntersectionObserver replaces `requestAnimationFrame` — shimmer waits until the tag is 80% in viewport before triggering. Animation changed to 2 alternate iterations (500ms each, 100ms delay): glint sweeps left→right then right→left, returning to neutral. No longer fires if the task lands off-screen. |
| **2.63.2** | **Style: poem share button — aligned to week "see more" component.** Removed `border`, `border-radius`, `padding`, `letter-spacing` from `.btn-poem-share`; `top/right` set to `0`; `.poem-block padding-right: 64px` removed. Hover/revealed states stripped of `border-color`. Transition aligned to `160ms ease`. Both CTAs now identical in token usage. |
| **2.63.1** | **Feat: "see more" CTA on This week section.** `#weekMoreBtn` button positioned `absolute` top-right of `#weekSection`. Desktop: fades in on `:hover` via CSS only. Mobile: `touchstart` on the section adds `.touched` class (shows button for 2.5s); `_moreWired` guard prevents double-attach on re-renders. Click calls `toggleMemory()` to open the memory panel. |
| **2.63.0** | **Feat: soonPulls wired into `_memoryForAI`.** `assets/insights.js`: when `patterns.soonPulls >= 3`, a plain-English line is added to the AI context — "You often pull tasks back from Soon — tends to overestimate future availability." Verified via a before/after probe (2026-08-09): `triageUndos` and `reviveReasons` did not land usefully in nudge context and are held for better surfaces (triage screen, week reflection). New `scripts/ai-test.mjs`: automated integration test for the AI tier — 5 scenarios (ping, nudge, focus companion, triage hints, week reflection) with format/word-count assertions. Exits 0 when no key is present. |
| **2.62.2** | **Style: undo toast — Undo button pinned to far right.** Added `margin-left: auto` to `#undoToast .undo-main button`. `.undo-main` is already `display: flex; width: 100%`, so the button now hugs the right edge with the task label anchored left. |
| **2.62.1** | **Data: memory recording for triage undo, Soon pull-back, Past revive, and silent delete.** `assets/insights.js`: added `patterns.triageUndos` (counter), `patterns.soonPulls` (counter), `patterns.reviveReasons` (reason map) to schema + migration guards. New functions: `_memoryOnTriageUndo()`, `_memoryOnSoonPull(taskText)` (also fires letgoReasons for the pulled task), `_memoryOnRevive(taskText, reason)`. Call sites in `index.html`: `triageUndo()` calls `_memoryOnTriageUndo`; `pullFromSoon(id)` calls `_memoryOnSoonPull(task.text)`; `reviveFromPast(id, reason)` calls `_memoryOnRevive(task.text, reason)`. `_hideUndoToast()` now calls `_memoryOnTaskLetgo(_pendingDeleteText, '')` before clearing `_pendingDeleteText` — captures the silent delete (user let toast expire without tapping Undo or a reason chip). |
| **2.62.0** | **Feat: triage completion — immediate task cleaning + Undo button.** `triageApplyAll` now captures a full pre-triage snapshot (`_triageSnapshot`: manualTasks, doneIds, soonTasks, pastTasks, checkedIds, uncheckedIds) before mutating state. Render calls (`renderManual/Trello/Soon/Past/updateStats`) moved to execute immediately after showing the completion screen — lists are clean behind the overlay rather than waiting for the 3s close. The Undo button (`#triageUndoBtn`, `.triage-undo-btn`) is shown on the completion screen; `triageUndo()` cancels the auto-close timer, restores all snapshotted state, resets `triageDismissedToday = false` and removes `triage_dismissed` from localStorage (so bar can reappear), resets `triageDecisions`, hides overlay, hides undo button, re-renders lists, triggers Dropbox backup. Auto-close still fires after 3s (stored in `_triageAutoCloseTimer`); if not cancelled by undo, sets `_triageSnapshot = null` (commit) then calls `triageClose()`. |
| **2.61.6** | **Fix: triage "Let go" now commits decision immediately (flow-completion bug).** Root cause: `triageShowReason(id)` only swapped DOM buttons with reason chips — it never set `triageDecisions[id]`. When any OTHER task was decided, `renderTriageList()` re-rendered from scratch and wiped the chips. The "Let go" intent was lost, `remaining` never reached 0, and `triageApplyAll()` never fired. Fix: `triageShowReason` now commits `triageDecisions[id] = 'letgo'` immediately (fires `_haptic`, recalculates remaining, calls `triageApplyAll()` or `renderTriageList()` normally). `renderTriageList` now injects optional reason chips inline on any decided letgo card that has no reason yet — chips call new `triageSetReason(id, reason)` which just records the reason and re-renders. Last task: "Let go" triggers completion immediately (reason = '', acceptable). Non-last tasks: reason chips persist in the decided card row between decisions. |
| **2.61.5** | **Fix: blank screen on wake / PWA background return during focus mode (BUG-071, third recurrence of BUG-004/056 family).** `_forceRepaint` now skips passes when `document.visibilityState === 'hidden'` (no point repainting while re-slept). Repaint schedule extended from 5000ms ceiling to `[500, 1500, 3000, 5000, 8000, 12000]ms`. New `_wakeFocusCheck()` runs alongside every repaint pass: calls `_focusReanchor()` to re-attach `.focused` element if sync re-rendered it away, then reads `getBoundingClientRect()` and corrects `body.top` drift if the focused task has scrolled out of the viewport (covers the body.position:fixed scroll-lock scenario active during focus mode). Both Mac sleep and PWA-backgrounded return go through the same `visibilitychange → _onWake` path. |
| **2.61.4** | **Fix: undo toast reason chip feedback (BUG-070 follow-up).** `_recordDeleteReason(reason, btn)` now accepts the clicked button as a second arg (onclick passes `this`). Selected chip gets accent border + color via `--accent`/`--accent-dim` tokens; other chips dim to `opacity: 0.3` and are disabled. Toast auto-dismisses via `setTimeout(_hideUndoToast, 800)` after chip tap — clears `_undoTimeout` first to prevent double-dismiss. Previously the row just silently dimmed to 35% with no per-chip highlight and no dismissal. |
| **2.61.3** | **Fix: undo toast reason chips unclickable (BUG-070).** Root cause: `#undoToast` used `flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap` — on narrow screens the four reason chips stayed in the same flex row as "Task removed [Undo]" and either extended off the right edge of the viewport (clipped, untappable) or wrapped awkwardly. Fix: changed toast to `flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start` with `max-width: min(420px, calc(100vw - 32px)); width: max-content`. `.undo-main` gets `width: 100%`. `#undoReasonRow.triage-reason-row` now uses `border-top` + `padding-top` instead of `border-left` + `margin-left` (separator is now horizontal, above the chip row). |
| **2.61.2** | **Extract shared poem logic to `assets/poem-utils.js`.** New file exports `_SOUTHERN_TZ`, `_poemForDate(dateStr?)`, `_escPoem(s)`, `_poemHTML(text)`. Loaded by both `index.html` (via `<script>` after util.js/poems.js) and `poem.html` (after poems.js). `index.html`: removed local `_SOUTHERN_TZ` constant, removed full `_poemOfTheDay()` implementation (now a one-liner wrapper `return _poemForDate()`), removed local `_poemHTML()`. `poem.html`: removed local `_SOUTHERN_TZ`, `poemForDate()`, `escHtml()`, and inline widow-guard; render block now calls `_poemHTML(poem.text)` / `_escPoem(poem.author)`. `sw.js`: `/assets/poem-utils.js` added to `CACHE_APP_SHELL`. Edge function copy (`netlify/edge-functions/poem.js`) intentionally unchanged — server-side Deno context cannot import static assets. |
| **2.61.1** | **index.html redundancy cleanup.** Added `_saveManual()` and `_saveDone()` one-liner helpers following the `_saveSoon()`/`_savePast()` pattern — replaced 20 + 8 = 28 verbatim `localStorage.setItem` call sites. Added `_copyToClipboard(text, onCopied)` utility (clipboard API + textarea execCommand fallback) and collapsed the two identical ~25-line clipboard blocks (in `_shareDailyPoem` and the task copy handler) to single calls. Also fixed a latent bug in `_shareDailyPoem`: the `else` (no clipboard API) branch was copying poem text instead of `poemUrl`. Deleted `@keyframes emptyFadeIn` (identical to `taskSlideIn`; updated `#manualEmpty.fading-in` to use `taskSlideIn`). Removed dead wrappers: `_memoryClearAll`, `saveMeetingName`, `_aiProactiveSuggestion` (and its orphaned `_aiProactiveDismissedThisSession` variable and call site in `toggleDone`). Net: ~−140 lines. |
| **2.61.0** | **Poem share image (canvas card).** `poem.html` — new `_shareAsImage()` function and "save as image" button in the page footer (hidden in `?embed=1` mode). On tap: waits for `document.fonts.ready`, draws a 1080×1350 canvas — `#0e0e10` background, `#c8f060` ✦ at top, "T O D A Y" brand label (very dim), poem text centred vertically in DM Mono 32px/300wt/1.9 line-height, author below in muted 22px, date + `today-here.netlify.app` at bottom (near-invisible). `_wrapText()` helper splits input at `\n` then wraps per line at `maxW = 856px`. On supported platforms (`navigator.canShare({ files })`) uses Web Share API to hand a `File` blob to the OS — native sheet offers "Save Image", AirDrop, Instagram, etc. Falls back to `<a download>` blob link on desktop. Button reveals via `document.getElementById('shareImgBtn').style.display = ''` after poem renders. No server, no OG interaction. |
| **2.60.0** | **Season moments: 6 → 24 sekki solar terms.** `assets/insights.js` — replaced the 6-entry `SEASON_MOMENTS` map (4 meteorological season starts + 2 solstices) with all 24 sekki divisions, one every ~15 days. Dates pinned to typical MM-DD (±1 day some years, same tolerance as the existing solstice lines). Each line is a short spare observation written for Scandinavia: `'08-07': 'The sun pulls back. Autumn is on its way.'`, `'08-23': 'Mornings have an edge to them now.'`, `'09-08': 'Dew on the grass. The year is cooling.'`, etc. The two solstice lines are preserved verbatim (`06-21` Midsummer, `12-21` shortest day). Show-once gating via `_noticedEligible('season:' + todayISO)` unchanged — each calendar date gets its own key. Next firing: Aug 23 (Chushu — end of heat). |
| **2.59.1** | **Poem page OG via Netlify Edge Function.** `netlify/edge-functions/poem.js` intercepts `GET /poem.html`, fetches `/assets/poems.js` from the same CDN origin, evaluates the array via `new Function('return ' + arrayText)()`, runs the same season-filter + `pool[days % pool.length]` selection (no southern-hemisphere TZ flip — server has no viewer TZ), then fetches the static page via `context.next()` and regex-replaces the four placeholder OG/Twitter tags with the real author + poem text. `netlify.toml`: `[[edge_functions]] path = "/poem.html" function = "poem"`. Crawlers now see actual poem text in og:description and author in og:title. |
| **2.59.0** | **Shareable poem permalink + `/poem.html`.** New standalone page `poem.html` at site root — takes `?date=YYYY-MM-DD`, replicates `_poemOfTheDay()` deterministic selection logic (seasonal filter + `pool[days % pool.length]` where `days = Math.floor(new Date(date.toDateString()).getTime() / 86400000)`), mirrors TODAY's dark aesthetic (DM Mono, `#0e0e10`, `#c8f060` accent). Southern hemisphere TZ flip applied identically via local `_SOUTHERN_TZ` list. OG meta (`og:title` = author, `og:description` = poem text, `og:image` = today-og.png) set by JS. "Open TODAY ↗" CTA + small ✦ TODAY brand mark. `?embed=1` param applies `.embed-mode` class (tighter padding) for iframe use. `_shareDailyPoem()` updated: `poemUrl = 'https://today-here.netlify.app/poem.html?date=' + _localISO()`, `navigator.share` now passes `{ title, text, url: poemUrl }`, clipboard fallback copies `poemUrl` (not raw text). SW: `poem.html` added to `CACHE_APP_SHELL`. |
| **2.57.0** | **Focus ✦ ask hidden until AI connected + conditional "Your name".** CSS: `body:not(.ai-connected) .focus-ai-timer-btn { display: none; }` hides the button entirely when no AI key is saved. `_aiRenderConfig`: after rendering provider rows, `document.body.classList.toggle('ai-connected', _aiIsConfigured())` gates button visibility reactively. `_focusAskAI`: reverted to plain `if (!_aiIsConfigured()) return;` guard — no two-tap hint state machine. "Your name" card: `document.getElementById('meetingNameWrap').style.display = bothConnected ? '' : 'none'` (shown only when both Gemini + Claude keys are present). HTML: `#meetingNameWrap` gets `style="display:none"` default to prevent flash on init. `bothConnected = hasGemini && hasClaude` (already computed in `_aiRenderConfig`). Will revisit when Granola is added. |
| **2.56.1** | **Splash coda: ethereal drift-away.** `STAGGER` raised from 100ms → 250ms; `CODA_DUR` raised from 200ms → 700ms; coda lines now use `cubic-bezier(0,0,0.2,1)` easing (fast start, very slow finish — each line "haunts" before fully vanishing). The logo letter fades (TO/DAY) and date row keep `ease` at 250/200ms. `overlayDelay` formula unchanged — uses the updated constants. Max exit time at 4 coda lines: ~1750ms; at 3 lines (haiku): ~1500ms. Worst-case total (18s safety + exit): ~19.9s. |
| **2.56.0** | **Splash exit animation: staged letter-group + coda fade.** `_doSplashDismiss` replaced. Exit sequence: (1) `.l:not(.a)` spans ("TO") fade 250ms; (2) 150ms later `.l.a` spans ("DAY") fade 250ms; (3) 50ms later `#splash-date-wrap` fades 200ms; (4) `sBurst` fires at t=300ms; (5) coda lines wrapped in `<span class="splash-coda-line">` via `innerHTML.split('<br>')` and faded sequentially with 100ms stagger, 200ms each; (6) `.poem-author` fades last; (7) `#splash` overlay fades 420ms after last coda line starts. `overlayDelay` is computed: `300 + (n-1)*100 + 200` for n coda lines, 420ms fallback when no coda. Total worst-case: 18s (safety ceiling) + ~1.5s exit = 19.5s — under the 20s requirement. New `scripts/splash-test.mjs`: 3 passes — no-coda timing (<10s), coda timing (<20s), logo DOM structure (2 TO + 3 DAY spans). No CSS changes — `.splash-coda-line` is a plain inline span, `opacity:0` fades in place. |
| **2.55.0** | **Delete reason + input auto-focus.** When a task is deleted via × button, the undo toast now shows 4 reason chips (`not relevant`, `no energy`, `lost interest`, `replaced`) below the "removed" line. Picking one calls `_memoryOnTaskLetgo(text, reason)` — same counter as triage letgo. If user taps Undo first, `_pendingDeleteText` is cleared and no reason is recorded. HTML: `#undoToast` restructured with `.undo-main` wrapper + `#undoReasonRow`. CSS: `#undoToast` changed to `flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start`. `_showUndoToast` populates reason row when `type === 'task'`; `_hideUndoToast` and `_undoDelete` clear it. New `_pendingDeleteText` var and `_recordDeleteReason(reason)` function. Auto-focus: `document.getElementById('newTask').focus()` fires on both the no-splash fast path and after `_doSplashDismiss` completes, guarded by `window.matchMedia('(hover: hover)')` (desktop only) and `!document.querySelector('.focusing')` (don't steal focus from focus mode). |
| **2.54.0** | **Triage letgo: skip-reason picker.** When the user taps "Let go" in triage, the action buttons are replaced inline by 4 reason chips — `not relevant`, `no energy`, `lost interest`, `replaced`. One tap picks the reason and commits the letgo decision. Reason stored as `reason` field in `today_triage_history` and incremented in `appMemory.patterns.letgoReasons` (new field, migration guard added). `_memoryOnTaskLetgo(taskText, reason)` updated to accept reason param. Trello letgo branch also gets reason tracking. PROCEDURAL memory panel insight fires once ≥8 total letgos exist and a dominant reason accounts for ≥35%: "most let-go decisions: no energy (58%)". CSS: `.triage-reason-row` (flex, fadeIn) and `.triage-reason-btn` (mono, pill, muted, hover brightens) using existing tokens — `--font-mono`, `--text-xs`, `--space-1/2/3`, `--radius-full`, `--border`, `--surface2`. `triageShowReason(id)` replaces the actions innerHTML in-place; falls back to direct letgo if DOM not found. |
| **2.53.0** | **Focus companion: memory-aware questions.** `_focusAskAI` now adds three new signals to `_ctx`: time of day (morning/afternoon/evening), peak-hour match (`appMemory.preferences.peakHour` ± 1h → "their peak productive hour"), and today's session count (from `stat_focus_mins_today ÷ 25`). Also builds `_inferCtx` from up to 4 confirmed behavioral inferences across all memory types and appends it to the system prompt as "What we know about this person: ...". System prompt updated to instruct the AI to use peak-hour and multi-session-day context, and to ask something that fits who the person is, not just what the task says. |
| **2.52.1** | **BUG-068 recurrence fix.** `renderTrello()` patch path (trello.js) still read `_getTrelloFocus()` (daily map, wiped every morning) for the 🍅 badge — a Rule 27 miss when v2.48.4 moved the source of truth to `_getTrelloFocusTotal()`. The patch runs every 7s and overwrote the correct badge from `taskHTML()` with the now-empty daily count each morning. Also fixed a secondary stale read in the focus companion context (index.html) using the same wrong map. Both switched to `_getTrelloFocusTotal()`. |
| **2.36.7** | **Poem: Publilius Syrus, maxims 499–500 (trans. Darius Lyman, 1856).** "It is an unhappy lot which finds no enemies. / It is an unhappy lot which an enemy does not envy." Year-round. Sequential pair — parallel structure, escalating tightness. Corpus 92 → 93. |
| **2.36.6** | **Fix: Morning nudge echoed the top task instead of noticing something.** Root: "position 1 is where the user put their priority" in the prompt gave the AI an easy out — when no signal stood out, it named task 1 verbatim and called it done. Fix: reframed the instruction as "say something the user could not see just by looking," added an explicit block against echoing the top item for no reason, and strengthened the quiet-morning escape (a count, the day, a simple note) for when nothing stands out. |
| **2.36.5** | **Fix: BUG-059 — task card age reset by sync after focus.** Cards that brightened after a focus session went back to aged on refresh. Root: `mergeRemoteData` built the task data map with a blind remote-wins spread — `lastActive` was overwritten by the stale Dropbox value (the backup hadn't landed yet). Fix: after the spread, keep whichever `lastActive` is higher (max wins, same principle as focus-minute merging). Local focus session is never clobbered by a not-yet-uploaded remote copy. |
| **2.36.4** | **Fix: Monday intention drew from completed tasks.** `_fetchMondayIntention` included `recentCompletedTasks` (last 15, 30-day window) as "Last week's work" context — but the AI used it as intention material, surfacing finished work as if it still needed attention. Completed tasks are Sunday's domain (reflection); Monday should only look forward. Removed `recentCompletedTasks` from the prompt entirely; context is now pending tasks only (top 5), or "Fresh week, nothing waiting yet." |
| **2.36.3** | **Fix: Noticed block not synced between devices.** `appMemory.noticed` (show-once bookkeeping) and `appMemory.recentCompletedTasks` (theme-of-week input) were both excluded from `mergeRemoteData`'s selective memory merge — the comment at `_noticedLines` explicitly called it "device-local, acceptable." Not acceptable once users see divergent Noticed content. Two additions to the merge block: (1) `recentCompletedTasks` union by `text+date`, 30-day cutoff, sort ascending — so theme-of-week reads the same completed-task history on both devices; (2) `noticed` shallow spread with remote winning — so show-once gates are shared and the same observation doesn't fire twice, once per device. Updated the stale comment in insights.js. |
| **2.36.2** | **Fix: tag prefixes polluted keyword mining — «"today:" keeps coming up this week».** Can's device report: the Noticed theme-of-week surfaced his tag, not a theme. All three keyword miners in insights.js split on whitespace only, so a `tag:` prefix (the `^[a-z0-9]{1,12}:\s+` pattern taskHTML renders as a chip) survived as a countable "word" — structurally guaranteed noise for consistent taggers. New `_stripTag()` helper applied at all three sites: `_memoryOnTaskComplete` keyword tracking, `_memoryForAI` themes (AI prompt), `_noticedLines` theme-of-week. No bookkeeping migration needed: the burned `themeWord: "today:"` can't recur (stripped at source), and the once-per-week gate stands for this week. |
| **2.36.1** | **Fix: BUG-057 — About's weekly block (Sunday reflection / Monday intention) differed between devices.** `week_reflection_<date>` / `monday_intention_<date>` were device-local caches — each device generated its own AI text. Fix mirrors the `day_nudge_ai` pattern (v2.27.0b): both keys in the Dropbox backup payload, remote-always-wins merge in `mergeRemoteData` (guarded against no-op rewrites), About re-renders live if open when the merge lands. First device to push owns the day's text. |
| **2.36.0** | **Feature: AI can open triage on request — `open_triage` action.** Can's finding: typing a triage request (bar + ✦, or the panel's NL input) hit a wall — the action vocabulary had no way to open the sorting flow, so the AI answered with words and no door. New vocabulary entry `open_triage {}` with intent-family guidance (not keyword-bound: "triage", "review my tasks", "move things to soon", "I'll do these later" all qualify; ONE named task → `move_soon` instead) and the `move_soon`-style guard: only on explicit ask, never AI-suggested. `_aiExecute` case: empty undone → calm message ("Nothing waiting — the list is already clear."), else `closeAI()` + `triageExpand()`. Deliberately no hour gate: the 8pm window governs when the app *invites* triage (bar), not when it obliges a request — capability vs. invitation. |
| **2.35.5** | **Polish: poem typography — orphan-word guard on all four poem surfaces.** Can's finding: authored poem lines that wrap on narrow screens strand a single word on its own line. New `_poemHTML(text)` helper (beside `_poemOfTheDay`) replaces the shared `split('
').map(esc).join('<br>')` pattern at all four render sites (About daily poem, ✦ brief, clean-slate echo, splash coda): ties the last two words of each authored line with ` ` so a wrap can never orphan the final word — deterministic, every engine; author line breaks untouched. Plus `text-wrap: pretty` on `.splash-poem-text/.panel-haiku/.brief-poem/.empty-poem` (progressive enhancement, Safari 26+/Chrome 117+) for overall wrap quality. |

Three plumbing edits + one guideline: `pendingWithAge` carries `revived`; `ctx.tasks.pending` map keeps it; `pendingList`/`agingList` annotate "(brought back from past)"; new message guideline names it the strongest importance signal but with the no-chase guard — "the choice was theirs, already made — acknowledge at most once, never 'you brought this back, so…'. Observed, not assigned" (same voice pattern as v2.35.1). Parked-table item closed. |
