Three prompt changes in the proactive system prompt: peak-time line adds "name the hour"; pre-peak line adds pointing a demanding task at the upcoming peak ("the report might sit better around 2pm"); message guideline now requires tying the suggestion to the rhythm explicitly, hour included, with a voice guard — "their rhythm is observed, not assigned: 'usually', never 'should'" (Philosophy: no urgency, no coaching). No code-path changes. |
| **2.35.0** | **Feature: Roadmap #7 second brick — "Noticed" block in About (Personalization.md G3, un-gated by the #1 verdict).** Surfaces what TODAY has learned — **delta-gated, never fact-stated**: each line appears once when something *changes*, then never again (Wallpaper escapes 1+2; empty → block hidden, escape 3). Four line types in `_noticedLines()` (insights.js — it owns `appMemory`), max 2 shown: (1) habit streak milestone crossings 7/14/30/50/100 → "Meditation — 30 days now." (once per habit per milestone, `noticed.habitMilestones`); (2) best-streak proximity (within 2 of best, ≥5) → "Day 12. Your best is 14." (once/day); (3) peak hour first established (≥30 completions signal gate) or moved → "Most things get done around 2pm." (`noticed.peakShown`); (4) theme of the week (word ≥3× in 7-day completions) → "'invoice' keeps coming up this week." (once/week). Show-once bookkeeping in `appMemory.noticed` (device-local — merge path doesn't sync it, acceptable). Render: `#noticedBlock` in `renderInfoStats`, `#todayNudgeBlock`'s quiet shell; a dated day-cache (`noticed_lines_<date>`, `_pruneLS`-cleaned) keeps lines visible the rest of the day so they don't vanish on second open. Scope: weather awareness excluded — needs geolocation + external API = a Connections data-boundary decision, parked. W3 due 2026-08-02. |
| **2.34.0** | **Feature: Roadmap #5 — first-run experience (two reductive changes).** UX finding: a brand-new user's screen led with "From Trello — not connected yet" (third-party brand before their first task) and showed the day's poem twice in ninety seconds (splash coda + clean-slate echo) — while the philosophy's one core action (add) had no pointer. The gap: the app couldn't tell "clean slate, day done" from "blank slate, day one." (1) `renderTrello`: empty section now hides whether connected or not — Trello discovery lives in ✧ Connections; `loadTrello` re-reveals the section for the "Getting your cards…" state since it's no longer pre-visible. (2) `updateManualEmptyState`: when `appMemory.totalTasksCompleted` is 0 and the list is empty, the poem echo is replaced by a one-time pointer line — "What matters today? Start below." Gone forever after the first completion; Dropbox restore brings the counter, so returning users get the echo once sync lands. Rejected: tours, coach marks, sample tasks (anti-philosophy), progressive surfacing (over-engineered). Pattern: point-of-need discovery over upfront onboarding (v2.31.0 name-capture precedent). |
| **2.33.10** | **Refactor: Roadmap #3 — `insights.js` extracted (sixth module, ~415 lines).** Moved to `assets/insights.js`: `AI_NAMES`, `_pickAIName`, the `appMemory` declaration + init IIFE + schema migration, `_saveMemory`, `_memoryOnTaskComplete`, `_memoryOnFocusComplete`, `_memoryOnStreakUpdate`, `_memoryOnDaySummary`, `_memoryForAI`, `_getProactiveObservations`, `_pickObservationToMention`. First extraction where the module OWNS its state — `appMemory` moves with it (main script only mutates properties, never reassigns; classic scripts share the global lexical environment so the `let` binding is visible unchanged). Also the first that runs at eval (init IIFE + migration) — eval-time deps are only `safeJSON`/`_localISO` from util.js, which loads first. Stayed inline: `applyNewDayCleanup` (day-boundary cluster; calls `_memoryOnStreakUpdate`/`_memoryOnDaySummary` at call time), the `appMemory` merge block in `mergeRemoteData` (sync cluster), suggestion-cooldown pruning. `sw.js` `CACHE_APP_SHELL` gains `/assets/insights.js`. Next: `sync.js` (~510, Non-Delegation — the ceiling candidate). |
| **2.33.9** | **Polish: fade gradient below section headers.** `.section-header` had a hard bottom edge (solid `--bg`) — tasks clipped abruptly under the pinned label, while the logo header softens content with its 10px `::after` gradient. Added the identical `::after` to `.section-header` (10px, `--bg` → transparent, `bottom: -10px`, `pointer-events: none`). The existing `padding-top: var(--space-4)` on `.section-header` already clears the logo header's gradient; task rows sit >10px below the label so the new gradient only shows during scroll-under. BUG-050 verified on device (snappiness good) — this was the remaining visual gap Can flagged. |
| **2.33.8** | **Fix: BUG-050 final pass — section headers glide with the logo header during departure, no snap.** Previous pass used two constants (`h` while pinned, `0` at departure threshold) — the single crossing write was hidden behind the departing header's backdrop, but fast scrolling made the jump visible. Root cause: the departure zone (~h pixels of scroll travel where the header bottom goes from h to 0) wasn't tracked at all. Fix: replace the threshold conditional with `Math.min(h, Math.max(0, document.body.offsetHeight - window.scrollY))` — pure arithmetic from scroll position. During departure (`scrollY` between `bodyH−h` and `bodyH`), the formula evaluates exactly to `bodyH − scrollY` (the logo header's visible bottom by geometry); outside that window it saturates to `h` (pinned) or `0` (gone). No `getBoundingClientRect()` reads (iOS compositor lag) — entirely derived from scroll position. |
| **2.33.7** | **Poem corpus: round 20 — two additions (93 → 95, target reached).** Ransetsu 'New Year's day' (2 lines, winter, Chamberlain 1902 no. 103 — Bashō-endorsed); Seibu 'daylight dawns' (2 lines, year-round, Chamberlain 1902 no. 64 — poet's death song, reads as dawn poem). Rejected: Moritake butterfly, Hokushi 'burnt out' (deferred). Source: archive.org/details/basho-and-the-japanses-poetical-epigram. |
| **2.33.6** | **Poem corpus: round 19 — three additions (90 → 93). All source citations verified in browser.** Dickinson 'To Make a Prairie' (5 lines, year-round, Wikisource); Teasdale 'February Twilight' (8 lines, winter, archive.org/details/darkofmoon0000sara); Epictetus Encheiridion §8 trans. George Long (3 lines, year-round, Gutenberg #10661 — was wrongly cited as #45898). Stewart/Seneca two entries also verified (archive.org/details/seneca-dialogues-aubrey-stewart); 'says he,' noted as editorial omission in entry 2. |
| **2.33.5** | **Refactor: Roadmap #3 — `trello.js` extracted (fifth module, 487 lines).** Moved to `assets/trello.js`: `TRELLO_API_KEY`, `_getTrelloBoardName`, `getSavedConfig`, `trelloAuth`, `loadTrelloBoards`, `onBoardChange`, `loadTrelloLists`, `saveAndLoad`, `clearTrello`, `loadTrello`, `renderTrello`. Stayed inline (with reasons in the module header): `trelloTasks`/`trelloConnected` declarations (main script owns state), `syncTrello` (nested in the 7s polling closure), `setTrelloIcon` (panel-UI cluster), `_getDueStr` (shared with `taskHTML`), focus/first-seen helpers (aging cluster), `dropboxDisconnect` (was mid-block, not Trello). Same classic-script pattern as the four prior extractions — all cross-references resolve at call time, nothing runs at eval. `scripts/design-lint.mjs` Rule 27 parity check updated to read `renderTrello` from `trello.js` (was silently index-only). `sw.js` `CACHE_APP_SHELL` gains `/assets/trello.js`. Next risk-ascending: `insights.js` (~384), `sync.js` (~510, Non-Delegation). |
| **2.33.4** | **Polish: add-task input hover.** `.add-task-input:hover { border-color: var(--muted) }` — joins the neutral-control hover convention (`.btn-icon`, `.add-mic-btn`, `.btn-ghost` all lift border/color to `--muted` on hover). Placed before the `:focus` rule so focus keeps the accent border (equal specificity, cascade order). Existing `border-color` transition (`--dur-fast`) animates it. Bare `:hover` (no media guard), consistent with siblings. |
| **2.33.3** | **Fix: BUG-050 sixth pass — `.app`'s `overflow-x: clip` removed.** Fifth pass improved but slight jitter persisted. Can's observation cracked it: logo header (sticky+blur, body child) and input bar (fixed) scroll perfectly smooth on iOS — only section headers (inside `.app`) jitter, desktop clean. Since all have constant positions, the difference is ancestry: on iOS WebKit, ANY overflow clipping on an ancestor (even `clip`, which avoids creating a scroll container) demotes sticky descendants to main-thread positioning — one frame behind the compositor. `.app` clip removed; root `html { overflow-x: hidden }` keeps horizontal protection (root-level overflow doesn't demote — the smooth logo header proves it). Rule 1 rewritten: no overflow of any value on `.app` or any sticky ancestor. Blur kept everywhere (exonerated by the smooth blurred logo header; Can wants the glass). Fallback if jitter persists: fixed label bar + IntersectionObserver (Can's idea — loses push-off animation, zero per-frame work). Watch regression: content painting outside the app column (drag-reorder, celebrations) — the old clip's job. |
| **2.33.2** | **Fix: iOS focus-zoom on input tap.** Tapping the add-task bar zoomed the whole page on iPhone — iOS auto-zooms when a focused input's font-size is under 16px, and `.add-task-input` was 14px (`--text-md`); `#meetingNamePromptInput` was even 11px. Viewport meta can't prevent it without `maximum-scale=1` (a11y cost — rejected). Fix: ≤600px media block forces `font-size: var(--text-lg)` (16px) on all seven focusable text inputs (`.add-task-input`, `.habit-input`, `.ai-nl-input`, `.ai-key-input`, `#meetingNamePromptInput`, `#meetingNameInput`, `.config-input`). New Rule 5c: every focusable input ≥16px on mobile; add new inputs to the list. |
| **2.33.1** | **Fix: BUG-050 fifth pass — scroll-driven animation removed, status-bar scrim added.** Fourth pass failed verify: animating `top` (a layout property) can't run on WebKit's compositor — it sampled main-thread, one frame behind, the same lag it claimed to eliminate; `fill: both` also kept `top` under animation control through the whole scroll. Deleted (`@supports` block, `sec-hdr-depart` keyframes, `--hdr-h`). The constant-snap is now universal: `--sec-sticky-top` = header height while pinned, 0 at departure (max() clamps to safe area), one write at the crossing hidden behind the departing header's backdrop. Second symptom (new): content visible in the safe-area strip above section headers after the main header departs — the v2.31.6 clamp pins them *below* the status bar and nothing covered the 0→inset strip. New `#statusBarScrim`: fixed, `height: env(safe-area-inset-top)`, `--bg`, `z-index: 5` (above content 1, below glass header 10 and section headers 9), `pointer-events: none`. Desktop: zero height, no-op. Also: BUG-056 verified (2026-07-18) → archived. |
| **2.33.0** | **Feature: Roadmap #7 first brick — the day's nudge line persists in About until midnight.** The morning strip was dismiss-once-and-gone; the #1 verdict (Can reads and reflects on the line every morning) cleared the wallpaper concern for a second home. Three changes: (1) `checkDayNudge()`'s noon branch no longer deletes `day_nudge_ai_<date>` — the dated key self-expires at day change (`_pruneLS` on next write clears stragglers). (2) New `#todayNudgeBlock` in About above the stat tiles — `#sundayBlock`'s shell but quieter (plain `--border`, no tint, muted "Today" label); rendered in `renderInfoStats()` from the cached line, hidden when absent. (3) `mergeRemoteData`'s nudge branch re-renders About live when the panel is open (same pattern as the v2.32.1 focus-tile fix). Side effect: the ✦ daily brief now shows nudge+poem all day (cache no longer empty after noon) — the chips fallback only fires when generation genuinely failed, which shrinks the not-useful-chips surface (see Parked discovery). Dia's return-to-the-brief insight, on surfaces we already owned. |
| **2.32.3** | **Polish: morning nudge quotes task text verbatim (Roadmap #1 verdict iteration).** Can's verdict (2026-07-18): reads the nudge every time (W1 passes), never wrong, but task references were sometimes hard to map back to the list. Root cause: the system prompt's blanket "No quotes" rule — meant to stop the model wrapping its reply in quotation marks — also banned quoting task text, forcing paraphrase. Fix in `_fetchDayNudgeAI`: instruction now requires using a task's exact words ("a short verbatim fragment is fine … never paraphrase or describe a task in different words"); system prompt narrowed to "Never wrap your reply in quotation marks; quoting a task's own words inline is good." Verdict recorded in Backlog #1; calendar day-shape and action-chip paths both untriggered. |
| **2.32.2** | **Fix: safe-area bottom for input bar CTAs, triage overlay, and meeting overlay.** Three related omissions: (1) `.add-task-row` on mobile had `bottom: 0; padding-bottom: calc(var(--space-3) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0))` — but `align-items: center` centers over the full bar height (12px top + 44px content + 46px bottom = 102px), placing buttons at 29–73px from screen bottom; home indicator covers 0–34px, so button bottoms clipped 5px into the indicator. Fix: `bottom: env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)` (bar floats above the indicator); `padding` now symmetric 12px top and bottom; `::after` fill covers the gap below. (2) `#triageOverlay` and (3) `#meetingOverlay` both use `position: fixed; inset: 0; align-items: flex-end; padding: var(--space-4)` — no bottom safe-area compensation, so triage actions and meeting Add tasks/Discard were 16px from screen bottom (behind the home indicator). Fix: `padding-bottom: calc(var(--space-4) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))` on both. Desktop: `env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)` = 0 — no change. |
| **2.32.1** | **Fix: BUG-050 fourth pass — residual section-header jitter on iOS.** JS scroll listeners run on the main thread ≥1 frame behind iOS compositor-thread scrolling, so the v2.27.3 analytic `--sec-sticky-top` updates were always describing where the scroll *was* — headers chased the finger through the sticky-header's departure window. Fix: CSS scroll-driven animation (`@supports (animation-timeline: scroll())`) — `.section-header` animates `top` from `max(var(--hdr-h), safe-area)` to the safe-area inset over `animation-range: calc(100dvh − var(--hdr-h)) → 100dvh` (the exact departure window; header is sticky in body, body = viewport height). Sampled from the scroll offset in the same rendering update that paints the scroll — no lag by construction. `--hdr-h` published by the existing ResizeObserver (resize-only). Per-scroll JS listener not attached on supporting browsers (iOS 26+/Chrome 115+). Older-browser fallback replaced as well: `--sec-sticky-top` snaps between two constants (header height ↔ 0) at the departure threshold instead of continuously tracking — constants can't jitter, and the crossing write is hidden behind the departing header's opaque backdrop. **Also: BUG-041 third pass** — splash logo slid down mid-fade on iOS cold start (late viewport settle re-centered `#splash-inner`'s `margin: auto`); `startSplash` now freezes the column's resolved position as an explicit margin before reveal. | Not a WAAPI-rule case: scroll-derived progress re-samples (doesn't restart) across `_forceRepaint` display toggles. Also this session: About focus tile re-renders live when sync merges a higher `stat_focus_mins_today` (was stale until panel reopen); 26 lines of dead CSS removed (`.brief-week`, `.brief-week-label`, `.readme-actions`, `.btn-icon-lg`, `.panel-header`, `.panel-status-line`); daily brief falls through to proactive AI when the nudge cache is empty (poem-only brief was the empty-state echo in a different container). |
| **2.32.0** | **Feature: meeting review auto-selects your tasks.** `item.mine` was already computed by Gemini (based on `userName` in the extraction prompt) but never used in the UI — v2.25.4 removed pre-selection when names weren't reliably set. Now that names are captured at first meeting tap (v2.31.0), attribution is reliable. One-line change: `meeting-item${item.mine ? ' selected' : ''}` in `_meetingRenderReview()`. All items remain tappable; user can deselect any auto-selected item or select an unselected one before adding. |
| **2.31.9** | **Fix: BUG-004 recurrence — blank app after long Mac sleep.** Repaint passes on wake topped out at 1500ms, but Mac GPU re-init after hours of sleep can take 3–5s. The user's click restored the UI because pointer events get higher paint priority — the deferred passes just missed the window. Added two more `_forceRepaint` calls at 3000ms and 5000ms in `_onWake()`. No other code implicated: `_clearStaleFocusing` and `fill._pulseAnim` toggle are both correct (no active focus session means neither fires). |
| **2.31.8** | **Polish: daily brief drops the shape line.** `_showDailyBrief()` was showing `X done · Yh Zm focused` between the nudge and poem. Both stats are visible elsewhere (done count in the list, focus time in About) — the shape line was the only rule-based, always-redundant piece in a brief otherwise made of unique-per-day content. Removed `doneCount`/`focusMins` reads, `shapeParts` logic, and `.brief-shape` CSS rule. Brief is now: nudge + poem. Fallback to proactive AI (when both are empty) unchanged. |
| **2.31.7** | **Polish: splash poem contrast swap.** `.splash-poem-text` was `color: var(--muted)` (low contrast on the dark splash background) while `.poem-author` had no explicit color, inheriting near-full `--text` brightness with `opacity: 0.7`. Swapped: poem text → `color: var(--text)`; author → `color: var(--muted)` explicit + `opacity` removed (same token-clean approach as v2.31.4's `.brief-poem-author` fix). `.poem-author` is shared with the About daily poem and empty-state echo — correct in both: author is always secondary. |
| **2.31.6** | **Fix: BUG-050 — section headers slip behind iOS safe area on mobile.** `--sec-sticky-top` reaches 0 when the main header scrolls away, but iOS PWA draws content under the status bar (~47–59px of `env(safe-area-inset-top)`). Fix: `.section-header { top: max(var(--sec-sticky-top, 0px), env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)); }` — `max()` provides the safe area as a floor. Desktop: `env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px)` evaluates to 0, no effect. |
| **2.31.5** | **Polish: brief contrast pass.** `.brief-shape` (`--text-sm2`, `--muted`) and `.brief-poem-author` (`opacity: 0.6`, `--text-micro`) were too faint. `.brief-poem-author`: removed `opacity: 0.6`, color → `var(--muted)` explicit, size `--text-micro` → `--text-xs`. `.brief-shape`: size `--text-sm` → `--text-sm2`. `.brief-poem`: color `var(--muted)` → `var(--text)` (prose, meant to be read). |
| **2.31.4** | **Polish: brief poem author contrast fix.** `.brief-poem-author` had `opacity: 0.6` compounding on top of `var(--muted)` (~3.4:1 contrast), making the author line near-invisible. Removed the extra opacity layer; color kept as `var(--muted)`. |
| **2.31.3** | **Fix: two mic buttons on mobile.** v2.28.0 made `_meetingSupported()` capability-only (dropped the `!('ontouchstart' in window)` guard), so mobile with a Gemini key showed both the voice note button and the meeting button. Fix in `_voiceNoteInit()`: if `_meetingSupported() && _aiGetKey('gemini')` is true, voice note button is hidden. Voice note falls back to showing on mobile only when meeting is unavailable (no key, or MIME unsupported). |
| **2.31.2** | **Polish: name prompt joins the pill family.** v2.31.0's `#meetingNamePrompt` was a one-off shell (`radius-lg`, `--border` border, bespoke lowercase micro-label, "start →" with an arrow no other button uses). Now a sibling of `#meetingPill`: `radius-full`, `--accent-dim` border, `--text-sm` container size. Label span removed — the placeholder carries the ask ("Your first name — to spot your tasks"). Start button restyled to the `.meeting-pill-stop` voice: mono `--text-sm`, `0.06em` tracking, `color` transition (hover → `--accent-check-hover`), plain word "start". Input drops to `--text-sm` to match the pill scale. |
| **2.31.1** | **Polish: name card hint — voice regression + wrap.** v2.31.0's hint rewrite ("edit or add names for meeting attribution") reintroduced "attribution", the exact technical term v2.25.8 removed, and broke the panel's sentence-case hint convention. Reverted to "Spots your tasks in meetings" — the card's edit role is self-evident from chips + input; the hint says what names are *for*. Also added `line-height: 1.5` to `.name-card-hint`, matching `.config-hint` / `.connection-card-hint` (matters when the header stacks at ≤480px and the hint wraps). |
| **2.31.0** | **Feature: inline name capture on first meeting mic tap.** `toggleMeeting()` now checks `_getUserNames().length === 0` before calling `_meetingStart()` — if empty, shows `#meetingNamePrompt` instead. Prompt is a fixed element (`bottom: 76px`, same stacking as meeting pill) with a muted label "your name", a plain text input, and an accent "start →" button. Enter/button: saves name (via `_saveUserNames`, syncs to Dropbox, updates Connections chip list) then calls `_meetingStart()`. Empty submit: starts meeting without a name (user chose to proceed). Escape: dismisses prompt without starting. Show/hide uses the same `.show`/`.visible` class pattern as the meeting pill (opacity transition). Mobile override: `bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px) + 80px)` applied alongside pills. Connections name card hint updated: "edit or add names for meeting attribution" (the card is now an edit surface, not discovery). |
| **2.30.1** | **Fix: BUG-055 — done tasks from today wiped on second-device first-open.** `applyNewDayCleanup()` moves done tasks to PAST — correct when tasks were done yesterday, wrong when a second device opens for the first time today after work was done on another device. Root cause: the cleanup guard only checks `stat_last_visit` (device-local), so a phone that last opened yesterday correctly fires cleanup — but the Dropbox restore had already merged today's done tasks into `doneIds`, and cleanup moved those to PAST too. Fix: before graduating done tasks to PAST, build `_checkedTodayIds` from `_getCheckedIds()` filtered to `_localISO(c.at) === today`. Tasks with a today check timestamp stay in `manualTasks` and keep their `doneIds` entry; only tasks checked on prior days graduate to PAST. The `today_checked_ids` set is synced via Dropbox (since v2.18.21), so the second device has the timestamps it needs after restore. |
| **2.30.0** | **Feature: Sunday/Monday content moves to About; ✦ becomes purely daily.** `_sundayBlock` in `renderInfoStats()` now renders on both Sunday (`_isSun`) and Monday (`_isMon = new Date().getDay() === 1`). Sunday: existing week reflection (label "This week", cache `week_reflection_<today>`, `_fetchWeekReflection` with rule-based fallback). Monday: new intention block (label "New week", cache `monday_intention_<today>`, `_fetchMondayIntention()`, no fallback — hides on AI failure). `checkSundayNudge()` extended to Monday (`_day !== 0 && _day !== 1` guard); reuses `btn-icon-sunday` class and `sunday_nudge_seen_<date>` key (per-date, no collision). `_showDailyBrief()` stripped of all weekly content — now fully synchronous (no `async`, no awaits); comment updated to "nudge + day shape + poem (purely daily)". CHANGELOG entry for v2.29.0 updated to drop "Sunday or Monday" mention. |
| **2.29.1** | **Poem: Seneca, Moral Letters XIII (Gummere trans.).** "There are more things, Lucilius, likely to frighten us than there are to crush us; we suffer more often in imagination than in reality." Trans. Richard Mott Gummere (1917), Loeb Classical Library vol. 1 (Gummere d. 1922). Verified vs Wikisource Moral_letters_to_Lucilius/Letter_13. Year-round. Round 18. |
| **2.29.0** | **Feature: Roadmap #7 — daily brief on empty ✦ tap + task type summarization.** Empty ✦ tap now calls `_showDailyBrief()` instead of `toggleAI()` (close is still close). Brief is a composed view inside the AI panel: (1) this morning's AI nudge line (from `day_nudge_ai_<today>` cache — already fetched by the morning init path), (2) day shape (done count + focus time, live), (3) Sunday/Monday layer — Sunday calls `_fetchWeekReflection()` (reuses same `week_reflection_<today>` cache as About panel); Monday calls new `_fetchMondayIntention()` (cache `monday_intention_<today>`); both gated on AI key + online, (4) today's poem. Fallback: if all parts empty (no nudge, no done, no poem) → proactive `_aiLoad()` as before. CSS: `.brief-container / .brief-nudge / .brief-shape / .brief-week / .brief-week-label / .brief-poem / .brief-poem-author`. **Task type summarization** (underlying data layer for brief + proactive AI): `appMemory.recentCompletedTasks` — rolling 30-day, max 50, `{text, date}` entries written in `_memoryOnTaskComplete()`; guard for older schemas. `_memoryForAI()` now derives top-5 keyword themes from `recentCompletedTasks` and appends "Recent task themes: …" to the pattern context string (feeds nudge + proactive AI). `_fetchWeekReflection()` now includes up to 15 recent completed task names in the Sunday prompt — the AI can name the kind of work done, not just numbers. `_fetchMondayIntention()`: new async function, prompts "what most deserves attention this week" from last 15 completed tasks + first 5 waiting tasks. |
| **2.28.1** | **Fix: legacy PAST tasks never purging.** `_purgePast()` had `if (!t.zoneChangedAt) return true` — tasks without the timestamp field (predating it being introduced) were kept forever, accumulating in PAST indefinitely. Fixed: missing `zoneChangedAt` now tombstones + purges the task instead of keeping it (same tombstone path as normal purge, so BUG-054 protection holds). Secondary hardening: tasks with an unrecognised status also now fall through to the 30-day aged purge instead of keeping forever. |
| **2.28.0** | **Feature: meeting mode v2 — mobile (iOS PWA) port (Roadmap #9, mobile half).** Gate: `_meetingSupported()` had `!('ontouchstart' in window)` — replaced with a capability check (`getUserMedia` + `MediaRecorder` + at least one supported MIME). Both mic buttons now visible on mobile; desktop path unchanged. Chunk sizing: iOS `MediaRecorder` produces `audio/mp4` (AAC), ignoring `audioBitsPerSecond`; AAC can hit ~192 kbps → 6-min chunk ~11.8MB base64 (doubles Netlify's 6MB limit). Fix: `_mtg.chunkMs = (mime === 'audio/mp4') ? 120000 : MEETING_CHUNK_MS` (2-min iOS chunks ≈ 3.95MB worst case). Pre-send size guard: `blob.size > 4300000` → drop + error. onstop double-restart guard: `if (wasLive && _mtg && _mtg.recorder === rec)` — the identity check prevents a preempted recorder's late onstop from restarting after a health check already started a fresh one. Dead-stream guard at top of `_meetingRecordChunk`: checks `track.readyState === 'ended'` before building a recorder. `rec.onerror` added; calls `_meetingHealthCheck()`. **Screen Wake Lock**: `_meetingWakeLock()` acquires `navigator.wakeLock.request('screen')` (iOS 16.4+, non-fatal on failure); acquired in `_meetingStart` and reacquired in `_meetingHealthCheck` (auto-released on hide, must reacquire on visible). Released in `_meetingStop` and defensively in `_meetingTeardown`. **Suspension state machine**: `visibilitychange` handler — hidden branch stamps `_mtg.hiddenAt`; visible branch calls `_meetingHealthCheck()`. `_meetingHealthCheck()`: reacquires wake lock, then checks track `readyState`; if dead → `_meetingSuspendEnd()`; if alive and recorder recording → no-op; paused → `rec.stop()`; inactive/null → clear timer, restart chunk. `_meetingSuspendEnd()`: computes minutes from `hiddenAt`, sets `_mtg.suspendNote` ("Listening stopped when the screen locked — kept the first N min"), calls `_meetingStop()`. `_meetingStop()`: when `suspendNote && hiddenAt`, computes `finalChunkSecs` from `hiddenAt` (so "Digesting last X min" counts pre-lock audio only). **Honest-note UI**: `_meetingRenderReview()` prepends `.meeting-suspend-note` div (muted xs text) in all three states when `state.suspendNote` is set. CSS: `.meeting-suspend-note { color: var(--muted); font-size: var(--text-xs); line-height: 1.5; padding: var(--space-2) 0 var(--space-3); }`. New `_mtg` fields: `wakeLock`, `hiddenAt`, `suspendNote`, `chunkMs`. Pending: real-device verify (iPhone PWA + Gemini key). |
| **2.27.3** | **Fix: BUG-050 second pass — section headers jittered a few px on mobile scroll.** The v2.27.1 fix measured `sticky-header.getBoundingClientRect().bottom` per scroll event, but iOS moves sticky elements on the compositor thread — the rect reads a few px behind the real position during scroll, and feeding those oscillating values into `--sec-sticky-top` shook the section headers (desktop scrolls on main thread, unaffected). Fix: compute the offset analytically from `scrollY` — `max(0, min(headerHeight, body.offsetHeight − scrollY))` (header is body's first child, body = 100vh, so it stays pinned until `scrollY > bodyHeight − headerHeight` then recedes) — plus rAF-coalesced scroll updates. While pinned the value is a constant, so no style invalidation at all; during departure it decays smoothly from scroll position. |
| **2.27.2** | **Feature: meeting mode language support (Roadmap #9, language half).** One prompt line added to `netlify/functions/meeting-extract.js`: "Phrase each item in the language spoken in the meeting — do not translate to English." Gemini already transcribes 100+ languages natively; the English prompt was just phrasing extracted items in English. Auto-detect, no setting (zero-config posture). Mobile half of #9 (touch gate, AAC mime, wake lock / suspension UX) remains open in Backlog. |
| **2.27.1** | **Fix: BUG-050 — sticky section headers broken (too low / snapping mid-page).** Root cause: `--sec-sticky-top` was set from `sticky-header.offsetHeight` (static). The sticky-header intentionally scrolls off on deep scroll; after it left the viewport, section headers kept their fixed `top: offsetHeight` offset and floated in empty space. Fix: replaced static measurement with scroll-aware `getBoundingClientRect().bottom` — a `window.scroll` listener updates `--sec-sticky-top = Math.max(0, bottom)` so section headers track the visible bottom of the sticky-header and fall to `top: 0` once it has scrolled away. ResizeObserver retained for header resize events. |
| **2.27.0** | **Feature: revive from PAST → SOON (Roadmap #8).** Replaces Can's manual ritual (copy aged item's text → new task → move to SOON), which created a new ID and reset age basis / orphaned session counts / looked fresh to AI insights. (1) `reviveFromPast(id)`: aged/`let_go` only (done stays — PAST is acknowledgment), same ID, clears `status`, fresh `zoneChangedAt`, increments `revived` counter (future insight signal), immediate `dropboxBackup(true)`. (2) `renderPast()`: aged/let_go rows get hover-reveal `↩︎ soon` button (`.zone-badge.pull-btn`, same pattern as SOON's "← pull in"; `#pastList` is already `.zone-list` so hover CSS applied for free); done rows keep static badge. Side effect: raw `let_go` (underscore) no longer shows as UI badge text on those rows. (3) **Merge guard now timestamp-aware**: SOON merge's phantom-task guard (`pastIds.has` unconditional skip) became `_stillPast(t)` — remote SOON entry passes only with `zoneChangedAt` strictly newer than local PAST entry's, so device A's revive survives device B's merge while stale remote soon entries stay blocked. Verified headless both directions + Sync.md/Data.md updated. **Also in 2.27.0** (post-2.26.0-commit fixes riding this version): (a) splash coda pre-populates poem content at decision time, before animation — `#splash-inner`'s `margin:auto 0` centres the full height from frame one, so the poem fade is opacity-only with no layout shift (was: content injected at fade time pushed the logo up jerkily); `_runPoemCoda` now guards on pre-populated content instead of re-injecting. (b) Morning nudge cross-device consistency: `day_nudge_ai` merge changed fill-if-empty → remote-always-wins — both devices independently generating before first sync each kept their own line forever; now whichever line reaches Dropbox propagates everywhere. (c) `.empty-poem` `letter-spacing: normal` → `0` (explicit intent, was a no-op-looking shadow reset; design-review finding). (d) Your name sync (fill-if-empty → last-write-wins via `user_names_at` ISO timestamp in backup payload); mobile layout fix for name card header (stacks title + hint vertically at ≤480px, input no longer overflows); `renderMeetingNames()` now called inside `renderConnections()` so live sync updates apply while the panel is open. (e) Section headers sticky: `.app` `overflow-x: hidden` → `clip` (enables sticky inside without creating a scroll container); `.section-header` `position: sticky`, offset via `--sec-sticky-top` CSS var set by ResizeObserver on the sticky header; `padding-bottom` instead of `margin-bottom` so background covers the gap cleanly. |
| **2.26.0** | **Feature: daily poem promoted to a morning moment (Roadmap #2, solution A+C from mock review).** (A) **Splash coda**: on the day's first splash (`poem_splash_date` vs `_localISO()`, separate from the 30-min `splash_shown_at` rule), after the typewriter's cursor hold, `_runPoemCoda()` fades `#splash-poem` (the day's `_poemOfTheDay()`) in under the date (900ms), holds `min(4000 + lines×600, 9500)`ms for a read, tap skips; then `_onSplashAnimDone()` as normal. Key written at fade-in so an early bail doesn't burn the day. New `_splashPoemHold` flag exempts the hold from the 6s anim-stall safety; new 18s ceiling backstops. Splash markup wrapped in `#splash-inner` (`margin:auto 0`, `overflow-y:auto` on `#splash`) so long poems scroll on mobile instead of clipping (flex `justify-content:center` clips overflow at top invisibly). `_doSplashDismiss` fades the poem with logo/date. (C) **Clean-slate echo**: `updateManualEmptyState()` now shows the same poem via `_poemEchoHTML()` — empty list = poem+author (was "Nothing added yet"), all done = block ✦ (`.echo-star`, keeps breath) above poem (was "✦ All done"); plain-text fallbacks kept for missing corpus. **Corpus**: Yeats 'Innisfree' + Housman 'Loveliest of trees' removed (14-liners read as a commitment, not a breath, in the coda; 91→89, longest now 11 lines — Rilke 'Autumn', 9th-c. Irish 'My tidings'). |
| **2.25.10** | **Fix: four meeting-chunking bugs (code review of the pipeline).** (1) **Payload over Netlify's 6MB limit** — `MediaRecorder` had no `audioBitsPerSecond`, so browser-default ~128 kbps × 6-min chunks ≈ 7.7MB base64 → every full-length chunk failed, retried once, dropped (6 lost minutes each, masked in short meetings). Now `audioBitsPerSecond: 32000` (~1.9MB encoded, speech-adequate). (2) Retry cross-meeting leak: `_meetingSendChunk` retry re-captured `_mtg` fresh — a discarded meeting's retrying chunk could write items/context into a newly started meeting; retries now reuse the originally captured state (new `priorState` param). (3) Crash on quick Discard: empty-final-chunk branch in `onstop` called `_meetingRenderReview(_mtg)` unguarded — `_mtg` null if Discard beat `onstop`; added guard. (4) Stale "~60s chunks" comments ×3 (module header, `_meetingRecordChunk`, netlify fn header) + `_mtg` shape comment missing `chunkStartedAt`/`finalChunkSecs`/`processingFinalChunk`; Components.md pipeline section updated (bitrate, 6-min, retry semantics) + stale "mine pre-selected" line corrected to v2.25.4 behaviour. |
| **2.25.9** | **Polish: CURRENT badge breath ends naturally.** Was `iterations: Infinity` + `anim.cancel()` on a `3 * 2400`ms timer — cancel landed mid-breath, visible snap. Now IntersectionObserver starts with `iterations: 3`; finite iterations end on final opacity-1 keyframe. |
| **2.25.8** | **Polish: text-size consistency pass.** `.meeting-owner` `--text-xs` → `--text-sm`. `.name-card-title` restyled to `.config-label` style. Connections hint copy: "for meeting attribution" → "used in meeting mode to spot your tasks". |
| **2.25.6** | **Fix: Trello fetch errors silenced when offline.** `loadTrelloBoards`/`loadTrelloLists` catch blocks check `navigator.onLine` before `showStatus`. |
| **2.25.5** | **Fix: Gemini and Claude CTAs disable correctly when offline.** Call `_applyOfflinePanel()` at end of `_aiRenderConfig()`. |
| **2.25.4** | **Fix: meeting review starts all items unselected, in meeting order.** Attribution via Gemini name-matching was unreliable — items wrongly pre-selected or dimmed. All items now start unselected; owner label stays as context hint only. (Reversed in v2.32.0 once names reliably captured.) |
| **2.25.3** | **Refactor: `assets/celebration.js` extracted (Roadmap #3, fourth module).** `_flashAccentGlow` + ember drift IIFE moved out of `index.html`. |
| **2.25.2** | **Polish: triage bar, morning nudge, config panels fade in/out.** Two-class fade pattern (`.show`/`.visible`). Pill colours + tokens; mobile pill safe-area; voice note clear-btn; meeting review task style; quota error immediate surface. |
| **2.23.7** | **Polish: meeting review panel + pill.** Panel redesigned: eyebrow + state-driven title + 3 states. Pill: "×" → "stop"; dot `--accent` → `--danger`. |
