| **2.23.0** | **Polish: meeting mode + AI config + morning nudge.** 6-min chunks, exponential backoff, "Digesting…" banner, pill redesign, review hover transitions, AI config two-provider rows, name aliases chip input, connections panel design-review, triage overlay design-review, nudge to ✦ anchor. BUG-052 ✅ + BUG-053 ✅. |
| **2.22.2** | **Fix: six meeting mode bugs.** Double-start race; WAAPI accumulation; null deref; final-chunk review; MIME once; timer drift. |
| **2.22.1** | **Polish + fix.** Zone-indicator dot removed; habit countdown inline; AI provider commit only on key save. |
| **2.22.0** | **Feature: Meeting mode (desktop-only v1) — listens to a meeting, leaves behind only tasks.** Mic SVG button in the add bar (between input and ✦; revealed by `_meetingInit()` only when desktop no-touch + Gemini key — Anthropic has no audio input). Recording: `getUserMedia` → `MediaRecorder` webm/opus in ~60s self-contained chunks via stop/restart (not `timeslice` — later chunks aren't independently decodable); each chunk base64-POSTs to new `netlify/functions/meeting-extract.js` (Gemini 2.5 Flash native audio, transcribe-internally + extract prompt, transcript never in the response) with a rolling context string carrying speaker hints across chunks. Chunk failure = retry once then drop (a lost minute beats a dead meeting; logged to error dot as [Meeting]). **Non-blocking listening**: `#meetingPill` above the add bar (breathing dot `_KF_BREATHE_SMALL` + elapsed + stop) — app stays fully usable, tasks can be added mid-meeting (Can's explicit ask; the full-screen mockup was rejected). **Review** (`#meetingOverlay`, triage-overlay clone): my items pre-selected accent, others' greyed 45% with owner label — tap to grab/drop (Can-confirmed model), live Add-count, accept path = `manualTasks.push` + `renderManual` + `dropboxAutoSave`. **Identity without voice ID**: no stored voice fingerprint ever — `today_user_name` (new input in AI config section; fill-if-empty merge, in backup payload) tells the prompt whose commitments to flag; review tap is the final filter. **Ephemeral**: audio/items/context live only in module-level `_mtg`, nulled in `_meetingTeardown()`; no meeting localStorage keys. iPhone/mobile is v2, gated on v1 extraction quality (wake lock, iOS AAC quirks deliberately out of scope). |
| **2.21.0** | **Feature: habit nudge + countdown.** `#habitsBtn` breathes (shared `icon-colour-pulse` keyframe) 10pm–3am when active habits are incomplete; clears on panel open (`habit_nudge_opened_<habitISO>`) or instantly when the last habit is checked (hook in `toggleHabitDone`'s allDone branch). Inside the panel, `renderHabits()` shows a muted countdown line — `Xh Ym left today` (10pm–midnight) or `before 3am` (midnight–3am, no ticking minutes — removes clock anxiety while surfacing the boundary); hidden when all done or outside the window. |
| **2.20.0** | **Feature: Sunday nudge.** `#infoBtn` breathes (new `icon-colour-pulse`: muted→accent colour-only, 2.4s — Can picked 1b over dot-badge and scale variants after 6 mockup rounds) on Sundays when `today_daily_history` has data, signalling the weekly summary inside About. Clears on open via `sunday_nudge_seen_<date>`. |
| **2.19.0** | **Feature: unified morning nudge — one surface instead of two.** The separate manual (`#morningNudge`, under Your tasks) and Trello (`#trelloNudge`, under From Trello) nudges competed for the same morning attention; merged into a single `#dayNudge` placed between the SOON and Trello sections. One `checkDayNudge()` replaces both check functions; one `_fetchDayNudgeAI(review, carriedOver, cards)` replaces both fetchers (AI now sees manual tasks with ages AND Trello cards with overdue/checklist markers, asked to name the one thing most worth noticing). Rule-based fallback surfaces only what's important: carried-over tasks and overdue cards lead (max two clauses); yesterday's review shows only when nothing is pressing. The old "N carried over · " prefix on AI text is gone — counts live in the AI facts. One dismiss key (`day_nudge_dismissed_`), one AI cache (`day_nudge_ai_`); noon cleanup prunes legacy pre-2.19.0 keys. `_DISMISS_SYNC`: new `day_nudge_dismissed` row; legacy `trello_nudge_dismissed`/`morning_nudge_dismissed` fields kept as rows mapped to the new key so mixed-version devices still hide each other's nudges (remove post-transition). Also in this version: poem round 16 — Mother Askani 'Thanks for today, / please for tomorrow.' added (year-round, source: original; corpus 88). |
| **2.18.40** | **Refactor: `_DISMISS_SYNC` registry — per-day nudge dismiss sync is now table-driven.** Follow-up to v2.18.39, the "medium consolidation" deliberately deferred there. Each per-day dismissable surface has two sync touchpoints that must agree: its backup-payload field and its `mergeRemoteData` block — BUG-051 and BUG-053 each happened because one touchpoint was missing for one nudge. New top-level registry `_DISMISS_SYNC = [{field, prefix, el}]` (trello + morning nudges) now drives both: the payload spreads `Object.fromEntries(...)` over it, and the merge iterates it (same `'1'`/local-key-guard/hide-element semantics, byte-equivalent to the replaced blocks; `_todayISO` had no other users). Adding a future dismissable surface (e.g. Roadmap #7 digest card) = one registry row. Triage dismissal intentionally excluded — different model (app-day string key + `triageDismissedToday` global + two elements); migrating it would be a backup-schema change. No behavior change; cross-device dismiss propagation should be re-verified on devices. |
| **2.18.39** | **Refactor: five duplication clusters extracted — no behavior change.** Motivated by BUG-051/053 (the same dismiss-sync gap fixed twice because the two nudges are copy-paste siblings). (1) `_pruneLS(prefix, exceptKey)` in `assets/util.js` — the backward localStorage prune loop appeared verbatim ×4 (both nudges' dismiss + AI-cache paths). (2) `_raceAINudge({cacheKey, cachePrefix, fetchPromise, fallbackMsg, onShow})` — the 1s-fallback/settled-flag/cache-write AI race was byte-identical in both nudges; now one helper, `onShow(text, isAI)` carries the morning nudge's addPrefix semantics. Preserved exactly: 1000ms fallback, cache written even after settle (tomorrow's fast path), no mid-read swap (BUG-034). (3) `_undoneTasks()` → `{manual, trello}` — the undoneManual/undoneTrello filter pair repeated ×7 across triage functions; destructured to keep local names. (4) `_parseAIText(data)` — plain-text ai-assist response extraction repeated ×3 (`_fetchTriageHints` kept its own JSON parsing — genuinely different). (5) `_pulseCheck(check)` — the 3-line uncheck opacity pulse duplicated in habit + task paths. Deliberately skipped: full nudge factory (touches whole morning surface), mergeRemoteData block consolidation (Non-Delegation zone, explicitness wins), module extraction (Roadmap #3). |
| **2.18.38** | **Fix: BUG-053 — morning nudge dismissal now synced across devices.** Same gap as BUG-051 (Trello nudge) but for the manual/carried-over-tasks nudge: `morning_nudge_dismissed_YYYY-MM-DD` was written locally on dismiss but never included in the Dropbox backup payload or `mergeRemoteData()`. Mirrored the BUG-051 fix exactly — added to payload + merge block, no full-restore handling needed (next 7s sync tick applies it). Two nudges share near-identical dismiss code; only one got the cross-device fix at the time. |
| **2.18.37** | **Poem curation round 15 (corpus 86 → 87) — first round under the app-moment/corpus-fit tests + tightened search process.** Brief gained two new checks (does it work *beside* a task list, not just in isolation; does it sit next to Bashō/Marcus Aurelius in tone) and an anthology-first search rule (`site:gutenberg.org OR site:en.wikisource.org OR site:archive.org` on named PD anthologies, not region/theme-first — the last four region-first searches, South America/Africa/indigenous/Scandinavia, returned zero adds). Found Cranmer-Byng's *A Feast of Lanterns* (1916, d.1945) via the new process — companion to *A Lute of Jade* (already gave us Li Po/Tu Fu). **Added: Wen T'ung 'Morning'** (c. 1100 AD, spring) — passed both new tests cleanly, literal pre-task-list dawn moment. Cut Po Chü-i 'Rain at Dawn' (mood without resolution, re-cut on app-moment test) and Heine/Bowring (searched specifically to test the country-tiebreaker rule — failed corpus-fit on tone, confirming the tiebreaker shouldn't override the brief just to diversify geography). Ou-yang Hsiu 'Bell Hill' passed both tests but held back — China is now the most-represented country in the corpus, parked pending the tiebreaker. |
| **2.18.36** | **Fix: BUG-052 — splash dismissal no longer waits for sync bookkeeping.** The load-side splash gate (`_onAppLoadDone`) fired only after the whole startup chain: Trello fetch → token refresh (extra round-trip when the 4h token expired — every first open of the morning) → download+merge → `get_metadata` rev seed → awaited `dropboxBackup(true)` push when local differed. The last two don't change what's on screen; they added 1–3 awaited round-trips (up to the 6s cap) felt as a late "explosion". Now the gate fires right after pull+merge+render; rev seed + conditional push run after as `deferredSyncBookkeeping`, and `startTicker()` follows them (rev-baseline-before-ticker invariant kept — unseeded rev would trigger a redundant first-tick restore). |
| **2.18.35** | **Fix: Bashō Milky Way haiku duplicate removed from poem corpus.** The haiku ("A rough sea, and the Milky Way…") had been added early in the corpus and again in round 12 — both identical text. Kept the round 12 entry (verified against archive.org scan); removed the unverified early entry. Corpus 87→86, autumn count 11→10. Wordsworth 'My Heart Leaps Up' confirmed intentionally truncated at 4 lines. |
| **2.18.34** | **Fix: coffee icon on Buy Me a Coffee CTA shrunk by the v2.18.25 emoji fix.** The `U+FE0E` text-presentation selector added to ☕ forced the OS to render the text glyph — which is tiny — instead of the full-colour emoji. ☕ on this button is intentional emoji (branding/recognition, not system UI), so removed FE0E from that element and added ☕ to the linter `EXEMPT` set alongside 🍅. |
| **2.18.33** | **Fix: Trello nudge was counting done tasks.** `checkTrelloNudge` built its `cards` array from raw `trelloTasks` without filtering `doneIds` — the same `.filter(t => !doneIds.has(t.id))` guard every other caller uses. Done tasks were counted in the nudge total and passed to the AI summary prompt. One-line fix at line 3625. |
| **2.18.32** | **Refactor: `_KF_BREATHE_SMALL` shared keyframe constant.** Post-batch code review (v2.18.26–31) found no correctness bugs; one cleanup: the small-element breathe keyframes (`opacity 1→0.5→1` + `scale 1→0.85→1`, 2400ms) were duplicated verbatim at three call sites (both nudge `_showNudge` closures + AI badge). Hoisted to `_KF_BREATHE_SMALL` in `assets/util.js` next to `_KF_BLINK`, with the Motion.md small-element rule in the comment; all three sites now reference it. Done-star stays inline on purpose (intentionally gentler values 0.6/0.92). No behavior change. |
| **2.18.31** | **Poem curation round 14 (corpus 85 → 87) — Stoics via Farquharson.** First pass (Casaubon 1634 + Higginson 1911: M.A. IV.3/V.1, Cleanthes hymn, Epictetus Ench. VIII) cut 4/4 on diction — Can: "no modern translations? there should be." Re-proposed the two Marcus passages in **A. S. L. Farquharson's 1944 Oxford translation** (d. 1942 — the one worldwide-PD *modern-English* Meditations; Hays/Hard copyrighted until 2070s): **IV.3 'Men look for retreats…'** and **V.1 'At dawn of day…'** — both added (year-round), verified vs the 1944 archive.org scan. **Turkish/Sufi investigated and closed:** Turkish — nothing worldwide-PD in English (Yunus Emre first seriously translated post-1956; Gibb 1900s = ornate Victorian rhyme); Sufi — Nicholson Rumi 1898 (ecstatic-abstract, thou-heavy) and Bell Hafiz 1897 (rhymed Victorian) verified and rejected on brief-fit; Barks copyrighted (declined before). Mystic lane already covered (Hafiz, Khayyám ×3, Kabir ×2, Tagore ×4). |
| **2.18.30** | **Poem curation round 13 (corpus 82 → 85) — first round under the new worldwide-only PD rule + Can's merged brief.** Proposed 5 (Yaha summer-room haiku, Alcman night fragment/Symonds, Plato 'Beneath the Pine'/Mackail, 'Meadow at Noon'/Mackail, Leonidas 'Roadside Pool'/Mackail); Can added 1/3/5 — **Yaha no. 131** (Chamberlain 1902, summer), **Plato Nature V** and **Leonidas Nature III** (Mackail's *Select Epigrams from the Greek Anthology* 1890, Gutenberg #2378, both summer). Summer 8→11 (thin-season fix). Mackail scan = new productive source (spare prose translations; "thou" diction accepted when content is right — quaint flag didn't disqualify). Can: "good with antiquity from Greek", next lead = Stoics. Dead ends: Brinton Nahuatl (ornate), Ryder Bhartrihari (preachy), Leopardi (no worldwide-safe translation). |
| **2.18.29** | **Poem curation round 12 (corpus 80 → 82) — widened country/century per Can.** Proposed 5 verified candidates spanning Germany 1780 (Goethe/Longfellow 'Wanderer's Night-Song II'), Prague 1902 (Rilke/Lemont 'Autumn'), Greece ~600 BC (Sappho fr. 95/Wharton), Japan 17th c. (Bashō ×2, Chamberlain 1902 scan). Can cut 1/3/4 — **added: Rilke 'Autumn'** (Gutenberg #38594, cross-checked vs 1918 scan; Rilke d. 1926, Lemont d. 1947 — PD worldwide; autumn) and **Bashō 'A rough sea, and the Milky Way…'** (Chamberlain 1902 no. 84; autumn kigo). Taste: melancholy-but-held in; the two calm-evening/pastoral pieces out. Chamberlain scan located on archive.org (~100 more numbered haiku — lead now productive). |
| **2.18.28** | **Poem curation round 11 (corpus 78 → 80).** Proposed 5 verified candidates (Yeats Innisfree, Lowell 'Wind and Silver', Pound 'Metro', Housman 'Loveliest of trees', Crapsey 'Snow'); Can cut 2/3/5 — **added: Yeats 'The Lake Isle of Innisfree'** (1890, d. 1939 PD worldwide, summer, verified vs Wikisource) and **Housman 'A Shropshire Lad' II** (1896, d. 1936 PD worldwide, spring, verified vs Wikisource). Notable taste signal: the two rhymed-lyrical keepers beat the imagist minis this round. Leads: **'London Snow' closed** (verified 37 lines — too long for the panel); Innisfree lead resolved; Teasdale 'February Twilight' located to *Dark of the Moon* (1926), not *Flame and Shadow*. Backlog stale-count fixed (said 66; corpus was 78). |
| **2.18.27** | **Fix: BUG-032 seventh pass — per-letter rise removed, splash logo now a single-unit opacity fade.** Six passes tried to *time* the staggered `splashLetterRise` around iOS glyph raster; structurally unwinnable for two reasons found on re-analysis: (1) starting a CSS `transform` animation on iOS/WebKit promotes each `.l` letter to its own compositing layer **at animation start**, re-rastering the glyph — the exact late-paint the passes were fighting; rejecting `will-change` (pass 5) never prevented this because the animation itself promotes. (2) The v2.17.133 "warm paint" showed the logo fully opaque at the *lowered* position for ~2 frames before snapping transparent and fading in — itself a visible flash reading as "letters coming up from the bottom." Fix: deleted `splashLetterRise`, the `.go .l` animation + 5 stagger delays, the `.instant` modifier, and the double-rAF warm dance. `#splash-logo` base is now `visibility:hidden; opacity:0`; `startSplash` animated path flips visibility (nothing paints — still opacity 0), commits the baseline, then fades the whole logo to 1 over `.5s var(--ease-out)` as ONE unit/one raster; a frame-late glyph raster lands at near-zero opacity with no motion to betray it. Ceiling path reveals statically (`opacity=1`, no fade). Fonts gate + 2000ms ceiling + `_splashStarted` guard unchanged. Motion.md rule added: never per-letter transform animations on text. |
| **2.18.26** | **Polish: nudge dot breathe now readable — opacity+scale, per small-element convention.** The v2.18.24 `.nudge-dot` used the canonical Breath Pattern verbatim (opacity 1→0.65→1, 1800ms) — on-token but imperceptible: a 35% luminance-only dip on a 6px dot has too few pixels to register. The app's own small-element precedent (done-star, AI badge) pairs opacity with **scale** at a deeper dip and slower cycle. Both `_showNudge` `_breathe` calls switched to the AI-badge treatment: `opacity 1→0.5→1` + `scale(1)→0.85→1`, 2400ms. Motion.md § Breath Pattern gains the rule: **small elements (≤ ~10px) pair opacity with scale — opacity alone doesn't read at that size**; the opacity-only form is for larger surfaces. |
| **2.18.25** | **Tooling + the violations it caught: design-lint (tier 1) & `/design-review` (tier 2).** New `scripts/design-lint.mjs` — static, no-browser, ~1s — checks the *mechanical* half of the design rules: hardcoded hex/rgba outside `:root` (Rule 19), `transition: all`, undefined CSS vars, banned vocabulary (Philosophy.md table), exclamation marks in UI strings (voice Rule 3), missing `U+FE0E` emoji selectors (Rule 20, via Unicode `\p{Emoji}`), and a soft Rule 27 render-path parity WARN. New `.claude/commands/design-review.md` → `/design-review` for the *judgment* half (voice/philosophy/psychology/Wallpaper Test) on demand. Building the linter surfaced real pre-existing violations, now fixed: 3 exclamation marks in status strings (`'Connected! …'`→`'Connected. …'` ×2, `'Dropbox connected!'`→`'Dropbox connected'`) and 7 emoji-capable glyphs missing FE0E in real UI (`⬇` install, `☕` coffee button, `↩` triage soon ×2, `↗` task-link ×3) — HTML contexts got `&#xFE0E;`, JS string literals got the literal `︎`. Lint now green; wired into the pre-commit checklist beside the smoke test. (Linter itself went through two self-review fixes: the emoji check first tried a self-calibrating watchlist that one stray `✦︎` inverted → replaced with `\p{Emoji}`; the exclamation check first matched only `!` before a quote → widened to catch mid-string, and both UI-text checks now skip `//` comments so a `⚠️` in a dev comment isn't flagged.) |
| **2.18.24** | **Polish: morning nudge presence redesign.** `.morning-nudge` (shared by both `#morningNudge` and `#trelloNudge`) sat flush to the section edge with the same 10px `--muted` weight as `.section-count` next to it — read as a footnote, easy to skip. Explored via a design-lab pass (baseline + typography/surface/pull-quote directions, three compact variants, three variants built from literally-reused shipped components — `.badge`, week-grid number/label pairs, `.streak-label`) before landing on a mix: **B2's short segmented phrasing + D3's plain monochrome typography**, no accent color on any word (Can: "the carried over number doesn't have to be another colour"). Implementation: `.morning-nudge` becomes a quiet `--surface` panel with a 2px `--accent-dim` left edge (`radius-md`, `padding: 7px var(--space-3)`) — a home of its own, like a task row, instead of blending into the header. `.nudge-dot` switches from static `--muted` to `--accent` and picks up the existing **Breath Pattern** (`_breathe`, 1800ms WAAPI opacity 1→0.65→1 — never CSS, per the BUG-028 lesson) instead of sitting flat; called fresh inside both `_showNudge()` closures right after `innerHTML` is set, so the animation is naturally discarded on every re-render (no explicit cleanup needed). The recap text itself — content, wording, two-tier AI race, per-day dismiss — is completely unchanged; only the frame around it changed. |
| **2.18.23** | **Fix: BUG-051 — Trello nudge dismissal now syncs across devices.** `trello_nudge_dismissed_YYYY-MM-DD` was written to localStorage on dismiss but never included in the Dropbox backup payload or `mergeRemoteData()`. Model: identical to `triage_dismissed` (BUG-001). Fix: added `trello_nudge_dismissed` to the backup payload (value = `'1'` if dismissed today, else `''`); added a merge block after the triage-dismissed block in `mergeRemoteData()` — if remote shows `'1'` and local hasn't dismissed yet, sets the local key and hides `$.trelloNudge` immediately. No full-restore handling needed (same as triage_dismissed — next 7s merge tick applies it). |
| **2.18.22** | **Fix: BUG-049 — new Trello cards look aged on arrival.** Trello card age was decoded from the card's MongoDB ObjectID creation timestamp (`_getCreatedFromTrelloId`), so a card created long ago in Trello but only just given a due date / moved to the today list rendered instantly dimmed (opacity 0.35), even one due *today*. Fix: age from a new `today_trello_firstseen` map (`{trello_<id>: ms}`) recording when each card first entered YOUR filtered list — the direct analog of manual `lastActive`/`createdAt`. New helpers `_getTrelloFirstSeen`/`_setTrelloFirstSeen`/`_trelloAgeBasis(id)` (falls back to `Date.now()` = fresh if unseen). Recorded + pruned in `loadTrello()` (departed cards dropped to bound the map). Both age paths switched (`taskHTML` + `renderTrello` patch — Rule 27). Synced like `today_trello_focus` (v2.18.17) but **MIN-merge** (earliest sighting wins — a card's true first-seen is the earliest any device saw it), **no date guard**, and **NOT cleared** in `applyNewDayCleanup` (must persist across days to age). `_getCreatedFromTrelloId` removed (dead after this). One-time transition: on first load post-update every current card gets `firstSeen = now` → briefly fresh, then ages naturally (no history of true entry time). |
| **2.18.21** | **Fix: done-today count inflated ("blew up") — retired the monotonic counter, derive from checked_ids (BUG-045, counter-retirement pass; same bug, counter-retirement pass).** `stat_tasks_done_today` only ever incremented (never decremented on uncheck) and merged cross-device via `Math.max`, so check/uncheck/re-check cycles and two synced devices (easy after v2.18.16 auto-restore) inflated it without bound. BUG-045's date guard (v2.18.14) only fixed the midnight carry-over, not this. Fully retired the counter: removed all 3 increment sites, the daily reset, the merge branch, the restore branch, and the backup payload fields. New `_doneTodayCount(dayISO?)` counts `checked_ids` entries dated for the day whose check is the winning op (no later `unchecked_ids` timestamp) — self-correcting and coherent across devices without `Math.max`. Readers updated: evening triage summary, `applyNewDayCleanup` daily_history snapshot (passes `yesterdayISO`, computed before checked_ids clear), `_getWeeklyStats`. **Review caught a real bug:** the unchecked Map value is the `at` string, so the guard must compare `c.at > uAt` not `c.at > u.at` (`.at` on a string is `undefined` → every uncheck would wrongly zero the task). Verified with a 7-scenario standalone test. |
| **2.18.20** | **Polish: "README" → "Read Me" in the About panel.** The daily-poem section header was a repurposed dev pun ("README"); renamed to the sentence-case "Read Me" to fit the human-voice guideline (Philosophy.md) — a gentle invitation rather than file-system boilerplate. Label-only change. |
| **2.18.19** | **Polish: triage Done button — neutral + moved to end.** The Done CTA shared Keep's accent (green) treatment and led the action row. Removed `.triage-btn.done` from the `.keep`/`.done` accent CSS rules (it now falls back to the neutral default `.triage-btn` style — same as Soon/Let go); reordered both render branches so Done is last: manual `Keep / ↩ Soon / Let go / Done`, Trello `Keep / Let go / Done`. Only Keep keeps the accent now, so the row's one positive colour points at "Keep". The `.done` class stays on the button as a semantic hook (no styling). Done badge (post-decision label) left accent — unchanged, out of scope. |
| **2.18.18** | **Polish: BUG-032 — smoother splash letter-rise.** The `splashLetterRise` keyframe used easeOutExpo (`--ease-out`, `cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)`) over a tiny `translateY(0.12em)` (~11px) distance in `.4s`. That curve does ~90% of the travel in the first ~120ms then crawls the last ~1px sub-pixel for ~280ms; on non-composited text the browser pixel-snaps each frame, so the tail rendered as discrete steps ("pop then stutter"). Tuned to a gentler easeOutQuint **inlined** `cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)`, distance `0.18em`, duration `.55s`, stagger `.04s→.07s` (delays .06→.34). Kept the `--ease-out` var untouched (container fade + app transitions still ride it). Base `.l` `translateY`, keyframe `from`, and the JS comment all moved to `0.18em` in lockstep (the byte-identical invariant that prevents the v2.17.97/112 first-frame jump). No GPU layer promotion — it would re-raster glyphs and risk the BUG-032 raster fix. BUG-032 orchestration (visibility/warm-raster/container-fade) untouched. |
| **2.18.17** | **Fix: BUG-043 cross-device — Trello focus map now syncs via Dropbox.** `today_trello_focus` (the per-card activity signal that un-dims aged cards) was local-only — not in the backup payload, not in `mergeRemoteData()`. A focus session on device A never propagated to device B, so the card stayed dimmed on the other device. Fix: `_setTrelloFocus()` now auto-stamps `today_trello_focus_date = _getAppDay()` on every write; `applyNewDayCleanup()` clears both keys; backup payload carries both; `mergeRemoteData()` union-merges the focus maps (date-guarded, parallel to the focus-mins and done-count guards) and calls `renderTrello()` when cards are newly un-dimmed; full-restore path restores the map if it's from today, otherwise clears it. |
| **2.18.16** | **Fix: BUG-047 — Dropbox connect on fresh install now auto-restores.** `_dropboxExchangeCode()` was calling `dropboxAutoSave()` immediately after OAuth — writing empty local state over the existing Dropbox backup. Fix: detect fresh install (`manualTasks`, `habitsList`, `soonTasks`, `pastTasks` all empty), probe Dropbox via `get_metadata` to check if a backup exists, and call `dropboxRestore(false)` if one is found. Reconnect-with-local-data falls back to `dropboxAutoSave()` as before. Error path also falls back to save. |
| **2.18.15** | **Fix: BUG-046 — Trello board selector re-render loop.** `loadTrelloBoards()` called `renderConnections()` to create the `<select>` element, but `renderConnections()` calls `loadTrelloBoards()` whenever there is a Trello token but no board saved. This created a tight loop: each board API response (~200–500ms) triggered a full panel re-render, which started another fetch, which re-rendered again. On mobile this manifested as the board dropdown and Dropbox buttons flickering continuously and the native picker being dismissed before the user could select. Fix: removed `renderConnections()` from inside `loadTrelloBoards()` — both call paths that reach this function (`renderConnections→loadTrelloBoards` on panel-open, `renderConnections→loadTrelloBoards` after auth) already have the `boardSelect` element in the DOM by the time the board fetch completes. |
| **2.18.14** | **Fix: done-task count inflated by yesterday's sync after midnight (BUG-024 class).** `stat_tasks_done_today` had no companion date stamp, so the same class of bug that hit focus minutes (fixed v2.17.44) also affected the done count. After midnight, the first 7s sync tick downloads yesterday's Dropbox backup (e.g. "3 tasks done yesterday") and `Math.max(0, 3)` restores 3 as today's count — before any tasks are done today. Any tasks completed post-midnight then accumulate on top of the inflated base. Added `stat_tasks_done_today_date` parallel to `stat_focus_mins_date`: set on every increment (triage done, normal checkbox) and on daily reset in `applyNewDayCleanup()`. Backup payload now carries the stamp (fallback `''`, never `_getAppDay()` — same lesson as BUG-024 v2.17.46). `mergeRemoteData()` and the full-restore path now date-guard the done count exactly as they do for focus minutes. |
| **2.18.13** | **Fix: BUG-041 third pass — iOS PWA white flash was the OS launch screen, not the WebView first paint.** The "white → dark → splash" sequence on iPhone home-screen cold launch decomposes as: frame 0 = OS-painted launch screen (white, because no `apple-touch-startup-image` was registered) → frame 1 = WebView first paint (`#0e0e10`, the v2.18.2/v2.18.7 fix working) → splash. The two prior passes only ever controlled frame 1. Added a full `apple-touch-startup-image` set (one solid `#0e0e10` PNG per current iPhone resolution, generated by `scripts/gen-splash.mjs`, precached in `sw.js`) plus the `apple-mobile-web-app-capable` meta. **Gotcha:** iOS caches launch images at add-to-home-screen time — the PWA must be removed and re-added for the fix to take effect; a plain relaunch keeps the old white frame (likely why earlier verification was inconclusive). |
| **2.18.12** | **Fix: Trello age-bucket dimming now updates every 7 s and on wake.** The `data-age-bucket` patch inside `renderTrello()` is purely local and network-free, but it was only called when `syncTrello()` detected a change in `board.dateLastActivity`. Cards whose board had no recent Trello activity never had their opacity updated in the DOM, causing cross-device inconsistency: the device that triggered a Trello sync saw correct aging; the other kept stale buckets until a manual Refresh. Fixed by calling `renderTrello()` unconditionally in `syncAll()` (every 7 s) and in the `visibilitychange` wake handler. |
| **2.18.11** | **Fix: BUG-043 parity — manual tasks un-dim on partial focus too.** The v2.18.8 fix made Trello cards un-dim on any focus session (even Escape without completing), but manual tasks still required a completed pomodoro (`_logSession` → `lastActive`). `closeUI` now also refreshes `lastActive` and removes `data-age-bucket` for manual tasks when any focus time was spent. Both types now align: any engagement un-dims, pomodoro completion is not required. |
| **2.18.10** | **Fix: morning nudge always shows carried-over count.** The AI tier-2 sentence was replacing the rule-based message entirely when cached or when it won the 1s race — silently dropping the count. Rule-based path was unaffected (count is in the text). AI path now prepends `"N carried over · "` when `carriedOver > 0`, so the count is always visible regardless of which tier wins. |
| **2.18.9** | **Fix: PAST zone now shows all tasks.** The list was capped at 20 (`pastTasks.slice(0, 20)`) for "performance" but the count badge always showed the true total — a confusing mismatch for anyone with >20 PAST tasks. Removed the slice; at realistic personal-task volumes the full list renders instantly. |
| **2.18.8** | **Fix: BUG-043 — aged Trello cards now un-dim after any focus session.** Previously `today_trello_focus` (the activity signal for Trello card age) was only incremented when a full 25-minute pomodoro completed or when a task was checked off while focusing. Closing the focus UI early via Escape or task-switch — the common case — never updated the map, so the card stayed dimmed even after genuine engagement. Fix: `closeUI` now marks the card as engaged (`today_trello_focus[id] = 1`) when any focus time was spent and the count is still 0. Guard prevents double-increment if a completed pomodoro or `_focusOnCheck` already logged the session. Instant visual un-dim on close; the 7s Trello patch cycle confirms it. |
| **2.18.7** | **Fix: BUG-041 second pass — white flash on Android/Arc cold start.** v2.18.2 fixed iOS (inline `<html>` bg + `color-scheme:dark` meta) but a white frame survived on **Arc** (Chromium) on Android. Diagnosis: it's a **pre-first-paint frame** showing the browser's default canvas, which is **white in light system mode** (dark mode masked it — user's theme is dynamic). Our dark signals weren't applied early enough by Arc. Mitigation (additive, can't regress): (1) `<html>` inline `background` → `background-color` (the property Chromium's pre-FCP base-color heuristic reads); (2) added inline `background-color:#0e0e10` to `<body>` (heuristic can read body); (3) added `color-scheme: dark` to `:root` in CSS, not only the meta. Honest caveat: Arc's standalone-PWA rendering is partly browser-controlled — if a frame survives, the residue is Arc-side. Verify by forcing light mode + cold-launch. |
| **2.18.6** | **Fix: BUG-044 — delayed focus chime when not in focus mode.** `closeUI(false)` (Escape, task-switch, line 11983) cleared the tick timer + closed the UI/PiP but left `st.running=true` + `st.wallStart` set — a "zombie" session. The `visibilitychange` correction iterates all running states, so on tab return it jumped the zombie's `rem` to ≤0 and called `completeFor` → `playChime()` with no focus UI active = a late, out-of-context chime. (Click-outside already stopped the session before closeUI; the other callers didn't.) Fix: `closeUI` now stops the closing task's session (`running=false, paused=false, wallStart=null`) right after `_trackFocusTime`, centralizing what click-outside did manually. `continueTicking` has no callers (no intended background tick) and PiP is closed by closeUI, so after any closeUI there should be no running session — this is a correctness fix, not a behavior change. Resume-on-reclick (keys on `rem`, line 11667) and focus-minute accounting (keys on `rem`/`tracked`) unaffected. Also fixes a latent "switch task A→B leaves A running." No `_focusUIActive` guard added on the chime (would wrongly mute the legit PiP completion chime). |
| **2.18.5** | **Housekeeping (docs sync + DEV_HOURS).** No functional change. Synced memory docs that had drifted over the v2.17.135→v2.18.4 run: `Data.md` gained `today_trello_order_at` + the nudge keys (`morning_nudge_dismissed_<date>`, `trello_nudge_ai/dismissed_<date>`) and the triage `decision` enum now lists `"done"`; `Components.md` triage diagram updated (Done action, checkbox marker removed, Let go label) + Morning Nudge section (two-tier race, per-day dismiss flag, Trello nudge, v2.18.3 consistency); this changelog trimmed to a rolling 20 (v2.17.126→v2.17.87, 40 entries, moved to `Changelog-archive.md`). `DEV_HOURS` 213h→219h (+6, confirmed by Can). |
| **2.18.4** | **Fix: two Trello bugs.** **BUG-042 (order scramble across devices, worst on a new day):** `trello_order` rides in *every* backup, and `mergeRemoteData` adopted it unconditionally ("remote wins", unchanged since v2.12.28) — so any unrelated write from the other device re-asserted its possibly-stale order. Surfaced hardest at the day boundary, when both devices run `applyNewDayCleanup` → re-fetch → re-sync at once. Added a `today_trello_order_at` ISO stamp (set on reorder, line ~7277), carried in the payload; merge now adopts remote order only if `remote.trello_order_at > local` **or** the device has no local order yet (bootstrap). Empty-string defaults mean an untimestamped old-client backup never clobbers a timestamped local order. Full-restore path carries the stamp too. Additive field — backward compatible, no schema bump. **BUG-043 (aged Trello card won't un-dim after a focus session):** the `data-age-bucket` opacity uses age = `_getCreatedFromTrelloId(id)` (card creation, immutable) for Trello, vs `lastActive` for manual tasks — so manual tasks un-age on activity but Trello cards never could. Now a Trello card with a focus session today (`_getTrelloFocus()[id] > 0`, daily-reset) reads as active → full opacity, in both `taskHTML` and the 7s patch path. |
| **2.18.3** | **Design: nudge consistency pass** (morning + Trello nudge share `.morning-nudge`, so both update). Three changes from a product-design critique: **(1) Alignment** — `.morning-nudge` padding `var(--space-2) var(--space-4)` → `var(--space-2) 0`, so the dot sits flush at the section edge, on the same left line as the `.section-label` above and the task-box edge below (both at 0 inset; `.section`/`.task-list` have no horizontal padding). Previously the `space-4` left inset floated it to a third x-position. **(2) Dot role** — `.nudge-dot` recolored `var(--accent)` → `var(--muted)` and dropped `opacity: 0.6`. Accent is the active/positive signal (done-star, checked, focus); a passive recap shouldn't wear it. Now a neutral typographic bullet in the text's own tone. (Kept the dot over full removal to preserve the gentle-reminder's noticeability.) **(3) Quiet-voice tracking** — the secondary muted voice had drifted across three letter-spacings: `.section-count` (0), `.morning-nudge` (0.04em), `.empty` (0.06em). Standardized on **0.04em** — the app's de-facto quiet-text tracking (16 inline uses: `streak-label`, `habit-empty`, `week-col-day`…). `.section-count` gains it, `.empty` drops 0.06→0.04 (now also matches `.habit-empty`); the nudge was already correct. No `--tracking` token introduced — would orphan the other 13 inline uses; left as a future system-wide pass. |
| **2.18.2** | **Fix: BUG-041 — white flash before dark on mobile cold start** — distinct from BUG-032 (that's the *logo glyphs*; this is the *page canvas*). The dark background lived only in the inline `<style>` (`html,body { background: var(--bg) }`), so the first rendered frame — before the stylesheet parsed — used the UA default **white**. No `color-scheme` hint and no presentational background on `<html>`, and iOS PWAs ignore manifest `background_color` (Android Chrome honors it, which is why it was iOS-only). On warm cache the gap is sub-frame; on mobile cold start it's visible: white → dark → splash. Fix: added `<meta name="color-scheme" content="dark">` (UA paints a dark default canvas pre-CSS) + an inline `style="background:#0e0e10"` on `<html>` (the one literal that paints on frame 1, before CSS vars exist — deliberate Rule 19 exception, documented inline, kept in sync with `--bg`). `theme-color` (line ~311) only tints the status-bar chrome — not the cause. |
| **2.18.1** | **Polish: triage rows lose the leading checkbox marker** — the decorative (non-interactive) `.triage-task-check` 16px square was removed from both render paths in `renderTriageList()`, and the `margin-left: 28px` it forced on `.triage-actions`/`.triage-task-hint` dropped. The four-up action row (Done/Keep/↩ Soon/Let go) now spans the full row width and stays on one line on phones — chosen over a 2×2 wrap. Also removes a hardcoded `28px` magic number + the `.triage-task-check` CSS rule. |
| **2.18.0** | **Feat: "Done" action in evening triage** — undone tasks in the triage overlay now get a **Done** button alongside Keep / ↩ Soon / Let go (Trello cards: Done / Keep / Let go), for tasks completed but never checked off. `triageDecide(id,'done')` → `triageApplyAll()` runs `_markDoneInTriage()`: adds to `doneIds`, `_addCheckedId`, `_removeUncheckedId`, increments `stat_tasks_done_today`, `_memoryOnTaskComplete` — mirrors a normal check's data side **without** the celebration (the summary screen is the acknowledgment). Manual 'done' tasks stay in `manualTasks` like 'kept' (excluded from `movedIds`) so normal day-cleanup moves them to PAST with status 'done'; Trello 'done' tasks hide locally (in `doneIds`, not deleted from Trello). Stat is incremented before `_doneToday`/`today_day_review` are read, so the summary and morning nudge reflect them. **Also fixed:** the Trello 'letgo' branch called `_persistDone()` which was **never defined** (latent `ReferenceError` aborting `triageApplyAll` mid-run whenever a Trello card was let go) — replaced with a single `today_done` persist after both loops. CSS: `.triage-btn.done`/`.triage-task-badge.done` share Keep's accent treatment. |
| **2.17.141** | **Cleanup: connection rows no longer highlight on hover/tap** — `.connection-row:hover` (border-color → `--muted`) and its now-dead `transition: border-color` removed. The Trello/Dropbox connected rows are status displays, not buttons; on mobile the `:hover` stuck after a tap (classic sticky-hover) and read as a persistent selection. No other hover/active/focus rules on `.connection-row`/`.connection-card` (the real buttons inside keep their own `.btn-sm:hover`). |
| **2.17.140** | **Cleanup: removed sync-conflict count from the UI** — the "N sync conflicts auto-resolved" telemetry line under the Dropbox connection row (added v2.17.101/102) is gone, along with its `.connection-row-note` CSS and the `today_merge_anomalies` localStorage persistence that fed it (now orphaned). The conflict *resolution* logic is untouched — union merge still discards the losing intent correctly. `_logMergeAnomaly()` simplified to a console-only breadcrumb (kept for sync debugging); its two call sites in `mergeRemoteData()` remain. |
| **2.17.139** | **Fix: BUG-040 — morning nudge reappeared after dismiss** — regression from the v2.17.128 self-heal. The dismiss handler cleared `morning_nudge_count`/`today_day_review` but set no persistent flag, so every `visibilitychange` → `_onWake()` re-ran `checkMorningNudge()`, the self-heal recomputed the carried-over count from live `manualTasks`/`doneIds`, and the dismissed nudge resurrected with identical content (confirmed on desktop: each focus-away/focus-back). Fix: per-day `morning_nudge_dismissed_<date>` flag (same primitive as the Trello nudge) guards at the top of the function before self-heal runs; date-scoped so tomorrow's nudge and the across-day self-heal are unaffected. Old flags pruned on dismiss; Trello nudge got the same prune loop for parity. |
| **2.17.138** | **Feat: Trello morning nudge** — the Trello section now gets its own morning nudge mirroring the manual-tasks one: same two-tier race (rule-based tier 1 → AI tier 2 raced against a 1s timeout, AI result cached per day), morning-only (hides at noon), dismissible per day. New `checkTrelloNudge()` + `_fetchTrelloNudgeAI(cards)`; rule-based reads card count + overdue flag, AI names the natural starting point. Called from the end of `renderTrello()` (covers the 7s sync tick) and `checkMorningNudge()` (covers wake). `#trelloNudge` element reuses the existing `.morning-nudge` CSS. |
| **2.17.137** | **Fix: BUG-039 — all-habits-done celebration never fired** — `toggleHabitDone()` checked `habitsList.every(...)` including archived habits; archived habits have no completion for today, so `allDone` was permanently false for anyone who'd ever archived a habit. Broke silently when archiving landed in v2.17.106. Fix: filter to `habitsList.filter(h => !h.archived)` before the check (length guard + `.every()`), matching the pattern already in `renderHabits()`. |
| **2.17.136** | **Fix: BUG-038 — red error dot on mobile when offline** — two gaps: (1) both `reg.update()` calls (30-min interval + visibilitychange) had no `.catch()`, so on iOS Safari the SW-update rejection could route through `window.onerror`; (2) `window.onerror` had no network-error filter, while `unhandledrejection` already did (including `'Failed to update a ServiceWorker'`). Fix: `.catch(() => {})` on both update calls + same network-error string filter added to `window.onerror`. Suppression-by-classification — an offline update check failing is expected, not an app error, consistent with how `_logSyncError` and `unhandledrejection` already treat network drops. |
| **2.17.135** | **Fix: task list behind on morning open (day-cleanup backup race)** — `applyNewDayCleanup()` fires a `dropboxBackup(true)` at the end to push the cleaned state. On morning wake via `visibilitychange` (app resuming from background, not a full reload), this upload raced `syncDropbox()`'s metadata fetch: if the upload landed on Dropbox before the metadata fetch returned, `syncDropbox` saw the stale local rev, downloaded mobile's own stale write, and the 7s ticker found no further rev change — leaving the task list behind until the other device made another write. Fix: wrapped the cleanup backup in a 3s `setTimeout`, matching the documented triage grace-window pattern (same race class). `zoneChangedAt` timestamps in `mergeRemoteData` already protect the done→PAST move independently of this backup. |
| **2.17.134** | **Polish: splash font-load ceiling reduced to 2000ms** — was 2500ms; fonts are SW-cached and preloaded so they load well under 100ms on a warm cache; tighter ceiling means the static fallback fires sooner on a genuinely stalled load. |
| **2.17.133** | **Fix: BUG-032 fifth pass — transform-only reveal (root-cause class fix)** — four prior passes all tried to *time* `.go` to a `document.fonts` signal, but on iOS the API reports the face "loaded" before WebKit rasterises the glyphs at the real rendered size. With `font-display:block` the glyph box paints blank until raster lands, so the per-letter opacity ramp raced the real paint → glyphs appeared mid-rise. Reworked: `#splash-logo` is `visibility:hidden` until ready; gate switched to `fonts.load()` promise raced against 2500ms ceiling; `startSplash` flips to `visible` at start position, forces in-view raster across two rAFs, then fades the whole container as one unit while a transform-only keyframe runs the rise. `.l` base is now `opacity:1`; `.instant` ceiling rule drops opacity. |
| **2.17.132** | **Fix: BUG-036 — This Week data diverged across devices** — `today_daily_history` was local-only, never in the Dropbox backup, so each device snapshotted its own days at midnight. Added `daily_history` to the backup (schema **5.2 → 5.3**) and a union merge in both `mergeRemoteData()` and the full-restore path via `_mergeDailyHistory(local, remote)` — union by date, keep richer snapshot on duplicates, cap 30 days. Backward compatible. |
| **2.17.131** | **Poem curation** — removed Edward Thomas's 'Adlestrop' excerpt from `assets/poems.js`. Corpus 69 → 68. |
| **2.17.130** | **Fix: BUG-032 fourth pass — splash logo rise smoothness** — `document.fonts.check()` confirms the face is loaded but the glyph raster cache is per-size; first paint at `clamp(48px,9vw,96px)` collided with frame 1 of the rise. Fix: paint letters once at `opacity:0.02`, force layout, then on next rAF revert and add `.go` — rise runs on already-rasterised glyphs. ~16ms cost, no new assets. |
| **2.17.129** | **Fix: morning nudge Tier 2 now shows on first open** — BUG-034 fix (3s guard) inadvertently blocked Tier 2 on every cold start since AI typically takes >3s. Replaced swap-after-display with an all-or-nothing race: AI fetch vs 1s timeout — whoever wins determines what's shown. No content swap while reading. Cached days show Tier 2 instantly. |
| **2.17.128** | **Fix: BUG-033 second pass — morning nudge self-heals** — `checkMorningNudge()` now self-heals if `morning_nudge_count` is 0/missing, falling back to counting live undone tasks from `manualTasks`/`doneIds` directly and re-persisting. No dependency on day-transition timing. |
| **2.17.127** | **Fix: BUG-035 — Trello cards now age visually** — `taskHTML()` previously gated age calculation on `type === 'manual'`. Trello IDs encode a Unix creation timestamp in the first 8 hex chars; new `_getCreatedFromTrelloId(id)` extracts it. Both `type === 'manual'` guards removed — age bucket applies to any undone task. Trello patch path (7s cycle) also updated. |
| **2.17.126** | **Fix: BUG-032 splash logo appears mid-rise (third pass)** — prior fixes (v2.17.97, v2.17.112) gated the `.go` rise on `document.fonts.load()` but kept a blind `setTimeout(startSplash, 800)` fallback. On slow cold starts that timeout fired first, adding `.go` while Syne was still in its `font-display:block` period — the rise animated **invisibly** and the glyphs popped into view mid-rise once the font finally painted. Root mechanism corrected: it's not a fallback-font position swap (block hides the fallback entirely), it's a desync between the rise animation and font paint. Replaced the load+800ms-timeout race with a `requestAnimationFrame` poll of `document.fonts.check('800 96px Syne')` + `check('300 13px "DM Mono"')` — `.go` is added only on a frame where both faces are genuinely usable, so the rise always plays from `opacity:0` with visible glyphs. Generous 2500ms ceiling (vs 800ms) reveals the logo statically via a new `.instant` modifier (`animation:none; opacity:1`) rather than stalling — even the ceiling path can't pop mid-rise. `startSplash` now takes an `animated` flag. Stays well under the 6s dismiss safety net. |
| **2.17.125** | **Fix: two morning nudge bugs** — (1) Nudge missing on first cold-start of the day: `applyNewDayCleanup()` sets `morning_nudge_count` after `init()` already called `checkMorningNudge()`, so count was stale/missing. Fixed by adding `checkMorningNudge()` after `applyNewDayCleanup()` in the sync startup block (~line 8955) — mirrors what the Dropbox restore path already does. (2) AI upgrade swapped message while user was reading: added `_nudgeShownAt = Date.now()` before the async fetch; if AI arrives >3s after the rule-based message was shown, skip the live DOM swap (AI text still cached, loads instantly on next open). |
| **2.17.124** | **Roadmap #3 — extract `assets/idle.js`** — moved the idle companion IIFE (7 ASCII creatures + activity tracking + idle detection, ~280 lines) out of index.html into its own classic-script file. Fully encapsulated: registers its own activity listeners + 5s idle interval on load, appends to `document.body` only when idle, zero app-state/app-function coupling (verified — no external refs to its internals). Load order irrelevant; loaded after poems.js, precached in `sw.js`. Moved by line-range (not retyped) to avoid ASCII-art transcription errors. Smoke test green (can't reach the 45s-idle path — manual device check: step away, creature fades in, any interaction dismisses). |
| **2.17.123** | **Roadmap #3 — finish leaf sweep into `assets/util.js`** — folded the last pure leaves out of index.html: `COLOR_ACCENT/BG/MUTED/BORDER` consts + `_breathe`/`_KF_BLINK` animation primitives. (No separate `motion.js`: `_pulseComplete` is a closure inside the focus IIFE at ~11176 and can't move without it, so a motion module would be ~5 lines — folded into util instead.) Pure utility extraction now complete; next modules are cohesive feature clusters (sound, idle, celebration, trello, insights). |
| **2.17.122** | **Roadmap #3 — first module extraction (`assets/util.js`)** — pulled the pure leaf utilities out of the single inline `<script>` into a new classic-script file loaded before the main script (and before `poems.js`): `safeJSON`, `_getAppDay`, `_localISO`, `_formatFocusTime`, `esc`, and the habit-time trio (`HABIT_ROLLOVER_HOURS` + `_habitNow` + `_habitTodayISO`). Chosen leaf-first — these are depended on everywhere (safeJSON 48×, esc 34×, _localISO 27×) but depend on nothing, so nothing extracted later can break them. Classic scripts share the global lexical environment, so the `function`/`const` globals stay visible to the main script (same pattern as `const POEMS`). `safeJSON` must precede the main script's state-init (`manualTasks`/`doneIds`) — load order guarantees it. `sw.js` `CACHE_APP_SHELL` now precaches `/assets/util.js` so offline boot still finds it. Smoke test boots clean using the extracted utils — proves the template. Out of scope this round (still in index.html): `_breathe`/`_KF_BLINK` (→ future `motion.js`), `_cacheElements`/`$` (DOM-coupled). |
| **2.17.121** | **Week-grid "best day" dot → composite metric** — the dot in About → This Week previously marked the single most-tasks day, which was **redundant with the bar above it** (bar height already encodes task count). Now it scores each day by a normalized weighted composite: `0.4·(tasks/maxTasks) + 0.35·(focus/maxFocus) + 0.25·(habitsKept/habitsTotal)` — tasks lead (app's primary verb) but a balanced day can out-score a pure task-grind day, so the dot carries signal the bars don't. `_days` now also carries `habitsKept`/`habitsTotal` (live for today via `_activeHabits`, snapshot for past days). Gates preserved: week total tasks ≥ 4, strict single leader (score `*1000` rounded → no dot on a genuine tie), today excluded from winning. Tooltip on the winning dot shows the breakdown (`Your best day · N done · Xm focused · k/t habits`) to mitigate composite opacity. Weights are tunable. |
| **2.17.120** | **Token audit — panel/tab CSS** — replaced all hardcoded px padding with spacing tokens: `.config-input` `8px 12px` → `space-2 space-3`; `select.config-input` background-position `12px` → `space-3`, `padding-right 32px` → `space-8`; `.btn-primary` `8px 16px` → `space-2 space-4`; `.btn-ghost` `8px 14px` → `space-2 space-3`; `.status-msg` `8px 12px` → `space-2 space-3`; `.habit-input` `10px 14px` → `space-3`; `.habit-add-btn` `0 18px` → `0 space-4`. Micro-gaps (2px/3px in stat grid, week grid, week col) left as acceptable micro-adjustments per Rules.md. Structural values (bar height, toast offset) left unchanged. |
| **2.17.119** | **Tokenise habit streak + × button width** — `.habit-streak` and `.habit-edit-delete` both had hardcoded `width: 28px` (not on the token scale). Changed to `var(--space-8)` (32px, nearest token). Both share the same token so they stay matched when the × replaces the streak in edit mode. |
| **2.17.118** | **About polish** — (1) Stat order swapped: Streak → Flow → Focus (was Focus → Flow → Streak). (2) Coffee/dev-hours footer: `.panel-section--flush` modifier removes `border-top` and the `.readme-actions` wrapper; layout now directly flex on the section. (3) Psalm 118 ("This is the day which the Lord hath made") removed from `assets/poems.js` (corpus now 65). |
| **2.17.117** | **Fix: habit × alignment in edit mode** — `.habit-edit-delete` had `height: 100%` and `line-height: 1` which caused the × to sit off-centre relative to the week dots. Removed both; added `width: 28px` to match `.habit-streak` (the element it replaces). Grid's `align-items: center` now handles vertical positioning cleanly. |
| **2.17.116** | **Habit archive undo snackbar** — archiving a habit now shows the same undo toast as task deletion. `archiveHabit` pushes a snapshot to `_archivedHabitStack` (max 10, same as `_deletedStack`) and calls `_showUndoToast` with `type='habit'`. Toast button now calls `_undoLast()` which dispatches to `_undoArchiveHabit()` or `_undoDelete()` based on `_lastUndoType`. `_undoArchiveHabit` sets `h.archived = false`, saves, re-renders, and refreshes edit mode. Timeout (5s) clears both stacks. |
| **2.17.115** | **About tab — README section spacing fix + tokenisation** — `#dailyPoem margin-bottom` removed (was a relic from before coffee/dev-hours moved to their own section; `panel-section` gap handles separation now). `.readme-actions margin-top` removed (was firing even when install button is `display:none`, adding phantom spacing). Install button gets its own `margin-top: var(--space-3)` inline so it still has breathing room from the poem when visible. `.dev-hours` and `.btn-coffee` padding tokenised: `7px 12px` / `7px 14px` → `var(--space-2) var(--space-3)`. |
| **2.17.114** | **About tab — dev hours + coffee below changelog** — moved `☕ Buy me a coffee` and dev-hours counter out of the README `panel-section` into their own section appended after Changelog. Install button stays in README. |
| **2.17.113** | **Version single-source + working-doc adoption (Cooked feedback gap analysis)** — `APP_VERSION` is now `Object.keys(CHANGELOG)[0]` (derived, not hand-typed — kills index.html-internal drift; only edit the changelog). `sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` stays hand-synced (separate SW context, no build) but `scripts/smoke-test.mjs` now asserts `today-v${APP_VERSION}` matches and fails the gate on drift — would have caught both version misses this session. Docs: Rules.md § Operating Mode (discipline runs unprompted, commit/push human-gated, push `dev` without re-asking); Housekeeping commit-type→segment map + mixed-commit rule + derived-version note; Wallpaper Test now produces a dated W3 row in Backlog "Watching" (closes the principle→artifact loop); Performance-audit destination-by-destination egress table. Smoke-test error-policy documented (why "any pageerror" is correct for a single-file app). No behavior change in the app beyond the derived version. |
| **2.17.112** | **Fix: BUG-032 splash logo shift (recurrence)** — v2.17.97 gated the logo reveal on `document.fonts.ready`, but iOS Safari resolves `.ready` before custom fonts paint on cold start, so the logo revealed in the fallback font and Syne swapped in a frame later — repositioning the glyphs within the `line-height:1` box (visible downward jump). Now gates on `document.fonts.load('800 96px Syne', 'TODAY')` + `document.fonts.load('300 13px "DM Mono"', 'JANUARY')` (resolves only when those faces are truly loaded), with the 800ms cap + `_splashStarted` idempotency kept. Also added `<link rel="preload">` for both splash woff2 files so they fetch before first paint. Column layout height was already font-family-independent (unitless line-heights on px sizes), so no structural change needed. |
| **2.17.111** | **Fix: morning nudge AI mentioned completed task** — `_fetchMorningNudgeAI` now filters `taskLines` against both `doneIds` AND `pastTasks` IDs. After midnight cleanup, manual IDs are cleared from `doneIds`; if a stale Dropbox backup re-adds a done task to `manualTasks` before `pastIds` is checked, the old filter would include it in the AI prompt. |
| **2.17.110** | **Input bar placeholder shortened** — "Add a task — or ask ✦ anything" → "Add a task or ask ✦" — fits mobile without truncation. |
| **2.17.109** | **About tab — README above Changelog** — swapped section order so README (poem + install + coffee) appears first, Changelog below. |
| **2.17.108** | **Mobile habit row padding correction** — v2.17.104 tightened both vertical and horizontal padding; vertical was unwanted. Reverted vertical to `space-2` (8px), horizontal stays at `space-2` (down from `space-3`). |
| **2.17.107** | **WAAPI splash migration — looping animation conversion complete (v2.17.103 follow-up)** — `splashCursorBlink` CSS infinite → `cursor.animate([{opacity:1},{opacity:0}], {duration:500, easing:'step-end', iterations:Infinity})` at typewriter completion. `splashStarBreath` CSS inline style → `_breathe(star, [...], 3200, 400)` at splash init. Both `@keyframes` blocks deleted + `#splash-cursor.blink` CSS rule deleted. Splash elements are dismissed before `_appReady=true` so `_onWake` never touches them — conversion is rule compliance, not a risk fix. All infinite CSS loops now WAAPI except `errorPulse` (explicit exemption: outside `#main-app`, `_forceRepaint` never reaches it, documented in Motion.md). |
| **2.17.106** | **Habits archive instead of delete** — `×` in edit mode now calls `archiveHabit(id)` (sets `h.archived = true`) instead of removing the habit and wiping completion history. `renderHabits` filters to `activeHabits = habitsList.filter(h => !h.archived)` so archived habits are invisible but their data survives in localStorage and Dropbox. `archived` flag propagates to all devices via the existing union-merge spread. `deleted_habit_ids` unchanged — still used only for true hard deletes (no UI path currently). |
| **2.17.105** | **Fix: BUG-030 checkmark jank on iOS cold start** — canvas pre-warm now exercises `createRadialGradient`, `arc`/`fill`, and `fillText` Metal shaders at off-screen coords (only `clearRect` was pre-warmed before, leaving GPU stalls on first `celebAnimate` RAF frame). Haptic switch `<input type="checkbox" switch>` element now created eagerly at IIFE init time instead of lazily on first tap, removing DOM append + style recalc from inside the task-check handler. |
| **2.17.104** | **Habit rows tighter on mobile** — `@media (max-width: 480px)` `.habit` padding `space-2/space-3` → `space-1/space-2` (8/12px → 4/8px), on-token. Desktop unchanged (`space-3/space-4`). |
| **2.17.103** | **WAAPI migration complete — suppress/restore machinery deleted (Roadmap #5)** — `.ai-badge`, `.done-star`, `.loading-dots`, thinking-`…` converted to WAAPI via new global `_breathe(el, kf, dur, delay)` helper (+`_KF_BLINK`); no cancel handles needed — every caller's element is removed/innerHTML-replaced with its state. Thinking ellipsis became a real span (`.ai-thinking-dots`) since WAAPI can't target `::after` (the old suppress entry never reached it either). `#errorIndicator` stays CSS — it's outside `#main-app`, repaint never touched it (its suppress entry was dead code). Deleted from `_onWake`: `_resumeAfterRepaint` array, 520ms restore block, `skipAnimSuppression` param; `.config-panel.open` per-pass suppression survives (BUG-023, one-shot anim). Stale `animationPlayState` compositor nudge (~11513, no-op since v2.17.94) replaced with `_pulseAnim.pause()/play()`. CSS: `starBreath`/`aiBadgeBreath`/`blink` keyframes deleted; reduced-motion block trimmed (WAAPI helpers gate on the same query in JS). New Rule 29 + Motion.md breath-pattern rewrite. Verified headless: animations running after full 1600ms wake sequence, opacity continuous across `_onWake`, reduced-motion → zero animations. |
| **2.17.102** | **Conflict count moved to its own line (UI fix on v2.17.101)** — Can flagged the inline "· N conflicts resolved" wouldn't fit: `.connection-row-status` is nowrap+ellipsis sharing its row with three buttons. Now a `.connection-row-note` div (text-micro, muted) rendered full-width below the Dropbox row, with explanatory tooltip. Verified at 390px headless — no truncation. |
| **2.17.101** | **Sync merge-anomaly counter (from Parked)** — `_logMergeAnomaly(kind, id, detail)` → `today_merge_anomalies` (local-only, latest 50, console.warn mirror). Detection in `mergeRemoteData`: a conflict = local AND remote each carry an op on the same task newer than `last_sync_read`, excluding echo ops (pushed ops return with identical timestamps — `lc !== rc` guard). Covers done-state (checked/unchecked maps) + zone moves (`zoneChangedAt`); text edits undetectable (no per-edit stamp). Surfaced as a quiet "· N conflicts resolved" beside the Dropbox saved-status in Connections — deliberately NOT the red dot: the merge succeeded, this is telemetry on whether the unhandled 1% is real. |
| **2.17.100** | **Smoke test (Roadmap #3a) + TDZ boot crash fix** — `scripts/smoke-test.mjs` (puppeteer-core + system Chrome, own static server, no repo-root node_modules): boots app headless, waits for splash dismissal via `#addTaskBar` opacity, adds + checks a task, fails on any `pageerror`. Wired into Housekeeping pre-commit. **First run caught a real bug:** v2.17.99's `_updateBarPlaceholder()` (called from `init()`, line ~9170) read `let _aiPanelOpen` declared at ~9497 — TDZ throw killed `init()`, splash never dismissed. Declaration moved above `handleBarSubmit`. The syntax check could never catch this class of error. |
| **2.17.99** | **Input bar discoverability (Roadmap #4)** — the hidden type-then-✦ ask path is now visible three quiet ways: (1) placeholder reads "Add a task — or ask ✦ anything" when AI is configured (`_updateBarPlaceholder()`, called at init / key connect / key clear / panel close; panel-open "Ask anything…" swap unchanged); (2) `.add-ai-btn.ask` — subtle `--accent-glow` inset ring + "Ask ✦" title while the bar holds text that ✦ would send (wired into `toggleClearBtn`, cleared when panel opens; rule placed before `.active` so panel-open state wins); (3) one-time tip line in the AI panel on first open (`ai_bar_tip_seen` flag, local-only). |
| **2.17.98** | **Tokenisation audit** — full cross-check of `:root` tokens vs usage (all style blocks). Found: `#triageBar` used undefined `var(--shadow-panel)` — resolved to nothing, so the triage bar rendered with **no shadow**; now `var(--shadow-triage)` per Tokens.md. `.info-stat-label` hardcoded 10px → `var(--text-xs)`. No unused tokens; no hardcoded hex/rgba outside `:root`; off-token px spacing is only 1–3px micro-adjustments + PiP (separate document, by design). |
| **2.17.97** | **Fix: BUG-032 splash logo shift on mobile** — logo letters animated from page render while the typewriter waited for `document.fonts.ready`; on mobile cold starts the logo rendered in the fallback font and the Syne swap re-centered the splash column (visible downward shift just before typing). Letter-rise now gated behind `#splash-logo.go`, added in `startSplash` after fonts load — logo only ever renders in Syne. Also added `_splashStarted` guard: when fonts took >800ms, both the fallback timeout and `fonts.ready.then()` fired `startSplash`, double-running the typewriter rAF loop. |
| **2.17.96** | **Polish: header alignment on mobile PWA** — `@media (max-width: 600px) and (display-mode: standalone)` sets `.header { align-items: center }` and zeroes `.header-actions { margin-top }` so the TODAY title and icon buttons sit at the same vertical midpoint. Desktop and browser (non-standalone) unaffected. |
| **2.17.95** | **UI copy: AI provider label drops version number** — Connections panel now reads "Gemini Flash" (not "Gemini 2.5 Flash") so the label doesn't go stale when models update. README aligned. |
| **2.17.94** | **Fix: BUG-028 sub-fix D — completed bar single flash on window return** — CSS animations restart from keyframe 0 on every `display:none/block` repaint pass, so a mid-pulse bar (opacity 0.65) must jump to 1 once per wake; the suppress/restore machinery could only move the jump, not remove it. Pulse converted to WAAPI (`_pulseComplete`, same pattern as v2.17.72 checkmark): timeline survives display toggles — measured continuous across the exact `_forceRepaint` cycle (opacity 0.766→0.760, headless Chrome). `timerCompletePulse` keyframes deleted, `.complete` dropped from `_resumeAfterRepaint`, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected via matchMedia gate. 2 add + 5 remove sites wired in the focus-timer module. |
| **2.17.93** | **Fix: BUG-029b ✦-submit answer swapped by racing proactive load** — fallout from the BUG-029 fix (v2.17.64): `handleBarSubmit` with text does `toggleAI()` → `openAI()` which resets `_aiLoadedOnce=false` and fires `_aiLoad()` (proactive observation), then `_aiSendFromInput` fires the user's question — two in-flight `_aiCall`s, last writer wins, so the answer gets swapped by the observation seconds later. Fix: (1) `_aiReqSeq` monotonic request id — `_aiLoad`/`_aiSendFromInput`/`_aiAskFromPanel` drop responses (and errors) whose seq is stale, killing every last-writer-wins race incl. rapid double questions; (2) `openAI(skipAutoLoad)` — the ✦-with-text path opens the panel without starting the proactive load at all (saves the wasted API call); `_aiSendFromInput` now calls `_showNlRow()` on success since the skipped auto-load no longer does it. |
| **2.17.92** | **Fix: morning nudge missing on second device** — `morning_nudge_count` is local-only and set inside `applyNewDayCleanup()`, but the Dropbox restore handler stamps `stat_last_visit = today` (deliberately, to stop cleanup deleting restored tasks), which makes cleanup early-return on any device whose first open of the day follows a remote change. Net effect: only the first device opened each morning got the nudge. Fix in the restore handler: if pre-stamp `stat_last_visit !== today`, compute `morning_nudge_count` from the restored undone manual tasks (same definition as cleanup: `manualTasks` minus `doneIds`), then `checkMorningNudge()` after the stamp (own noon guard). Verified: nudge renders from a seeded count at mocked 9:30am in headless Chrome. Note: streak/history skips on the second device are unaffected — those fields sync via the backup payload. |
| **2.17.91** | **Fix: text selection during mobile drag (Trello/habits)** — the `@media (pointer: coarse)` user-select guard only covered `#manualList .task`; Trello cards and habit rows are long-press draggable too but unguarded, so the native long-press selection (~300-500ms, racing the 380ms drag timer) fired on them. Guard extended to `#trelloList .task` + `#habitList .habit`; `_startGhost` also clears any selection that already started (`getSelection().removeAllRanges()`). Verified computed `user-select:none` on rows via headless Chrome. |
| **2.17.90** | **Fix: touch-drag ghost taller than its row** — `_startGhost` replaced the clone's className with `touch-drag-ghost`, stripping `.task`/`.habit` and their `display:flex`; children block-stacked, inflating the floating card 66.5px → 111px (measured, headless Chrome + touch emulation). Fix: `classList.add` keeps row classes; ghost now 68.5px (= row × 1.03 drag scale). Follow-up: keeping `.task` let `.task[data-age-bucket="old"]` dim the ghost to 0.35 — `.touch-drag-ghost` opacity now `0.85 !important` (card in hand always solid). Not caused by v2.17.76 (× margin is layout-neutral; `.dragging`/`.drag-over` deltas measured 0) — the always-visible × just made the misshapen ghost noticeable. Verified through the real long-press path via puppeteer-core CDP touch events. |

'Oread' not found in Sea Garden (#28665) — dropped; Sandburg/Amy Lowell collections not located on Gutenberg this round. |
